### [1] [Large-scale dynamics of equatorial thermal spots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13442)
*V. P. Goncharov*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 针对无粘不可压旋转流体薄球层模型，求得沿赤道移动的定常热斑解析解及自相似解，揭示热斑传播速度与方向依赖于热对比符号及半轴比b/a，并给出自相似圆形热斑半径的幂律演化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旋转流体薄球层中受科氏力与浮力共同作用的热斑运动机制，厘清热斑形态参数（半轴比）与其传播速度、方向的定量关系，并刻画自相似演化阶段的动力学特征。

Method: 在薄球层无粘不可压旋转流体模型框架下，采用解析方法求解定常热斑解与自相似解，通过引入赤道半轴a与经向半轴b描述热斑形态，分析b/a及浮力梯度对传播速度与方向的影响。

Result: 定常热斑的速度与方向同时取决于热对比符号和半轴比b/a：当b/a=π/2时热斑静止；b/a>π/2时冷斑西移、热斑东移；0.747≤b/a<π/2时方向反转，热斑西移、冷斑东移。自相似解仅以圆形热斑形式实现，半径按幂律演化，指数k由斑边缘跨锋浮力梯度决定：浮力梯度恒定时k=1/4，总浮力守恒时k=1/6。

Conclusion: 热斑的传播特性由其热对比性质与几何形态共同控制，半轴比b/a是关键调控参数，可实现静止及东西向运动的转换；自相似阶段的热斑严格为圆形，其半径幂律指数由浮力边界条件决定，为旋转流体中热斑动力学提供了完整的解析描述。

Abstract: For a model of a thin spherical layer of an incompressible rotating fluid subjected to the action of Coriolis and buoyancy forces, both analytical solutions in the form of stationary thermal spots moving along the equator and self-similar solutions are found. The influence of the parameters determining the shape of the spots on their propagation speed and direction is studied. It is found that the speed and direction of motion of the spots depend not only on the sign of their thermal contrast with the background flow, but also on the ratio of the semi-axes determining their shape. In particular, if the ratio of the equatorial semi-axis to the meridional one $b/a$ is equal to $π/2$, the spots are at rest. If the spots are elongated along the equatorial axis to such an extent that $b/a>π/2$, then ``cold'' spots move westward and ``hot'' spots move eastward. Under the condition $0.747\leq b/a<π/2$, the situation is reversed: ``hot'' spots move westward and ``cold'' spots move eastward. Self-similar solutions in the model are realized strictly in the form of circular thermal spots, and their radius varies according to a power law with a scaling exponent $k$, which is determined by the behavior of the cross-frontal buoyancy gradient on the spot contour. We note that the regime $k=1/4$ is realized under the assumption of a constant buoyancy gradient, while the regime $k=1/6$ corresponds to the conservation of total buoyancy.

</details>


### [2] [A conservative Godunov-type mesh-free hydrodynamics scheme for weakly compressible multiphase flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13473)
*Fabian Fritz,Stefan Adami,Nikolaus A. Adams*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一种Godunov型无网格水动力学格式，用于弱可压缩单相与多相流模拟，通过一致二阶重构、斜率限制、亚音速HLLC黎曼求解器与准拉格朗日粒子平流实现，并在多个基准算例上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱可压缩单相与多相流的数值模拟需要兼顾界面锐利性、稳定性与无网格方法的灵活性，避免粒子各向异性分布和相界面处的伪质量通量。

Method: 在粒子界面处对原始变量进行一致二阶空间重构并施加斜率限制；采用亚音速HLLC近似黎曼求解器求解移动黎曼问题；粒子以含自松弛机制的准拉格朗日物质速度平流以抑制各向异性分布；界面附近使用单侧重归一化梯度处理密度间断并对各相独立施加斜率限制；通过将松弛机制约束为沿界面切向作用以保持物质界面的拉格朗日特性；同相粒子相互作用的黎曼问题以两物质速度算术平均为中心，跨相相互作用以接触波速度为中心以消除相界处的伪质量通量。

Result: 在无粘Taylor-Green涡、物质界面平流、近静水平衡的表面重力波、Kelvin-Helmholtz不稳定性和重力分层下的Rayleigh-Taylor不稳定等挑战性基准算例上完成验证。

Conclusion: 该Godunov型无网格格式能有效处理弱可压缩单相与多相流，保持界面拉格朗日特性、抑制粒子各向异性并消除相界伪质量通量，在多种基准问题中表现良好。

Abstract: We present a Godunov-type mesh-free hydrodynamics scheme for the numerical simulation of weakly compressible single- and multiphase flows. The method employs a consistent second-order spatial reconstruction of the primitive variables at the inter-particle interface, applies slope limiting, and solves the moving Riemann problem using a subsonic HLLC approximate Riemann solver. Particles are advected with a quasi-Lagrangian material velocity incorporating an inherent self-relaxation mechanism that suppresses the formation of anisotropic particle distributions. Near material interfaces, one-sided renormalized gradients are used to handle density discontinuities and slope limiting is applied for each phase independently. The Lagrangian character of material interfaces is preserved by constraining the relaxation mechanism to act tangentially to the interface. For same-phase particle interactions, the Riemann problem is centered at the arithmetic mean of the two material velocities. For cross-phase interactions, it is centered at the contact-wave velocity. This choice naturally eliminates spurious mass fluxes across phase boundaries. The methodology is validated on a set of challenging benchmarks, including the inviscid Taylor-Green vortex, material interface advection, surface gravity waves near hydrostatic equilibrium, the Kelvin-Helmholtz instability, and the Rayleigh-Taylor instability under gravitational stratification.

</details>


### [3] [A Kinetic Energy Preserving and Entropy Conserving Two-Point Flux for Multi-species Compressible Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13503)
*Sai Shruthi Srinivasan,Siva Nadarajah*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种同时满足熵守恒(EC)与动能保持(KEP)的两点通量，用于多组分流动的高阶熵稳定格式，并在数值算例中验证其优于现有通量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着超音速飞行与航天工程发展，需要能准确模拟多化学组分相互作用与混合流动的高阶熵稳定格式；而高阶熵稳定格式的性质由两点通量决定，但现有文献中用于多组分熵稳定求解器的两点通量无法同时保持熵守恒与动能保持。

Method: 将Ranocha针对单组分流动的方法论推广到多组分情形，构造一个同时满足熵守恒(EC)与动能保持(KEP)的两点通量，并与文献中已有的两点通量进行对比测试。

Result: 采用密度脉冲对流与Taylor-Green涡两个算例测试表明：新提出的EC-KEP通量同时保持熵守恒与动能保持两种性质，而现有其他通量仅能保持其中一种性质。

Conclusion: 由于两点通量决定所导出格式的性质，所提出的通量是模拟多组分可压缩流动的理想选择。

Abstract: High-order entropy stable (ES) methods have attracted much interest in the computational fluid dynamics (CFD) research community over the past decade, as we seek to simulate practical engineering applications with high accuracy and robustness. As industry delves further into supersonic flight and space travel, we need schemes capable of modelling flows with multiple chemical species that interact and mix within the fluid. As such, there is a considerable amount of effort currently being focused on developing high-order ES schemes for multi-species (MS) flow. A key ingredient for these entropy stable schemes is the two-point flux, which determines the properties of the resulting high-order discretization. As such, the numerical scheme can possess important properties such as entropy conservation and kinetic energy preservation only if the two-point flux also mirrors these properties. At present, in the literature, the two-point fluxes used for high-order ES multi-species solvers are not kinetic energy preserving (KEP). In this short communication we develop a two-point flux that is both entropy conserving (EC) and kinetic energy preserving by adapting Ranocha's single-species methodology for multi-species. The newly proposed EC and KEP flux is tested against existing two-point fluxes in the literature using the density pulse advection and Taylor-Green vortex cases. It is shown that EC and KEP flux retains both properties while the other fluxes only retain one of the desired properties. Since the two-point flux dictates the properties of the resulting scheme, the proposed flux is the ideal option for simulating multi-species compressible flow.

</details>


### [4] [A learning-based joint flow and kinematic state estimation for bodies in highly disturbed flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13560)
*Hanieh Mousavi,Anya Jones,Jeff Eldredge*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的序贯估计框架，将运动学感知的非线性流动自编码器与在线滤波相结合，仅用稀疏测量即可同时重建非定常流场、气动载荷与翼型运动学，单次预测-同化循环仅需数毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏测量中准确估计非定常气动流场十分困难，尤其在强扰动、翼型运动学未知以及观测不完整的情况下，传统方法难以同时重建流场、载荷与运动状态。

Method: 采用运动学感知的非线性流动自编码器构建降阶表示，并结合对流式测量的在线滤波（预测-同化循环）。降阶空间中的预测算子与观测算子均直接从数据中学习，无需显式物理模型。

Result: 在二维不可压缩翼型绕流（随机涡阵风、任意俯仰运动）上验证：可从表面压力传感器、涡量垂直线传感器及含阴影区的合成测速数据中准确重建。压力传感器瞬态信息量通过其对主导观测模态的时变贡献量化。升力可由主导模态重建，而阻力、俯仰角与角速度需要更高阶模态。加入有限的离体测量可提升可观性并降低估计误差与不确定性。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动序贯估计框架能高效、准确地进行稀疏测量下的联合状态估计；不同气动量对模态阶数的需求不同，补充离体测量有助于增强可观性与降低不确定性。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of unsteady aerodynamic flows from sparse measurements remains a fundamental challenge, particularly in the presence of strong disturbances, unknown body kinematics, and incomplete observations. This study presents a data-driven sequential estimation framework for joint reconstruction of unsteady flow fields, aerodynamic loads, and airfoil kinematics from sparse measurements. The approach combines a kinematics-aware nonlinear flow autoencoder with online filtering of streaming measurements. In the resulting reduced-order representation, the forecast and observation operators are learned directly from data, with each forecast-assimilation cycle requiring only a few milliseconds. The framework is evaluated on two-dimensional incompressible flow over an airfoil subjected to random vortical gusts while undergoing arbitrary pitch-up motions. Results demonstrate accurate reconstruction from surface pressure sensors, vertical lines of vorticity sensors, and synthetic velocimetry data with representative shadow regions. The transient informativeness of pressure sensors is quantified through their time-varying contributions to dominant observation modes. While lift is reconstructed from the leading modes, drag, pitch angle, and angular velocity require higher modes. Incorporating limited off-body measurements alongside surface pressure improves observability and reduces estimation error and uncertainty.

</details>


### [5] [Vertical vortex gust encounters of a delta wing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13578)
*Zhecheng Liu,Kunihiko Taira,Jeff D. Eldredge*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 采用直接数值模拟研究垂直涡阵风与无尾NACA 0012三角翼（后掠60°、攻角10°、Re=1000）的相互作用，揭示其载荷响应机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市峡谷、山区、野火及飞行器集群等复杂环境飞行需求，需要理解垂直取向涡阵风的影响；已有研究多集中于展向与流向涡遭遇，垂直涡遭遇尚待补充。

Method: 对无尾NACA 0012三角翼（后掠角60°、攻角10°、雷诺数1000）进行直接数值模拟，改变垂直涡阵风的横向位置、尺度与强度，并结合力元分析（force element analysis）关联载荷与流场响应。

Result: 垂直涡阵风在翼面持续印压低涡核，产生逆压梯度，扭曲基准表面摩擦图样并诱发分离、抬升边界层；抬升的边界层被阵风应变拉长、扭转为叶瓣状涡结构，对升力有临界贡献。横向位置由翼根向翼尖移动时，低涡核与分离区越加集中于靠近阵风的前缘，改变叶瓣演化，使力与力矩响应对位置呈复杂依赖，而对尺度和强度大致单调。

Conclusion: 揭示了垂直涡阵风遭遇的基本过程：低涡核—逆压梯度—分离抬升—叶瓣涡的链路主导载荷生成，且载荷对横向位置的依赖比尺度、强度更复杂。

Abstract: The present study analyzes encounters of a vertical vortex with a tailless NACA 0012 delta wing with a sweep angle of 60 degrees at an angle of attack of 10 degrees and Reynolds number 1000 using direct numerical simulations. Motivated by the need to fly in complex settings such as urban canyons, mountainous areas, wildfires, or vehicle swarms, we focus on the influence of a vertical vortex gust on the delta wing, complementing previous efforts that predominantly studied spanwise and streamwise vortex encounters. The vertical orientation can impose strong transient loads on all six degrees of freedom, and particular attention is devoted to connecting these loads to the flow response. By varying the gust's lateral position, size, and strength, we find a fundamental process that characterizes vertical vortex gust encounters. The gust consistently imprints a low-pressure core on the wing surface. The evolving core creates an adverse pressure gradient that distorts the baseline skin-friction pattern and induces separation that lifts the boundary layers. These lifted boundary layers are elongated and twisted by the gust's strain into lobed vortical structures, which are shown by force element analysis to make a critical contribution to the lift. As the gust's lateral position is varied from root to tip, the low-pressure core and the induced flow separation are increasingly localized around the leading edge closest to the gust, modifying the lobe evolution and leading to complex dependence of the force and moment response on gust position compared to the largely monotonic dependence on gust size and strength.

</details>


### [6] [Lagrangian Dynamical Theory of the Velocity Gradient Tensor: Real Schur Form, Schur Frame, Characteristic Vorticity Modes, and Commutative Vorticity Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14034)
*Tao Chen*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出速度梯度张量（VGT）的拉格朗日动力学理论，基于其正判别涡区的实Schur形式，推导旋转不变量、特征涡模及交换涡算子对的演化方程，揭示应变率张量与压力（焓）Hessian张量在涡模转化与再分配中的新物理作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 速度梯度张量及其不变量在涡流动力学中至关重要，但已有理论多基于欧拉或张量不变量视角，缺乏在拉格朗日轨迹上、以实Schur形式为基的统一动力学描述；特别是特征涡模(R_N,S_N)及交换涡算子对的演化方程尚未系统建立，其与应变率张量、压力Hessian的相互作用机制不清晰。

Method: 从可压缩牛顿流体的Navier-Stokes方程出发，在正判别涡区采用VGT的规范实Schur形式，显式确定Schur标架角速度沿任意拉格朗日轨迹的变化，推导六个旋转不变量的一般演化方程；进而得到导出VGT不变量（含三个主不变量及其Schur表示）的演化方程，并以本征形式与分量形式推导特征涡模(R_N,S_N)的演化方程，再推广至交换涡算子对(Ψ_R,Γ_S)。

Result: 得到六个旋转不变量及导出VGT不变量沿拉格朗日轨迹的完整演化方程；首次在Schur标架内以本征与分量两种形式给出特征涡模(R_N,S_N)的演化方程，并扩展至交换涡算子对(Ψ_R,Γ_S)。物理上发现：拉伸应变率张量D_EL仅拉伸或压缩刚性旋转涡量R_N的积分线；剪切涡量S_N与剪切应变率张量D_SH的相互作用在旋转轴法平面内改变S_N；压力Hessian张量（可压缩流中为焓Hessian张量）在两种涡模/算子的相互转化与再分配中起新作用。

Conclusion: 所提拉格朗日理论为理解各类涡流中VGT组分与内禀涡模的动力学提供了系统框架，并揭示了压力（焓）Hessian在涡模转化与再分配中的新物理角色。

Abstract: The present work proposes a novel Lagrangian dynamical theory for the velocity gradient tensor (VGT) $\bm{A}\equiv\bm{\nabla}\bm{u}$, formulated on the basis of its canonical real Schur form in the traditional vortex region with positive discriminant. Starting from the Navier-Stokes equations for compressible Newtonian fluids, we derive the general evolution equations for the six rotational invariants in the real Schur form, in which the angular velocity of the Schur frame is explicitly determined along any Lagrangian trajectory. These results then enable us to obtain the evolution equations for the derived VGT invariants, which also encompass the three principal VGT invariants and their Schur representations. Notably, and perhaps for the first time, the evolution equations for the characteristic vorticity modes $(\bm{R}_{N},\bm{S}_{N})$ are derived in both intrinsic and component forms within the Schur frame, and are subsequently extended to the recently proposed commutative vorticity-operator pair $(\bmΨ_{R},\bmΓ_{S})$. We find that the straining strain-rate tensor $\bm{D}_{EL}$ acts solely to stretch or contract the integral lines of the rigid-rotation vorticity $\bm{R}_{N}$, whereas the interaction between the shear vorticity $\bm{S}_{N}$ and the shear strain-rate tensor $\bm{D}_{SH}$ alters $\bm{S}_{N}$ within the rotation-axis-normal plane. The theory further unveils a new physical role of the pressure Hessian tensor (more precisely, the enthalpy Hessian tensor for compressible flow) in the mutual transformation and redistribution of the two vorticity modes/operators. The proposed theory could be useful for understanding the dynamics of VGT constituents and intrinsic vorticity modes in a variety of vortical flows.

</details>


### [7] [Retraction dynamics of polymer-laden droplets impacting a smooth solid surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14055)
*Koji Hasegawa,Patrick Palmetshofer,Anne K. Geppert,Bernhard Weigand*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究通过高速侧视与底视成像，考察聚合物（PEO）液滴撞击蓝宝石基底后的接触线动力学，发现铺展阶段遵循经典惯性-毛细标度、几乎不受聚合物影响，而回缩阶段随PEO浓度升高显著减慢，并据此提出标度模型成功统一了各浓度与韦伯数下的数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对液滴撞击固体基底后接触线动力学中聚合物作用尚不明确的问题，作者假设聚合物浓度会改变撞击后的润湿行为，尤其影响回缩阶段，并希望通过系统实验揭示并量化这一影响。

Method: 采用含PEO（0–400 ppm）的液滴撞击平面蓝宝石基底，在韦伯数10–700范围内开展实验；利用高速侧视与底视成像观测铺展与回缩过程，并通过图像处理定量提取最大铺展因子、回缩速度与回缩接触角，进而提出标度模型关联归一化回缩速度、回缩接触角与撞击条件。

Result: 最大铺展因子遵循经典惯性-毛细标度，聚合物添加在现有条件下未产生可测变化；而回缩阶段强烈依赖PEO浓度，PEO含量增加导致回缩速度与动态接触角显著减小，铺展动力学保持类水行为。所提标度模型可将所有浓度与韦伯数下的实验数据有效归一化。

Conclusion: 聚合物对液滴撞击铺展阶段影响可忽略，但显著抑制回缩阶段，这一行为可归因于回缩接触线区域的附加阻力（其微观机制尚不明确）；所提出的标度模型能统一描述不同聚合物浓度与韦伯数下的回缩动力学。

Abstract: Hypothesis: The presence of a polymer in a droplet plays a significant role in contact line dynamics following droplet impact onto a solid substrate. We hypothesize that the polymer concentration modifies the post-impact wetting behavior. Experiments: In our experiments, we visualize the contact line dynamics of polymer-laden droplets impacting onto a flat sapphire substrate. Using high-speed side and bottom-view imaging, we analyze droplet spreading and retraction over a broad range of Weber numbers (10-700) and polyethylene oxide (PEO) concentrations (0-400 ppm). To systematically characterize the contact line dynamics, we used image processing to quantify the maximum spreading factor, receding velocity, and receding contact angle. Findings: The effect of the polymer on the spreading phase is negligible, as the maximum spreading factor follows classical inertial-capillary scaling, showing that polymer addition produces no measurable change in the maximum spreading under the present conditions. In contrast, the receding phase exhibits a strong dependence on the polymer concentration: increasing the PEO content leads to a significant reduction in the receding velocity and dynamic contact angle, while the spreading dynamics remain water-like. These results are consistent with an additional resistance associated with the receding contact-line region, while the microscopic origin of this resistance remains unresolved. To capture this behavior, we propose a scaling model that relates the normalized retraction velocity to the receding contact angle and impact conditions. The model effectively collapses the experimental data across all investigated concentrations and Weber numbers.

</details>


### [8] [Embracing Flow Unsteadiness: A High-Throughput Learning Platform Enables Vortex-Exploiting Bioinspired Propulsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14254)
*Fei Han,Xinyu Cui,Zhipeng Wang,Ning Yang,Hang Xu,Haifeng Zhang,Zhongming Hu,Jun Wang,Junfeng Du,Dixia Fan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出REEF物理-学习协同设计框架，结合高通量真实流固交互阵列SHOAL与分阶段算法V-STAR，使多类推进器力包络扩展至传统参数搜索的两倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Completed realizations are organized by observable fibers, distinguishing prescribed core kinematics from the surrounding force and transport mechanisms. These include a reversal of leading viscous work at fixed axis motion and first velocity gradient, a signed transition through a stationary singular center under one fixed canonical pressure trace, and a connected stretching family crossing the threshold of zero leading direct viscous vorticity supply. The results establish non-rigidity within observable fibers and show that axis kinematics alone do not determine the local pressure--viscosity partition. Direct profile selection and dynamical stress generation are treated as coupled components of construction, rather than as definitions of the entire design space.

*Nithin Somasekharan,Yadi Cao,Shaowu Pan*

TL;DR: 提出Deep Koopman Sensing数据驱动降阶同化框架，结合非线性自编码器与参数条件线性潜动力学近似Koopman算子，在四个流动基准上实现最低同化误差，表明潜动力学应面向状态估计任务而非仅优化前向预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体流动的实时重构对物理理解与流动控制很重要。当第一性原理模型在线数据同化（DA）代价过高时，学习型降阶模型是高效替代，但这类模型通常面向前向预测优化，而非状态估计，因此预测精度未必能反映同化性能。

Method: 提出Deep Koopman Sensing：将非线性自编码器与参数条件线性潜动力学结合以近似Koopman算子，构成数据驱动降阶DA框架。与参数化动态模式分解（pDMD）、多层感知机（MLP）和xLSTM在四个基准上对比：一维粘性Burgers、二维圆柱绕流、二维溃坝、三维球体绕流。并分别采用扩展卡尔曼滤波与集合滤波进行同化测试。

Result: 开环精度不能可靠预测同化性能。Deep Koopman Sensing在四个基准上均取得最低同化误差。使用扩展卡尔曼滤波时，传感器测量在所有四个基准上都能改善两个线性潜模型的估计，却会降低非线性模型的表现，尽管后者开环性能很强。使用集合滤波时，非线性模型不再被同化损害，但Koopman模型仍保持最低同化误差。

Conclusion: 潜动力学应面向下游估计任务进行设计，而不应仅依据预测精度来选择；Koopman式降阶建模是从稀疏、流式测量中实现实时流动重构的有效途径。

Abstract: Real-time reconstruction of fluid flows from sparse sensor measurements is important for both physical understanding and flow control. When first-principles models are too expensive for online data assimilation (DA), learned reduced-order models provide an efficient alternative, but are commonly optimized for forward prediction rather than state estimation. We propose Deep Koopman Sensing, a data-driven reduced-order DA framework that combines a nonlinear autoencoder with parameter-conditioned linear latent dynamics approximating the Koopman operator. We compare the proposed model with parametric dynamic mode decomposition (pDMD), a multilayer perceptron (MLP), and xLSTM across four benchmarks: 1D viscous Burgers, 2D flow past a cylinder, 2D dambreak, and 3D flow past a sphere. Our results reveal a marked distinction between forecasting and sensing: open-loop accuracy does not reliably predict assimilation performance, while Deep Koopman Sensing achieves the lowest assimilation error across all four benchmarks. More importantly, with an extended Kalman filter, incorporating sensor measurements improves the estimates of both linear latent models across all four benchmarks, whereas it degrades the nonlinear models, despite their strong open-loop performance. With ensemble filtering, the nonlinear models are no longer degraded by assimilation, while the Koopman model still attains the lowest assimilation error. These results show that latent dynamics should be designed for the downstream estimation task rather than selected solely for forecast accuracy, and demonstrate Koopman-based reduced-order modeling as an effective approach for real-time flow reconstruction from sparse, streaming measurements.

</details>

Abstract: Building-integrated wind turbines (BIWT) remain under-deployed in dense European urban contexts despite their potential contribution to net-zero energy targets. This paper presents a measurement-validated computational fluid dynamics (CFD) framework to identify optimal locations for micro-wind turbines on the envelopes of dense Parisian Haussmannian blocks. The methodology couples a multi-month on-site anemometry campaign on two case-study buildings, a calibrated steady RANS digital twin using the realisable k-$ε$ turbulence model, and the introduction of an architecturally integrated accelerator wing -termed aerofoil -placed at the envelope edge to amplify and stabilise the local flow. Quantitative validation against year-long anemometer records yields a mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) of approximately 18 % and a hit-rate FAC2 above 0.85, meeting the COST 732 acceptance criteria for urban CFD models. On the {É}cole Nationale Sup{é}rieure d'Architecture Paris-Val-de-Seine (ENSAPVS) case study, the inter-tower configuration produces a directional speed-up factor of 3.4 (95 % CI 3.18-3.62) for the prevailing north-westerly sector at +70 m above ground; the addition of the aerofoil yields a further +13 % local speed-up, equivalent to +44 % in extractable power. A capacity-factor analysis based on the measured Weibull distribution and the Savonius rotor power curve estimates an annual yield of approximately 69 MWh, offsetting around 6 % of the building's annual electricity consumption -a substantially lower but more realistic figure than the cube-of-mean estimate. The findings are synthesised in a decision dendrogram that maps wind-rose typology, building plan, height and implantation onto recommended device families, providing architects and engineers with a transferable design-stage tool.

</details>

Method: 作者设计并搭建了一套专用于软材料接触力学与摩擦研究的装置：除支持原位/工况下（in situ / in operando）界面光学监测外，可同时沿五个自由度驱动（三个平移和两个旋转）；三个平移均可实现大速度/大行程运动，其中负责法向接触加载的自由度还能施加高频/小振幅振动。接触的动态响应通过六轴力/力矩传感器和六轴位移/旋转传感器进行监测。文中描述了装置的结构、实现、对齐、标定与分辨率，并通过一系列弹性体接触实验验证其功能。

Conclusion: 该装置可用于研究在丰富且与摩擦学相关的运动学或动力学激励下的各类软界面力学行为，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: The mechanics and friction of contact interfaces involving soft materials like gel, rubber or human skin are of both fundamental and applied interest. Recent insights have been made into this field thanks to in situ observations of the contact interface. However, current soft-material-oriented tribometers enable only few degrees of freedom for the actuation of the contact, far from covering the richness of the loading conditions relevant to real tribological contacts. Here, we introduce an apparatus dedicated to the study of the contact mechanics and friction of soft materials which, in addition to in situ / in operando optical monitoring of the interface, enables simultaneous actuation along five degrees of freedom: three translations and two rotations. While all three translations feature large velocity/large stroke motion, the one responsible for normal contact loading can also apply high frequency/small amplitude vibrations. The contact's dynamical response is monitored using both a 6-axes force/torque sensor and a 6-axes displacement/rotation sensor. We first describe the structure of the apparatus, its implementation, alignment, calibration and resolutions. We then illustrate its capabilities through a series of experiments on elastomer contacts. Our apparatus will be useful to investigate the mechanics of a wide range of soft interfaces submitted to rich, tribology-relevant kinematic or dynamic stimuli.

Abstract: Using large-scale, three-dimensional discrete element simulations, we investigate the kinematic properties of granular chute flows of cohesionless grains, together with the evolution of the coordination number and the fraction of sliding contacts. Our results reveal that, under certain conditions, increasing the grain--grain friction coefficient leads to a decrease in the global dissipation of the system. This finding highlights that the overall dissipation is not solely governed by the energy lost during sliding contacts, but also by the probability that such contacts occur, a probability that decreases markedly as the friction coefficient increases. Together, these effects demonstrate the subtle and nontrivial interplay between grain-scale frictional interactions and macroscopic flow behavior in dense and cohesionless granular materials.

This enables sufficient harvested power and tuning capability of the resonant frequency through electrical methods. While much literature treats the optimisation of piezoelectric cantilevers, the optimisation range is usually limited by the transverse coupling coefficient k31${}^2$ of the material. This work introduces an innovative solution to extend the optimisation range and increase the coupling coefficient of piezoelectric cantilevers. This is achieved by minimising lateral strain in the beam using lateral bars to maximise the equivalent material coupling coefficient. The theoretical basis of this innovation is demonstrated through the exploitation of the constitutive equations of piezoelectric materials. The interest of the addition of lateral bars to increase the coupling coefficient is demonstrated and studied with simulations based on the finite elements method. Finally, a proof of concept is realised using a aluminum cantilever prototype integrating a lead-free KNaNbO3 (KNN) piezoelectric material. It is tested under vibration at 0.1 m/s${}^2$ with variable resistive loads. The results show that the coupling coefficient increases by 30% and the relative frequency bandwidth by 32% with resistive tuning of the resonant frequency, by fixing eight steel bars to the cantilever. The designed prototype is a highly performant leadfree vibration energy harvester. It produces a maximum power of 49.9 $μ$W at resonance, and its normalised power density is equal to 16.5 mW/G${}^2$/cm${}^3$. Its relative frequency bandwidth is equal to 3.1%.

Abstract: We survey a geometric approach to the Navier-Stokes equations developed by the author over the past fifteen years. The central object is a Riemannian space of fourteen dimensions whose metric is Ricci-flat precisely on the solutions of the Navier-Stokes system, so that the behaviour of a viscous incompressible fluid becomes a property of curvature. The space decomposes into a flat six-dimensional part and two dual quadruples of coordinates, Eulerian and Lagrangian, which exhibits the two classical descriptions of a fluid as two halves of a single geometry. We also recall the associated six-dimensional metric, whose integrability condition is the incompressibility of the fluid; the link with the projective geometry of second-order ordinary differential equations in the sense of E. Cartan; and the use of Cartan's invariants to construct a three-dimensional analogue of the Taylor-Green vortex. An application of the same construction to the equations of rotation of a rigid body is indicated.

If $a=0$, no non-constant Darboux polynomial exists. If $a\neq 0$, a non-constant Darboux polynomial exists if and only if \[ c=-ab\qquad\text{or}\qquad c=-2ab. \] In these two cases the unique irreducible Darboux polynomials, up to non-zero constant multiples, are respectively \[ y-ax,\qquad y^2-2bx. \] Consequently every non-constant Darboux polynomial is a non-zero constant multiple of a positive integral power of the corresponding irreducible factor. Finally, we relate this branch to the Quartic Inverse Riccati (QIR) class and discuss an invariant-based classification problem for quartic Abel equations.

This class includes periodic reductions, symmetry reductions, and a broad family of other reductions defined in terms of the associated Lax operator. The reduction constraints define integrable maps that enable solutions of the reduced systems to be extended to solutions of the corresponding differential-difference equations. The construction is illustrated using the Volterra hierarchy.

Result: 研究发现单表面热响应存在固有非唯一性：等价矩阵族导致无法通过改变频率、延迟时间、光束几何或空间偏移予以消除。此外，基于SVD灵敏度矩阵的可辨识性检验本身不足以排除全各向异性热导率张量重建中的全局等价解。研究进一步表明，三个相互正交的表面可提供互补信息，用于全张量恢复，并据此给出信号与测量取向的选择指导。

Abstract: Materials with full anisotropic thermal conductivity are widely used in advanced applications, creating a need for dedicated characterization of their thermal-conductivity tensors. Such tensors contain six independent components: three diagonal conductivities and three off-diagonal couplings. However, whether single-surface thermoreflectance measurements can uniquely determine an arbitrary tensor remains unclear. Here, we address this question by deriving a Schur-equivalent conductivity matrix that reproduces the single-surface thermal response in a planar transducer/substrate configuration. This equivalence establishes an intrinsic nonuniqueness that cannot be removed by varying frequency, delay time, beam geometry, or spatial offset. We also demonstrate that parameter-identifiability tests based on singular-value decomposition (SVD) of the sensitivity matrix cannot, by themselves, rule out globally equivalent solutions in full anisotropic thermal-conductivity tensor reconstruction. Combining systematic analysis with numerical validation, we provide guidance for selecting signals and measurement orientations for full-tensor characterization. We show that three mutually orthogonal surfaces provide complementary information for full-tensor recovery. These findings define the limits of single-surface measurements and establish a systematic route to full anisotropic thermal-conductivity tensor characterization.

We also report that most normalized harmonic ratios from the 1- and 2x10^6-cell samples fell between the water and agar references, indicating partial restriction of Brownian rotation in the cellular environment. These findings demonstrate that MPS can detect cell-associated MNPs while providing complementary spectral information about their ensemble-averaged physical confinement, supporting its application in magnetic cell labeling and cell-tracking studies.

The P3F configuration mitigates charge cancellation from the alternating stress distribution of higher-order TS modes, enabling measurable coupling across a broad sequence of overtones. Motivation: 磁热疗与MRI集成需要纳米颗粒同时具备强加热能力、T2造影效果和全身给药后的血液相容性，但现有材料难以兼顾这些性能，因此需要系统研究形貌和阳离子取代对综合性能的调控。

Method: 制备柠檬酸盐稳定的铁氧体纳米颗粒（Fe3O4、Co0.6Fe2.4O4、Zn0.3Fe2.7O4、Zn0.35Mn0.25Fe2.4O4），呈球形（8-10.5 nm）和准立方（9-12.5 nm）形貌，另制备35 nm Fe3O4立方体；结合形貌工程与尖晶石晶格阳离子取代，在临床兼容交变磁场下于卵巢肿瘤模拟体模中测量饱和磁化强度、矫顽力、T2加权信号衰减和热耗散，并进行SKOV3细胞毒性、血液相容性及生理流动下GelMA模型评估。

Result: 35 nm Fe3O4立方体和准立方Zn0.35Mn0.25Fe2.4O4产生最高体模温升，较小球形颗粒加热较温和；所有配方对SKOV3细胞具有细胞相容性（24 h存活率>96%）。血液相容性区分了主要候选材料：35 nm Fe3O4立方体虽溶血极小但引起红细胞变形，而9 nm准立方Zn0.35Mn0.25Fe2.4O4在高达1,000 μg mL-1时与PBS相比无变化；生理流动下GelMA模型进一步证实生物相容性。MRI体模显示Fe3O4立方体产生扩展信号空洞，可能模糊边界。

Conclusion: 9.5 nm准立方Zn0.35Mn0.25Fe2.4O4是领先的静脉注射候选材料，兼具强加热、T2对比与血液相容性；大尺寸Fe3O4立方体更适合局部瘤内热疗。

Abstract: Magnetic hyperthermia integrated with magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) requires nanoparticles that combine strong heating, T2 contrast, and hemocompatibility after systemic administration. Here, we prepared citrate-stabilized ferrite nanoparticles (Fe3O4, Co0.6Fe2.4O4, Zn0.3Fe2.7O4, and Zn0.35Mn0.25Fe2.4O4) in spherical (8-10.5 nm) and quasi-cubic (9-12.5 nm) forms, along with 35 nm Fe3O4 cubes. By coupling morphology engineering with spinel-lattice cation substitution, we tuned saturation magnetization, coercivity, T2-weighted signal attenuation, and heat dissipation in ovarian tumor-mimicking phantoms under clinically compatible alternating magnetic fields. The 35 nm Fe3O4 cube and quasi-cubic Zn0.35Mn0.25Fe2.4O4 generated the highest phantom temperature rises, whereas smaller spherical particles produced softer heating. All formulations were cytocompatible in SKOV3 cells, with >96% viability at 24 h. However, hemocompatibility distinguished the leading candidates: 35 nm Fe3O4 cubes induced red blood cell deformation despite minimal hemolysis, while 9 nm quasi-cubic Zn0.35Mn0.25Fe2.4O4 showed no change versus PBS up to 1,000 μg mL-1. Perfusable GelMA models under physiological flow further confirmed biocompatibility. MRI phantoms showed that Fe3O4 cubes produced extended signal voids that may obscure boundaries. Overall, 9.5 nm quasi-cubic Zn0.35Mn0.25Fe2.4O4 is the leading intravenous candidate, combining strong heating, T2 contrast, and hemocompatibility, whereas large Fe3O4 cubes are better suited for localized intratumoral hyperthermia.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文利用ERA5气象数据评估风对赤道空间电梯的影响，提出以气压加权RMS风速这一标量表征风荷载，分析赤道风载地理分布，并讨论设计约束与缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间电梯的缆索长期暴露于大气层中，风荷载可能显著影响缆索倾角与运载能力，但以往缺乏将风数据与电梯设计参数直接关联的量化框架。本文旨在建立这种联系，明确何种风速对特定电梯构成"过量风"，并为锚点选址与设计提供依据。

Method: 采用ERA5气象再分析数据集；通过解析推导，将风对缆索倾角和载荷容量的影响归结为单一标量——按气压平均的RMS风速；随后分析赤道区域ERA5风场数据，考察风载的地理分布与垂直分布，并结合闪电统计数据评估锚点候选区域。

Result: 赤道风载随地理分布差异显著：大陆上空较低，海洋上空较高，且主要贡献来自大气最低20 km。数据表明，若薄带式电梯设计正面迎风，现实赤道风将构成显著约束。结合风与闪电统计，识别出若干有前景的赤道锚点区域。

Conclusion: 风荷载是薄带式空间电梯设计的重要约束条件，其影响可通过气压加权RMS风速统一刻画；本文进一步探讨了风载对设计的启示，并提出多种缓解策略，以提升现实风况下的生存能力与运行载荷容量。

Abstract: This paper uses the ERA5 weather dataset to assess the impact of the wind on an equatorial space elevator. An analytical formulation establishes that the effect of wind on tether inclination and payload capacity can be captured by a single scalar: RMS wind velocity, averaged over air pressure. This formulation clarifies what constitutes excessive wind for a particular elevator and provides a direct link between wind data and elevator design parameters.
  Analysis of equatorial ERA5 data shows that equatorial wind loading varies strongly with geography with lower values over continents and higher values over oceans, with the majority contribution coming from the lowest 20 km of atmosphere. The data indicate that realistic equatorial winds pose a significant constraint on thin-ribbon elevator designs if align to face the wind. Combining wind and lightning statistics highlights several promising equatorial regions for anchor sites.
  Finally, the implications of wind loading for elevator design are examined, and several mitigation strategies are discussed to improve survivability and operational payload capacity under realistic wind conditions.

</details>

### [33] [Building a Production Greek-English Speech Recognizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13498)
*Christos Petrocheilos,Cleopatra Papadopoulou,Chris Porikis,Ioakeim Perros,Ayoub Kirouane,Themistoklis Nikolis*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 报告构建双语希腊语-英语ASR系统Sophea的工程实践，通过23轮迭代和ROVER集成使九项生产门槛全部达标，并公开方法与量化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可投产的双语希腊语-英语自动语音识别系统，需同时满足希腊语与英语的词错误率、语种识别及非语音音频幻觉等多项生产门槛，而单一训练数据配比难以兼顾所有指标。

Method: 采用六阶段数据流程，将音频质量过滤器对域内锚点进行校准（将希腊语打音频的丢弃率从98.7%降至10.6%）；进行23轮训练迭代、测试两种模型架构；通过预注册消融实验定位幻觉缺陷至某一训练数据包；使用三模型ROVER集成，并对两模型采用学习式逐片段仲裁器。

Result: 无任何训练数据配比同时通过全部九项门槛：满足希腊语噪声环境目标需约1500步密集域暴露，而保持英语语种识别仅容约250步（重平衡配比下约1250步，但牺牲希腊语准确率）。ROVER集成将门槛覆盖从单模型的4–7/9提升至9/9，重叠语音WER从53.35%降至37.87%（相对改善29%）。仲裁模型sophea/asr-k1在Open ASR Leaderboard八个公开英语测试集上平均WER为4.26%，实时希腊语噪声环境流量WER为25.88%。

Conclusion: 报告了五例测量工具产生貌似合理但错误结果的情况，以及七项经评估但未上线的方案；不发布模型权重与训练数据，仅公开方法论与量化结果。

Abstract: We report a multi-month engineering program to build Sophea, a production bilingual Greek-English automatic speech recognition system. We evaluate the system against nine production gates covering Greek and English word error rate, language identification, and hallucinations on non-speech audio. Across twenty-three training iterations and two model architectures, no training-data composition passed all nine gates simultaneously. Meeting the Greek noisy-environment target required about 1,500 steps of dense domain exposure, while preserving English language identification tolerated only about 250 steps, or about 1,250 with a rebalanced mix that reduced Greek accuracy. We describe a six-stage data pipeline in which calibrating an audio-quality filter against in-domain anchors reduced the discarded share of scored Greek audio from 98.7 percent to 10.6 percent. A pre-registered ablation isolated a hallucination defect to one training-data package. A three-model ROVER ensemble increased gate coverage from 4-7 of 9 for individual models to 9 of 9 and reduced overlapping-speech WER from 53.35 percent to 37.87 percent, a 29 percent relative improvement. A separate learned per-clip arbiter over two models is listed as sophea/asr-k1 (preview) on the public Open ASR Leaderboard, with 4.26 percent average WER across eight public English test sets, and reaches 25.88 percent WER on live Greek noisy-environment traffic. We also document five cases in which a measurement tool produced a plausible but incorrect result and seven substantial approaches that were evaluated but not shipped. No model weights or training data are released; we report methodology and quantitative results only.

</details>


### [34] [Subphonetic Acoustic Modeling via Optimal Transport for Pronunciation Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13694)
*Haopeng Geng,Jiun-Ting Li,Daisuke Saito,Nobuaki Minematsu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出一种拓扑感知的帧级声学模型，通过有序子音素状态与最优时序传输分类（OTTC）结合，学习音素内的稠密有序状态后验，兼顾识别与音素边界分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发音评估需要时间精确、具诊断意义且忠实于学习者实际发音的声学证据。现有声学模型难以同时提供识别与分割证据：基于CTC的音素识别器后验稀疏、峰值化，常漏掉音素边界和细粒度发音线索；文本相关的强制对齐器虽有可靠时间信息，但依赖转写文本，无法直接用于无参考发音分析。

Method: 提出拓扑感知的帧级声学模型，在每个音素内学习稠密的有序状态后验。核心思路是将有序子音素状态与最优时序传输分类（OTTC）结合，在神经声学模型中恢复音素内部状态结构，从而促进稠密的单调帧级状态判别，同时保留音素识别能力。

Result: 在朗读、自发和L2语音上的实验表明，相比神经基线，分割性能提升，识别性能具竞争力。下游评估显示在错误发音检测和自动发音评估上均有增益。探针分析表明，所学习的状态捕获了音素相关的声学结构，而非任意的帧级分布。

Conclusion: 有序子音素状态与OTTC的结合可同时改善音素边界分割与识别，并为无参考发音分析提供有效的帧级声学证据。

Abstract: Pronunciation assessment requires acoustic evidence that is temporally precise, diagnostically meaningful, and faithful to the learner's actual production. However, existing acoustic models often struggle to provide recognition and segmentation evidence simultaneously. CTC-based phone recognizers can predict phone sequences flexibly, but their sparse and peaky posteriors often miss phone boundaries and fine-grained pronunciation cues. In contrast, text-dependent forced aligners provide reliable temporal information when transcripts are available, but are not directly applicable to reference-free pronunciation analysis. In this work, we propose a topology-aware frame-wise acoustic model that learns dense ordered state posteriors within each phone. The key idea is to recover phone-internal state structure in a neural acoustic model by combining ordered subphonetic states with optimal temporal transport classification (OTTC). This combination encourages dense monotonic frame-level state discrimination while preserving phone recognition ability. Experiments on read, spontaneous, and L2 speech show improved segmentation over neural baselines with competitive recognition performance. Downstream evaluations further show gains in mispronunciation detection and automatic pronunciation assessment. Probing analysis suggests that the learned states capture phoneme-dependent acoustic structure rather than arbitrary frame-level distributions.

</details>


### [35] [DualSpecSE: A Dual-Path Speech Enhancement Network Integrating Mel and Complex Spectrograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13911)
*Xingchen Li,Ziqian Wang,Zikai Liu,Yike Zhu,Zihan Zhang,Longshuai Xiao,Lei Xie*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出DualSpecSE，一种双路径框架，联合建模Mel谱与复数谱以同时提升ASR性能与语音重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一谱表示难以兼顾ASR所需的高层声学特征与波形重建所需的高保真细节，因此需要联合建模粗粒度的Mel谱与细粒度的复数谱。

Method: 采用双路径架构：Mel分支学习粗粒度声学表示并输出增强Mel谱供ASR直接使用；复数分支精修细粒度谱细节以实现高保真波形重建。基于CleanMel的跨带与窄带模块（cross-band、narrow-band blocks），引入交互模块（interaction module）与融合模块（fusion module）实现两分支间的信息交换，并同时输出增强Mel谱与复数谱，无需预训练声码器。

Result: 实验结果显示，语音保真度、感知质量以及ASR性能均获得一致提升。

Conclusion: 双分支联合建模Mel谱与复数谱的框架可有效兼顾语音增强的感知质量与下游ASR性能，且无需依赖预训练声码器，代码与音频样本已公开。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose DualSpecSE, a speech enhancement framework that jointly models Mel-spectrogram and complex spectrogram in a dual-path architecture for improved ASR performance and higher-quality speech reconstruction. The Mel branch learns coarse-grained acoustic representations and produces enhanced Mel-spectrograms for direct ASR usage, while the complex branch refines fine-grained spectral details for high-fidelity waveform reconstruction. Built upon the cross-band and narrow-band blocks from CleanMel, DualSpecSE introduces an interaction module and a fusion module to enable effective information exchange between the two branches. The model simultaneously outputs enhanced Mel and complex spectrogram without requiring a pretrained vocoder. Experimental results demonstrate consistent improvements in speech fidelity, perceptual quality, and ASR performance. Codes and audio samples are available.

</details>


### [36] [HARP: Agentic Hybrid Retrieval and Analysis for Long-Form Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14116)
*Chin-Jou Li,Masao Someki,Woojeong Jin,Yashish M. Siriwardena,Tanmay Laud,Shanil Puri,Shinji Watanabe*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: HARP：面向长音频分析的智能体框架与基准，系统研究检索与证据表示。混合检索（关键词+向量）性能最稳健；结合元数据与检索音频作为证据后，答案准确率提升约10%、推理依据准确率提升约6%。细粒度评估表明仅看答案准确率会高估系统能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长音频分析需要在长时间录音中定位并整合分散的证据。现有工作主要通过结构化文本表示检索语义内容，但许多真实查询依赖声学证据，这类证据在连续表示或原始音频中保留得更好，因此需要系统研究不同检索与证据表示方式的影响。

Method: 提出HARP（Hybrid Audio Retrieval Pipeline），一个智能体框架与配套基准，用于系统研究长音频分析中的检索方式和证据表示形式。评估中比较了混合检索（关键词检索与向量检索结合）与单一模态检索，并考察将元数据与检索到的音频同时作为证据的效果。

Result: 混合检索（关键词+向量）表现出最稳健的性能。当同时使用元数据和检索音频作为证据时，相比单模态检索与证据，平均答案准确率提升约10%，推理依据（rationale）准确率提升约6%。细粒度评估显示，单纯以答案准确率衡量会高估系统能力，且HARP在各类查询上大体与人类性能趋势一致。

Conclusion: 将结构化检索与灵活的音频证据访问相结合至关重要；评估长音频系统时不应仅依赖答案准确率，还需纳入推理依据等细粒度指标。

Abstract: Long-form audio analysis requires systems to localize and integrate evidence distributed across extended recordings. While existing work primarily retrieves semantic content through structured textual representations, many real-world queries depend on acoustic evidence that is better preserved in continuous representations or raw audio. We introduce HARP (Hybrid Audio Retrieval Pipeline), an agentic framework and benchmark for systematically studying retrieval and evidence representations in long-audio analysis. Hybrid retrieval combining keyword and vector search shows the most robust performance. When paired with both metadata and retrieved audio as evidence, average answer accuracy improves by around 10% and rationale accuracy by around 6% over single-modality retrieval and evidence. Fine-grained evaluation shows that answer accuracy alone overestimates system capability and that HARP mostly follows human performance trends across query types. These results highlight the importance of combining structured retrieval with flexible access to audio evidence and evaluating long-audio systems beyond answer accuracy.

</details>


### [37] [Modeling, Scaling, and Decoding: Optimizing Controllable Speech Generation with Nonverbal Vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14231)
*Ziyu Zhang,Yun Chen,Taihui Wang,Hanzhao Li,Qicong Xie,Rilin Chen,Zhixian Zhao,Lei Xie*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出 NVV-aware DiTAR 系统，实现非言语发声（NVVs）的可控合成，在 ISCSLP 2026 NVVSpeech Challenge Track 2 中获得总成绩第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非言语发声（NVVs）对自然、富有表现力的语音至关重要，但其声学多样性高、在现有语料中分布不均衡，导致可控合成仍然困难。

Method: 系统基于 DiTAR，建模连续语音潜变量，将 16 类目标 NVV 编码为专用 token，并调整 stop prediction 以区分话语中部发声与话语边界。训练先在大规模双语含 NVV 语音上预训练，再在经定向合成增强与频率感知重平衡的语料上做持续监督微调。推理时选择声学 prompt、调整 LM-guidance 与噪声注入尺度，并采用 Best-of-N 采样与多指标筛选降低生成失败。

Result: 最终系统官方加权双语得分为 62.786，在 Track 2 参赛系统中普通话第一、英语第二、总成绩第一。消融实验表明，定向增强对低频 NVV 类别收益最大；稳健的候选选择需要平衡 NVV 正确性、文本保真度与感知质量。

Conclusion: 通过 token 化 NVV 类别、stop prediction 适配、数据增强与重平衡以及推理端多指标候选选择，可有效提升非言语发声的可控合成质量与类别均衡性。

Abstract: Controllable synthesis of nonverbal vocalizations (NVVs) is es- sential for natural and expressive speech, but remains challeng- ing due to their acoustic diversity and imbalanced distribution in existing corpora. To address these challenges, we develop an NVV-aware DiTAR system that models continuous speech latents, encodes the 16 target NVV categories as dedicated to- kens, and adapts stop prediction to distinguish mid-utterance vocalizations from utterance boundaries. Training begins with large-scale bilingual pre-training on diverse NVV speech, fol- lowed by continued supervised fine-tuning on a corpus en- hanced through targeted synthetic augmentation and frequency- aware rebalancing. At inference time, we select the acoustic prompt, tune the LM-guidance and noise-injection scales, and apply Best-of-N sampling with multi-metric selection to re- duce generation failures. The final system achieves an official weighted bilingual score of 62.786, ranking first in Mandarin, second in English, and first overall among participating systems in Track 2 of the ISCSLP 2026 NVVSpeech Challenge. Ab- lation studies show that targeted augmentation benefits under- represented NVV categories the most, while robust candidate selection requires balancing NVV correctness, lexical fidelity, and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [38] [Exploring Multimodal Turn-Taking Cues in Face-to-Face Conversation using Voice Activity Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14666)
*Willem Berner,Julio Cesar Cavalcanti,Kalle Åström,Gabriel Skantze*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文扩展了VAP模型，引入视觉特征（注视、头部、身体手部姿态、面部动作单元）以提升面对面会话中的轮换预测，结果显示视觉信息优于纯音频基线，且全部特征融合效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮换是口语交互的基本组成部分，人类自然依赖言语与非言语信号，但现有对话系统通常仅使用音频线索。本文旨在探究面对面会话中的视觉特征能否在纯音频之外进一步增强轮换预测能力。

Method: 扩展自监督Transformer模型VAP（Voice Activity Projection）以预测未来语音活动，引入从大规模Meta Seamless Interaction双人面对面会话数据集中提取的视觉特征，包括注视方向、头部运动、身体与手部姿态以及面部动作单元（FAU）。融合方式探索了拼接、交叉注意力融合、差分特征和可训练门控机制。

Result: 视觉信息相比纯音频基线提升了性能；FAU的信息量显著高于其他特征组；身体和注视特征提供互补信息，融合全部特征的模型表现最佳。此外，特定任务上的性能因训练与测试数据来自即兴（表演）还是自然（非表演）会话而有所不同。

Conclusion: 视觉特征可有效增强轮换预测，其中面部动作单元最具信息量，多类特征融合效果最优；同时，即兴与自然会话数据对特定任务性能存在影响，提示数据来源对模型泛化的重要性。

Abstract: Turn-taking is a fundamental component of spoken interaction, and while humans naturally rely on both verbal and non-verbal signals, dialogue systems usually depend on audio cues alone. This paper investigates whether visual features from face-to-face conversations can enhance turn-taking prediction beyond what is achievable from audio-only. We extend the Voice Activity Projection (VAP) model, a self-supervised transformer-based model for predicting future voice activity, by incorporating visual features extracted from the large-scale Meta Seamless Interaction dataset of dyadic face-to-face conversations. The visual features include gaze direction, head movement, body and hand pose, and facial action units (FAU). For incorporating the visual features, we explore concatenation, cross-attention fusion, delta features, and trainable gating mechanisms. Incremental learning across domains can be regarded as a recurrent update, in which the current model is obtained by updating the model carried over from previous domains. Conventional DIL approaches that rely on domain-invariant feature learning and weight regularization gradually overwrite or constrain parameters learned in previous domains, leading to catastrophic forgetting. Instead, this paper proposes a new domain-specific parameter-isolation architecture that retains all past domains. The proposed architecture mitigates catastrophic forgetting through a full-order recurrent update, constructing a new expert using domain-specific data conditioned on all previously frozen models. To achieve this, we incorporate data-free generative replay to reconstruct previous-domain data and cross-domain feature generation to recover later expert features missing from earlier domain samples. Finally, we apply the proposed model architecture to domain-agnostic incremental learning for audio classification, as defined in the DCASE 2026 Challenge Task 7. *Homayoon Beigi,Grace Conneely*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个有限差分弓弦模型，采用隐式求解的Stribeck摩擦，并据此诊断物理regime、检验Schelleng弓力极限、比较学习型弓控制器的泛化与边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望避免对摩擦、阻抗和品质因数的经验拟合，用已发表的实测参数构建可直接离散求解的弓弦物理模型；同时澄清现有regime诊断指标（无粘滞检验的分类器、谐波性度量）可能把小幅周期滑移误判为Helmholtz运动的问题，并系统评估学习型控制器在物理可玩范围内的能力边界。

Method: 建立有限差分弓弦模型，对Stribeck摩擦做隐式求解以保证离散网格上能形成粘滞相；摩擦、阻抗与品质因数取自已发表测量值。用regime诊断、四根弦上的Schelleng弓力上下限、以及六种容量匹配的学习型控制器（前馈与门控循环网络）在四根弦、每根二十个随机种子下的表现进行比较；另设置rigid finger stop以检验模型不变性，并用回归分析界定规则与学习控制器之间的关系，考察训练损失对容量和上下文长度的选择作用。

Result: 只有隐式求解才能定量捕捉粘滞摩擦，滞后接触力在任何弓力下都无法形成粘滞相。四根弦的粘滞占比为89.1%，接近理想值90%；Schelleng最大弓力在各弦上均被复现，但最小弓力不成立：实际遵循Z v_b β^{-1}，而非预测的Z^2 v_b β^{-2}，两处平方依赖降为一次。六种容量匹配控制器中，门控循环网络多数情况下优于前馈网络；前馈网络仅在模型可玩性图判定位于Helmholtz区域之外的起点上完成更多行程。最小门控变体失败，因为仅由输入计算的门无法清除锁存状态。训练损失既不决定容量也不决定上下文长度，且没有任何学习控制器优于生成其标签的查表规则；回归显示学习控制器得分对规则得分的斜率为0.32，低于1超过十个标准误，说明规则失效时控制器可超越规则，规则有效时则受规则约束。在刚性指止下被控对象可证明不变，因此音高间的迁移损失仅属于控制器，并可追溯到某一个特征。无粘滞检验的regime分类器会把小幅周期滑移标为Helmholtz运动，谐波性度量也会把弓从未真正抓住的弦评为高于Helmholtz运动。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了定量可靠的有限差分弓弦摩擦模型与regime诊断，修正了Schelleng最小弓力的标度、澄清了学习控制器相对查表规则的能力边界与局限性，并指出若干regime判据的失效模式。

Abstract: A finite-difference bowed-string model with implicitly resolved Stribeck friction is presented, with a regime diagnostic, the Schelleng bow-force limits on four strings, and a comparison of learned bow controllers. Implicit resolution is necessary, and quantitatively so: a lagged contact force cannot capture the string on a discrete grid, so no stick phase forms at any bow force. With friction, impedance and quality factor taken from published measurement rather than fitted, all four strings return a stick fraction of 89.1% against an ideal 90%. Schelleng's maximum bow force is recovered on every string. The minimum is not: it follows $Z v_b β^{-1}$ rather than the predicted $Z^2 v_b β^{-2}$, reducing both squared dependences to first powers. Six controllers at matched capacity, over four strings and twenty seeds each, place a gated recurrent network ahead of a feedforward one, by most under a mid-stroke disturbance. The feedforward network completes more strokes only from a start the model's own playability map places outside the Helmholtz region. A minimal gated variant fails because gates computed from the input alone cannot clear a latched state. Training loss selects neither the capacity nor the context length, and no learned controller improves on the lookup rule that generated its labels. That bound has a domain. Regressing the controller's score on the rule's gives a slope of 0.32, more than ten standard errors below unity, so the controller overtakes the rule where the rule fails and is bounded by it where it holds. Under a rigid finger stop the plant is provably invariant, so transfer loss between pitches belongs to the controller alone and is traced to one feature. A regime classifier without a stick test labels small-amplitude periodic slipping as Helmholtz motion, and a harmonicity measure rates a string the bow never grips above Helmholtz motion.

</details>

Abstract: Speaker recognition neural networks learn latent representations (i.e. speaker embeddings) from input utterances to recognise speaker identities. However, the internal mechanisms of these networks remain largely opaque, motivating research in explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) to understand them. Nevertheless, existing studies have analysed how speaker embeddings are organised, but rarely frame these analyses within XAI. Hence, this work proposes to explain and interpret the organisation of speaker embeddings from an XAI perspective.

The resulting hierarchical organisation (i.e. hierarchical clusters) is evaluated using the Cluster-Class Matching (CCM) method. Moreover, we propose a new method, termed Hierarchical Cluster-Class Matching (HCCM), to identify which hierarchical clusters best match individual semantic classes (e.g. male) and conjunctive semantic classes (e.g. UK & male), thereby interpreting the clusters using their matched classes. The matching degree is quantified using a new metric called the L-score, which makes imperfect matches diagnosable. HCCM's results show that hierarchical clusters analysed by SLINK are interpreted using different classes related to speaker identity, gender, and nationality, providing insight into semantics within the hierarchical organisation of our examined speaker embeddings.

TL;DR: 本文发现语音大模型在"边生成文本边生成声学token"的联合模式下，内部文本答案准确率显著低于纯文本模式，称之为输出模式差距（OMG），并提出联合输出在线策略蒸馏（JO-OPD）来缩小该差距。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错生成文本与声学token是语音大模型实现流式语音回复的常见方式，但生成的声学token会进入上下文并影响后续文本预测。作者观察到：在相同语音输入下，S2TS（语音到文本再语音）模式内部文本的答案准确率明显低于S2T（纯语音到文本）模式，这一差距即为OMG。该问题会损害语音回复内容的质量，因此需要加以缓解。

Method: 提出Joint-Output On-Policy Distillation（JO-OPD）。它以学生模型自身生成的S2TS轨迹为数据，将同一模型中较强的S2T策略蒸馏到联合生成过程中：在每个文本位置，S2T教师基于学生前序输出的纯文本投影给出软目标，而学生则从完整的交错历史进行预测；此外引入保留目标，对原生非文本预测进行正则化。

Result: 在Step-Audio-2-mini与Baichuan-Audio-Instruct两种交错生成架构上均验证了OMG的存在。在Step-Audio-2-mini上，JO-OPD将Spoken-MQA的OMG从42.87个百分点降至16.26，将语音渲染GSM8K的OMG从29.72个百分点降至13.04，S2T准确率基本不变，且降幅显著大于匹配的SFT基线。基于ASR的评估显示Spoken-MQA的口语答案准确率提升7.49个百分点。

Abstract: Autoregressive generation of interleaved text and acoustic tokens is a common approach to spoken-response generation in speech large language models. At each text position, the S2T teacher provides soft targets from a text-only projection of the student's preceding outputs, while the student predicts from the corresponding full interleaved history. A preservation objective further regularizes native non-text predictions. Experiments on Step-Audio-2-mini and Baichuan-Audio-Instruct reveal OMG across two interleaved generation architectures. On Step-Audio-2-mini, JO-OPD reduces OMG from 42.87 to 16.26 percentage points on Spoken-MQA and from 29.72 to 13.04 points on speech-rendered GSM8K, with little change in S2T accuracy and substantially larger reductions than matched SFT baselines. ASR-based evaluation further shows a 7.49-point improvement in spoken-answer accuracy on Spoken-MQA.

Abstract: Lossless audio coding is a highly mature field of research, with limited potential for significant improvements in pure compression performance. Existing standalone lossless codecs cannot achieve this seamless switching without discontinuity. In this paper, we propose a lossless codec based on time-domain aliasing cancellation (TDAC) that can match the overlap in the CELT mode of the Opus codec. This allows the transition between lossy and lossless coding to be achieved without discontinuity or the transmission of redundant information. We show that the proposed codec still achieves state-of-the-art lossless compression without being penalized by its use of TDAC.

Result: ALM生成的伪标签相比原始ALM评分能提升训练效果；VoxPA在所有基线中取得最佳表现。

Conclusion: OpenEnded为开放式任务的口语评测提供句级多维标注资源，伪标注策略与VoxPA基线可为ASA研究提供有效参考。

Abstract: The development of automated speaking assessment (ASA) is limited by the scarcity of public datasets, with most existing work relying on read-aloud speech, which limits applicability to real-world communication scenarios. In this work, we introduce OpenEnded, a corpus of English practice speech from Mandarin speakers in open-response tasks. Unlike prior open-response datasets that provide only holistic proficiency scores, OpenEnded offers utterance-level assessments of accuracy, fluency, and prosody. Approximately 10,000 utterances are collected and annotated using a hybrid framework: 1,000 are manually labeled via multi-rater scoring with discrepancy resolution to form a high-quality test set, while the remaining are pseudo-labeled by an audio language model (ALM) for training and development sets. We evaluate ALMs and existing ASA models on the OpenEnded test set and introduce VoxPA as an additional baseline. Results show that ALM-generated pseudo-labels improve training over original ALM scoring, while VoxPA achieves the best performance among all baselines.

</details>

*Lennart Uphaus,André Merboldt,Markus Hofbauer,Timo Gerkmann*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该文提出一种面向助听设备的低延迟（10 ms）深度神经网络方向性滤波方法，利用FiLM在推理阶段调控指向性模式，并通过保持跨通道频谱关系的损失函数维持目标指向性，取得与宽松延迟约束方法相近的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方向性滤波虽能在推理阶段自适应调整指向性模式的方向与形状，但忽略了真实助听场景的重要约束：场景高度动态、麦克风位置随头径与助听器佩戴位置变化、存在头影效应，以及严格的延迟限制。

Method: 提出低延迟（10 ms）DNN，将上述助听设备需求纳入设计；采用特征级线性调制（FiLM）在测试阶段调控指向性模式；并提出一种保持跨通道频谱关系的损失函数，以维持期望的指向性模式。

Result: 所提方法在严格延迟约束下，能够达到与放宽延迟约束的方法相似的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在满足助听设备实际约束（动态场景、麦克风位置变化、头影效应与低延迟）的前提下，仍可实现高质量的指向性模式调控，且性能接近高延迟方案。

Abstract: Latest advances in neural directional filtering show exceptional results in adapting the direction and shape of directivity patterns during the inference phase. However, in the existing methods for adapting directivity patterns during inference, important real-world constraints have been disregarded. Particularly for hearing devices, scenarios are often much more dynamic, microphone positions vary with head diameter and hearing aid placement, head-shadow effects occur, and strict latency constraints apply. In this work, we propose a novel low-latency (10 ms) deep neural network (DNN) taking the above requirements of hearing devices into account. As in recent work, we use feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM) to steer the directivity patterns during testing. To preserve the desired directivity pattern, a loss function is proposed that maintains the spectral cross-channel relationships. Interestingly, we are able to achieve similar results to methods with relaxed latency constraints.

</details>


### [48] [Acoustic Image Source Interpolation with Optimal Transport Barycenter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15981)
*Yuyang Liu,Rumeshika Pallewela,Jesper Brunnström,Isabel Haasler,Filip Elvander*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出基于最优传输重心框架的镜像源点云插值方法，可从已知源位置的点云推断新源位置的ISPC，无需重复声学测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 房间脉冲响应可通过镜像源模型与镜像源点云估计，但源移动会改变ISPC，在新源位置估计ISPC通常需要重复声学测量，效率低且不灵活。

Method: 采用最优传输重心框架，从已知源的ISPC插值新源位置的ISPC，联合估计镜像源关联与目标ISPC。OT地面代价利用镜像源与其物理源具有相同位移的特性。方法实现为网格化与无支撑两种配置；无支撑方法通过交替进行跨ISPC的镜像源关联识别与目标镜像源位置优化，求解非凸联合估计问题。

Result: 实现了无需重复测量的ISPC插值，可支持高效、灵活的房间声学建模。

Conclusion: 该OT重心框架为源移动场景下的ISPC估计提供了免重复测量的插值途径，适用于网格化与无支撑配置，促进高效灵活的房间声学建模。

Abstract: Room impulse responses can be estimated via the image source model (ISM) using the image source point cloud (ISPC) of a physical source. However, because the source movement changes the ISPC, estimating the ISPC at a new source position typically requires repeated acoustic measurements. We propose an optimal transport (OT) barycenter framework to interpolate the ISPC of a new source location from ISPCs of known sources. The method jointly estimates image-source associations and the ISPC at the new location. The OT ground cost exploits the property that the image sources undergo the same displacement as their physical sources. This approach is realized for both grid-based and support-free configurations. The support-free method addresses the resulting nonconvex joint estimation problem by alternating between identifying image-source associations across the ISPCs and refining the target image-source locations. This enables the interpolation of ISPCs without repeated measurements, facilitating efficient and flexible room-acoustic modeling.

</details>

### [49] [Extreme Events in an Active Fluid Medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14334)
*Joydeep Das,Abhishek Chaudhuri,Sudeshna Sinha*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 该文研究由两种调控活性应力的化学物种构成的活性流体，发现其在合并-涌现孤子动力学与时空混沌区制下出现极端事件，并揭示其受Péclet数与非线性生长项调控，约化模型表明极端事件与吸引子碰撞危机导致的吸引子尺寸突增相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端事件（如暴涨、突变）在活性流体系统中的存在性尚不明确，而活性输运在生物过程中作用重要，因此有必要探究活性流体中极端事件的产生机制及其参数依赖。

Method: 构建含两种化学物种的活性流体模型：一为慢扩散物种，一为快扩散物种，后者生长采用非线性logistic项；通过时间序列、分岔图、概率分布函数、回归映射及事件间隔分布分析极端事件；系统考察Péclet数与非线性生长强度的影响；并建立截断模态的约化耦合微分方程模型进行对比验证。

Result: 在浓度的时间演化与空间剖面中均观测到极端事件，出现在孤子式合并-涌现动力学和时空混沌区制；慢扩散物种在孤子区制中表现出极端事件的显著成簇和超极端事件；极端事件概率在超过临界Péclet数后上升，而增强非线性则抑制极端事件；约化模型同样呈现极端事件，其出现与吸引子碰撞引发的危机所导致的吸引子尺寸突增相关。

Conclusion: 结果证实活性流体系统中存在极端事件，其发生受Péclet数与非线性共同调控，并与吸引子危机机制相联系，对涉及活性输运的生物现象具有潜在意义。

Abstract: We observe the emergence of extreme events in an active fluid involving two distinct chemical species that regulate active stress. One species is slow diffusing and the other is fast diffusing, and the growth of the fast-diffusing species is modelled using a nonlinear logistic term. We demonstrate the presence of extreme events in the temporal evolution of the concentrations, as well as in the spatial profile of the system,in regimes of merging-emerging soliton-like dynamics and spatio-temporal chaos, through analysis of the time-series, bifurcation diagrams, probability distribution functions of the concentration of the two species, return maps and distributions of inter-event intervals. Interestingly, we also find evidence of pronounced bunching of extreme events and super-extreme events in the slow chemical species in the soliton-like regime. We go on to systematically explore the dependence of the extreme event occurrences on the Péclet number and the strength of the nonlinear growth term, and find that the probability of extreme events increases after a critical Péclet number, while increasing the nonlinearity suppresses extreme events. Lastly, in order to gain further insight, we investigate a modified mode-truncated reduced order model comprising of coupled differential equations mimicking this active fluid system. We find that this reduced order model also exhibits extreme events whose emergence is correlated with a sudden expansion in attractor size due to a crisis arising from attractor collision.So these results demonstrate the existence of extreme events in an active fluid system, and are of potential relevance to biological phenomena where active transport plays an important role.

</details>


### [50] [Deformation of sine-Gordon two-soliton solutions in $\varphi^4$ kink-antikink configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15091)
*Aliakbar Moradi Marjaneh,Danial Saadatmand,Fabiano C. Simas,Jarah Evslin,Dionisio Bazeia*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文通过场变形将可积sine-Gordon系统的精确解映射到非可积φ⁴模型，构造出解析的kink-antikink构型，并与标准加法叠加拟设比较真空结构与运动方程残差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非可积φ⁴模型中kink-antikink散射与共振现象的研究通常依赖数值模拟，需要数学一致、拓扑合理且物理动机充分的初始数据；而标准加法叠加拟设在小间距下并非最优近似，因此希望借助可积系统的精确解构造更好的解析构型。

Method: 通过场变形（field deformation）将可积sine-Gordon系统的精确soliton-antisoliton解映射到非可积φ⁴模型，得到两类由初始半间距和速度参数化的K-K̄构型；随后将其与标准加法叠加拟设比较真空结构和运动方程残差，并推导残差积分平方的封闭表达式。

Result: 推导出各构型对应的积分平方残差的显式闭式表达式。在小间距下，映射得到的soliton-antisoliton构型残差明显小于朴素叠加拟设，而朴素叠加拟设又优于映射的双soliton构型。

Conclusion: 映射场虽非φ⁴运动方程的精确解，但可提供数学一致、拓扑合理且物理动机充分的初始数据，适用于kink-antikink散射与共振现象的数值研究。

Abstract: In this work, we construct analytical kink-antikink $(K\bar{K})$ configurations in the non-integrable $\varphi^4$ model by mapping exact solutions of the integrable sine-Gordon system via a field deformation. This procedure yields two distinct classes of configurations, parametrized by the initial half-separation and velocity. We compare these profiles with the standard additive superposition Ansatz in terms of vacuum structure and equation-of-motion residuals, deriving explicit closed-form expressions for the corresponding integrated squared residuals. For small separations, the mapped soliton-antisoliton configurations exhibit appreciably smaller residuals than the naive superposition Ansatz, which in turn performs better than the mapped two-soliton configurations. Although the mapped fields are not exact solutions of the $\varphi^4$ equation of motion, they provide mathematically consistent, topologically sound, and physically motivated initial data for numerical studies of kink-antikink scattering and resonance phenomena.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>
