### [51] [The Limits of Reference-Free Speech Quality Metrics as Evaluators and Rewards on Modern Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13150)
*Antonis Asonitis,Juan Pablo Zuluaga Gomez,Francesco Verdini,Aref Farhadipour,Marzieh Razavi,Pierre-Edouard Honnet,Vijeta Avijeet*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文检验了UTMOS、DNSMOS、SCOREQ等无参考TTS质量预测器是否真正跟踪人类偏好，通过六个带人工评分的语料库上的成对比较协议发现：存在明显缺陷时预测器与听者一致，但当两句均干净时无单一预测器能可靠识别偏好样本，甚至不如"选最长音频"；校准后的复合信号最强但仍未达人类上限，且用单一分数做策略优化会诱发reward hacking。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无参考质量预测器（UTMOS、DNSMOS、SCOREQ）已成为TTS的默认自动评估器，并被越来越多地用作偏好优化的奖励信号。这两种用途都预设了预测分数能跟踪人类偏好，但该假设此前未被系统检验。作者希望验证这一前提，并诊断单一分数在何种情况下、因何原因失效。

Method: 构建成对比较评估协议：在六个覆盖从富含伪影到无缺陷TTS的人工评分语料库上，对每个预测器判断"其打分更高的片段是否为听者更偏好的片段"，并将可解释的韵律与信号处理特征纳入同一协议进行对比；同时以单一分数和复合信号分别作为策略优化的奖励进行后训练实验，并辅以独立留出评判器和人工听测。

Result: 当一方片段带有可听缺陷时，预测器倾向与听者一致；一旦两个片段都干净，没有任何单一预测器能可靠识别偏好样本，多个预测器的准确率甚至低于"直接选时长最长片段"的基线。经校准的互补信号复合体是所测试的最强评估器，但在最干净的音频上仍只能弥补与人类上限差距的一部分。后训练中，即使等权集成也在无校准数据时有效，而优化单一分数会引发reward hacking——指标趋近其最优，但独立留出评判器与人工听测均变差；复合奖励对此具有抵抗力并能改善模型。

Conclusion: 论文的贡献在于提出评估协议、给出各预测器在这些语料上的得分，并诊断单一分数失效的时机与原因，揭示当前无参考TTS质量预测器在无缺陷音频上跟踪人类偏好能力的根本局限。

Abstract: Reference-free quality predictors such as UTMOS, DNSMOS and SCOREQ are the de facto automatic evaluators for text-to-speech (TTS) and are increasingly adopted as reward signals for preference optimization. Both roles presuppose that the predicted score tracks human preference. In this work, we test this assumption across six human-rated corpora spanning the quality range from artifact-rich to defect-free TTS, evaluating each predictor on a pairwise task that asks whether the clip it scores higher is the clip listeners prefer, and we subject interpretable prosodic and signal-processing features to the same protocol. When one clip carries audible defects the predictors tend to agree with listeners. Once both clips are clean, no single predictor reliably identifies the preferred sample, and several fall below the accuracy of simply picking the longest-duration clip. A calibrated composite of complementary signals is the strongest evaluator we test, though on the cleanest audio it recovers only part of the gap to the human ceiling. Additionally, using even an equal-weighted ensemble of metrics helps as a post-training reward, where no calibration data is available. Optimizing a single score with policy optimization induces reward hacking, driving the metric toward its optimum while independent held-out judges and a human listening test deteriorate. The composite reward resists this behavior and tends to improve the model. Our contribution is the evaluation protocol, the predictor scores across these corpora, and the diagnosis of when and why single scores fail.

</details>


### [52] [From Masking to Merging: Rethinking SpecAugment for Efficient Audio Spectrogram Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13260)
*Minhee Park,Hyowon Ahn,Chanwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出SpecAugment-Patch Merging方法，通过对被掩码的频谱图块进行合并以减少Transformer处理的token数量，从而加速AST训练，吞吐量提升约13.9%而精度几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频频谱图Transformer（AST）训练成本较高，处理大量token导致计算开销大，亟需在保持精度的前提下加速训练。

Method: 先对输入频谱图在patch级别应用SpecAugment进行掩码，在加入位置嵌入后，选取r对掩码patch进行合并，从而减少Transformer需处理的token数量；通过调节合并对数量r实现速度与精度的权衡。

Result: 在AudioSet上将r从0增至100时，mAP几乎不变（34.07→34.08），吞吐量从43.3提升至49.3 samples/sec，相对提升13.9%；在ESC-50和Speech Commands V2上呈现类似规律，吞吐量稳步提升而精度变化很小。

Conclusion: 该合并方法能以极小的性能损失换取更快的训练速度，是一种简单有效的AST训练加速方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes SpecAugment-Patch Merging, a simple yet effective method to accelerate Audio Spectrogram Transformer (AST) training. We first apply SpecAugment to mask input spectrograms at the patch level, and after positional embeddings are added, the method selects r pairs of masked patches and merges them, reducing the number of tokens processed by the Transformer. Increasing the number of merged pairs r from 0 to 100 keeps mAP on AudioSet nearly unchanged (34.07 to 34.08) while throughput increases from 43.3 to 49.3 samples/sec, which is a relatively 13.9% improvement. Similar patterns appear on ESC-50 and Speech Commands V2, where throughput steadily improves with only minor accuracy changes, demonstrating that this merging approach provides faster training with minimal performance loss.

</details>


### [53] [ScorePrompts: Natural-Language Exploration of Symbolic Music Scores through Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13291)
*Emmanouil Karystinaios,Gerhard Widmer*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: ScorePrompts 是一个交互式乐谱分析系统：用户上传乐谱后可获得音乐结构的自然语言描述、针对特定片段的问答，并在五线谱上查看对应分析结果，同时保持中间结果可检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号音乐分析模型（MIR）输出多为数值或结构化结果，普通用户难以直接理解；而直接用语言模型从原始 MusicXML 推断音乐结构又不可靠。因此需要一个系统，将专业分析模型的输出转化为自然语言，并提供可追溯、可核查的解释性访问方式。

Method: 系统先由专业 MIR 组件分别估计和声、调性、终止式、曲式边界、织体与音符级角色，并将结果按音符、拍、小节、乐曲四个层级组织；随后用受 schema 约束的语言模型将这些结果转写为描述，而非直接从原始 MusicXML 推断结构。对于"第14–18小节有何变化"这类问题，采用确定性路由器选定相关小节与分析层级，返回简洁回答及底层结果和注意事项。Verovio 负责渲染乐谱并将返回信息链接到被引小节和音符级属性；界面还展示中间表格及各分析层级之间的分歧。

Result: 系统实现了自然语言描述、针对性问答与记谱可视化的一体化集成，并可呈现中间结果表与层级间分歧。该系统面向探索性乐谱分析与解释，而非乐谱编辑。

Conclusion: 演示表明，将现有分析模型、受约束的语言生成、问答检索与基于记谱的可视化结合，可在保持中间结果可检查的前提下，为符号音乐分析提供自然语言访问途径。

Abstract: We present ScorePrompts, an interactive system in which users upload a score, receive natural-language descriptions of its musical structure, ask questions about specific passages, and inspect the corresponding analysis results in staff notation. Specialist MIR components first estimate harmony, tonality, cadences, formal boundaries, texture, and note-level roles, organizing their outputs at note, beat, measure, and piece levels. A schema-constrained language model converts these results into descriptions rather than inferring musical structure directly from raw MusicXML. For questions such as "What changes in measures 14-18?", a deterministic router selects the relevant measures and analytical levels and returns a concise response together with the underlying results and caveats. Verovio renders the score and links the returned information to cited measures and note-level attributes. The interface also exposes intermediate tables and disagreements between analytical levels. ScorePrompts is intended for exploratory score analysis and explanation, not score editing. The demo shows how existing analysis models, constrained language generation, Q&A retrieval, and notation-based visualization can provide natural-language access to symbolic music analysis while keeping intermediate results inspectable.

</details>


### [54] [Predictive audio representations for early detection and tracking of hidden dynamic objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13595)
*Katerina Vinciguerra,Moritz Brandes,Danilo Hollosi,Letizia Marchegiani*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务音频感知系统，同时估计被遮挡交通参与者的数量、类型与到达方向；核心是JEPA式自监督预训练加BiLSTM多任务微调，并构建了多智能体非视距数据集，性能超越现有方法且可跨场景迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测潜在危险是安全的核心，而预测其他交通参与者的存在是危险预测的关键。被遮挡的交通参与者可能过晚才可见，导致自动驾驶车辆缺乏足够时间识别、规划并安全应对。已有研究表明听觉感知具有全向性且不受视野限制，可为早期发现被遮挡的道路使用者提供线索，但现有工作仅针对单车场景，且只能识别车辆类型或估计到达方向。

Method: 提出两阶段多任务流水线：第一阶段为受联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）启发的自监督预训练，直接作用于多通道原始波形，编码器在无标签条件下由过去上下文预测未来音频片段的潜在表示；第二阶段为监督式多任务微调，采用双向LSTM与三个分类头，同时估计车辆数量、类型与到达方向。由于无合适公开数据集，作者自行采集了包含多个交通智能体同时运行的非视距场景数据集。

Result: 实验评估表明，该方法优于现有技术水平；同时验证了其设计选择使模型能学习到鲁棒的表示，并可迁移到未见过的驾驶场景，保持合理且稳定的性能。

Conclusion: 多任务音频感知结合JEPA式自监督预训练可有效解决多名被遮挡交通参与者的数量、类型与方向联合估计问题，且具备跨场景泛化能力，为非视距危险预测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Predicting potential dangers is core to safety. Forecasting the presence of other traffic agents is core to danger prediction. Occluded traffic agents challenge detection systems as they might become visible too late, leaving the autonomous vehicle too little time to identify, plan and act accordingly in a robust and safe way. Previous works proved that auditory perception, being omnidirectional and not constrained by a field-of-view, provides fundamental cues for early spotting of different road users, even when hidden by other vehicles or infrastructures. Yet, those contributions deal with scenarios with only one vehicle present, and they either identify the type of vehicle or estimate its direction of arrival. In this work, we move forward, and propose a multi-task system which, simultaneously, estimates the number of vehicles present, their type, and their direction of arrival. Our methodological contribution is a two-stage pipeline: a self-supervised pre-training stage inspired by the Joint- Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) applied directly to multichannel raw waveforms, followed by supervised multi-task fine-tuning with a bidirectional LSTM and three classification heads. The pre-training stage trains the encoder without labels, pushing it to predict the latent representation of a future audio segment from its past context. To train and test our framework, since no suitable dataset was publicly available, we collected an ad-hoc one covering Non-Line-Of-Sight scenarios with multiple traffic agents simultaneously operating. Experimental evaluation shows that our method outperforms the state of the art; it also proves that our design choice allows the model to learn robust representations, which can be transferred to an unseen driving scenario, maintaining reasonable and stable performance.

</details>


### [55] [UniqueShip: Mitigating Data Leakage in Acoustic Ship Classification Benchmark Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13659)
*Connor Hashemi,Trevor Stout,Anthony Hoogs,Jason Parham*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出UniqueShip——一个面向水下声学目标识别（UATR）的机器学习基准数据集，源自公开的Ocean Networks Canada（ONC）仓库，包含4,218艘独特船只、2,460小时舰船辐射音频（含背景共3,437小时），并强调控制训练/评估集间的数据泄漏以提升模型评估可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UATR适合机器学习方法，但受限于缺乏大规模、多样且公开可用的标注数据集；同时已有数据集常采用随机划分，导致数据泄漏与虚高评估结果，无法真实反映模型的泛化能力。

Method: 1) 基于ONC公开仓库构建UniqueShip基准数据集，严格控制训练集与评估集之间的数据泄漏，避免模型记忆个体船只；2) 对比随机划分与谨慎划分在两个主流UATR数据集上的性能差异；3) 在UniqueShip上进行消融实验，分别考察独特船只数量与总音频时长对精度的影响；4) 提供卷积与Transformer骨干的基线模型，并分析船只元数据与分类性能的相关性。

Result: 随机划分导致虚高测试性能，相比谨慎划分精度提升10–48个百分点；将独特船只数量翻倍可提升精度2.4–2.6个百分点，而总音频时长翻倍仅提升0.8–1.3个百分点；单个船只特征对分类难度的预测能力远强于仅依据到水听器距离的预测。数据集含2,460小时音频、4,218艘独特船只（含背景3,437小时）。

Conclusion: 船体多样性应比总时长更主导数据集构建策略；UniqueShip为UATR研究提供了可复现、无泄漏的基准，数据集、代码与易下载划分已发布于uniqueshipdata.org以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Underwater Acoustic Target Recognition (UATR) of ships is well-suited for machine learning, yet its progress is hindered by the lack of large, diverse, and publicly available labeled datasets. In this work, we introduce UniqueShip, a machine learning-ready benchmark dataset for UATR applications sourced from the open Ocean Networks Canada (ONC) repository. Unlike previous datasets, we explicitly control for "data leakage" between the training and evaluation sets to ensure more reliable and generalizable model evaluation that does not encourage the model to memorize individual ships. We demonstrate that typical, random data partitioning in two prominent UATR datasets leads to falsely optimistic test performance, increasing accuracy by 10-48 percentage points compared to our more careful partitioning. Ablations on UniqueShip further show that doubling the number of unique vessels improves accuracy by 2.4-2.6 percentage points, while doubling total audio duration improves only by 0.8-1.3 points, indicating that vessel diversity should drive dataset curation more than total hours. We provide baselines with convolutional and transformer backbones, and analyze how ship metadata correlates with classification performance, finding that individual vessel characteristics predict classification difficulty far better than distance to the hydrophone alone. Overall, UniqueShip contains 2,460 hours of ship-radiated audio from 4,218 unique vessels (3,437 hours including background). We publish the dataset, code, and easy-to-download splits at uniqueshipdata.org to foster further UATR research.

</details>


### [56] [The VoiceMOS Challenge 2026: Evaluating Speech Enhancement, Emotional TTS and Accented TTS Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13792)
*Wen-Chin Huang,Wei Wang,Marvin Sach,Xiaoxue Gao,Nicholas Sanders,Erica Cooper,Toda Tomoki*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文介绍VoiceMOS Challenge 2026的结果：挑战赛重新聚焦语音评测，设三条赛道（增强语音绝对/比较类别评分预测、情感TTS自然度与情感相关任务预测、基于编解码器语音合成系统的说话人与口音相似度预测），吸引18支队伍参赛，多数超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前几届（尤其2025年）将评测范围扩展到音乐与通用音频，导致评测目标分散；本届希望重新聚焦于语音这一核心对象，并覆盖增强语音、情感TTS、编解码器语音合成等新兴场景下的主观评估自动预测需求。

Method: 组织VoiceMOS Challenge 2026（第五届），设定三条评测赛道：1）增强语音的绝对与比较类别评分（category ratings）预测；2）情感文本转语音系统的自然度及情感相关任务预测；3）基于编解码器的语音合成系统的说话人与口音相似度预测。以官方提供的基线系统作为比较对象，汇总各参赛团队成绩与代表性优胜系统。

Result: 共吸引全球18支团队参赛，其中大多数团队成功超越官方基线。论文汇总了挑战赛结果、代表性顶尖系统、参赛者反馈以及未来届次的改进方向。

Conclusion: 本届挑战赛通过重新聚焦语音并细分任务赛道，有效推动了主观语音评估自动预测的研究；多数参赛系统优于基线，说明该领域方法总体在进步，后续届次将依据参赛者反馈继续调整赛道与评测设计。

Abstract: We present the results of the VoiceMOS Challenge 2026, the fifth edition of a scientific challenge on automatic prediction of subjective speech assessments. After expanding the scope to music and general audio in 2025, we refocused the evaluation target on speech and organized three tracks: prediction of absolute and comparative category ratings for enhanced speech, prediction of naturalness and emotion-related tasks for emotional text-to-speech systems, and prediction of speaker and accent similarity for codec-based speech synthesis systems. The challenge attracted a total of 18 teams worldwide, with most teams successfully surpassing the provided baselines. We summarize the challenge results, representative top-performing systems, participant feedback, and directions for future editions.

</details>


### [57] [CRAF: Cross-View Residual-Aware Fusion for Deepfake Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13842)
*Minh-Xuan Phan,Khalid Zaman,Candy Olivia Mawalim,Masashi Unoki*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出CRAF跨视图残差感知融合框架，通过ALLM引导的跨视图注意力增强SSL表征，并利用残差提取与自适应门控融合，提升对未见欺骗攻击的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造语音日趋逼真，对未见欺骗攻击的泛化成为关键挑战。SSL模型擅长细粒度低层声学特征，ALLM提供高层上下文表征，二者互补，但直接融合无法显式分离共享信息与视图特有信息，限制跨视图融合效果。

Method: 提出CRAF（跨视图残差感知融合）框架：使用ALLM引导的跨视图注意力丰富SSL表征，并以ALLM作为高层参考，将ALLM可解释信息与SSL互补残差信息分离；残差经自适应门控选择性精炼，并通过SSL主导的融合方式整合。

Result: 在ASVspoof 5数据集上，CRAF结合Kimi-Audio取得EER 5.96%、minDCF 0.1192，展现出对未见欺骗攻击的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CRAF通过显式分离跨视图共享与互补信息，有效融合SSL与ALLM表征，验证了其在未见欺骗攻击场景下的泛化优势。

Abstract: Recent advances in speech synthesis and voice conversion have made deepfake speech increasingly realistic, making generalization to unseen spoofing attacks a critical challenge. Pretrained speech and audio models offer a promising direction for improving robustness to such unseen attacks. Self-supervised learning (SSL) models capture fine-grained, low-level acoustic characteristics, whereas Auditory Large Language Models (ALLMs) provide higher-level contextual representations. These complementary views can provide useful cues for improving generalization to unseen attacks. However, direct fusion does not explicitly disentangle information shared across the two views from view-specific complementary information, limiting effective cross-view integration. To address this, we propose CRAF, a cross-view residual-aware fusion framework that uses ALLM-guided cross-view attention to enrich SSL representations and adopts ALLM as a high-level reference to separate ALLM-explainable information from complementary SSL residual information. The residual is selectively refined through adaptive gating and integrated through SSL-primary fusion. Experiments on ASVspoof 5 show that CRAF with Kimi-Audio achieves an EER of 5.96% and a minDCF of 0.1192, demonstrating robustness to unseen spoofing attacks.

</details>


### [58] [Musical Timing in Studio Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13881)
*Konstantinos Tsioutas,George Xylomenos*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 研究分析391首多轨录音，探究录音方式（同空间合录、分轨叠录）对乐手时间协同精度的影响，并开发基于Mel频谱相似度的声音泄漏自动检测方法推断录音条件。结果表明存在共享房间声学与声音泄漏的录音时间变异性更低，而分轨叠录与更高的时间变异性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 录音技术从共享声学空间的现场合录到隔离棚录制与分轨叠录不等，这些方式在物理共在、视觉接触、声音泄漏和声学隔离上存在差异，可能影响乐手间的音乐协同。既有研究尚不清楚录音风格是否影响时间精度，特别是乐手同处一个空间演奏是否能获得更紧密的时间协同。

Method: 分析两个数据集共391首多轨录音，测量乐手之间的时间关系；并采用自动声音泄漏检测方法推断每首歌的录音条件：计算轨道间成对的Mel频谱图相似度，并利用所得相似度矩阵的汇总统计量识别具有共享声学内容的录音。

Result: 显示出共享房间声学与声音泄漏证据的录音，其时间变异性低于高度隔离的录音；分轨叠录则与时间变异性增加相关联。

Conclusion: 录音风格影响乐手的时间协同精度：同空间合录更利于紧密的时间协调，而隔离与分轨叠录会降低时间精度。研究所用的声音泄漏检测方法可有效推断录音条件，为后续音乐协同研究提供了可推广的分析工具。

Abstract: Studio recording techniques vary considerably, ranging from live recordings in a shared acoustic space, to isolated booth recording and overdubbing, where musicians record their parts separately, while listening to previously recorded material. These approaches differ in terms of physical co-presence, visual contact, sound leakage, and acoustic isolation, factors that may influence musical coordination. This study investigates whether the recording style affects timing precision; specifically, whether musicians performing together in the same space achieve tighter temporal coordination. To address these questions, two datasets with a total of 391 multi-track recordings were analyzed to measure the timing relationships between musicians. In addition, an automated sound-leakage detection method was employed to infer the recording conditions of each song: pairwise Mel-spectrogram similarities between the tracks were computed, and summary statistics from the resulting similarity matrices were used to identify recordings with shared acoustic content. The results suggest that recordings showing evidence of shared room acoustics and sound leakage tend to exhibit lower timing variability than highly isolated recordings, whereas overdubbing is associated with increased timing variability.

</details>


### [59] [DiTAR+: Dual Optimization for Robust Autoregressive Diffusion Speech Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13909)
*Ziyu Zhang,Tianlun Zuo,Hanzhao Li,Haoyu Zhang,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出DiTAR+，一个针对连续隐变量自回归扩散Transformer（AR-DiT）的双优化框架，通过扩张上下文采样与分层声学掩码解决长语音合成的解码不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR-DiT模型在零样本语音生成中潜力巨大，但在合成长语句或复杂语言结构时解码稳定性有限。该不稳定性主要源于扩散解码器历史感受野受限及声学惯性依赖，导致模型忽略语义条件。

Method: 提出DiTAR+双优化框架：1）扩张上下文采样（Dilated Context Sampling），在不破坏物理时间连续性的前提下扩展宏观历史感受野，防止累积误差传播；2）分层声学掩码（Hierarchical Acoustic Masking），阻止浅层关注声学前置上下文，显式解耦语义对齐与声学细节重建。

Result: 有效缓解发音错误与语义幻觉，增强困难句生成鲁棒性，并在长语音全程保持高说话人相似度。ZH-Hard集合上词错误率从12.478%降至9.893%；25-35秒扩展语句上说话人相似度从0.741提升至0.759，词错误率从2.778%降至2.173%，优于离散token和纯流匹配基线。

Conclusion: DiTAR+通过双重优化显著提升连续隐变量自回归扩散模型的解码稳定性与生成质量，在长语音及复杂语言结构场景下超越现有基线方法。

Abstract: Continuous-latent Autoregressive Diffusion Transformer (AR-DiT) models have demonstrated immense potential in zero-shot speech generation. However, they still suffer from limited decoding stability when synthesizing long utterances or complex linguistic structures. This instability primarily stems from a restricted historical receptive field and an acoustic inertia dependency within the diffusion decoder, which causes the model to ignore semantic conditions. To address these challenges, we propose DiTAR+, a dual-optimization framework. First, we introduce Dilated Context Sampling to expand the macro-level historical receptive field without violating physical temporal continuity, thereby preventing cumulative error propagation. Second, we propose Hierarchical Acoustic Masking to prevent shallow layers from attending to acoustic pre-context, explicitly decoupling semantic alignment from acoustic detail reconstruction. Extensive experiments show that our framework effectively mitigates pronunciation errors and semantic hallucinations, enhances generation robustness on challenging sentences, and maintains exceptionally high speaker similarity throughout the entirety of long-form utterances. On the linguistically challenging ZH-Hard set, DiTAR+ reduces the word error rate from 12.478% to 9.893%, and on extended utterances of 25 to 35 seconds it improves speaker similarity from 0.741 to 0.759 while simultaneously lowering the word error rate from 2.778% to 2.173%, outperforming both discrete-token and pure flow-matching baselines.

</details>


### [60] [StepAudio 3 Realtime Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14005)
*Bin Lin,Bo Zhao,Boyang Zhang,Boyong Wu,Chao Yan,Chen Geng,Chen Wu,Cheng Yi,Chengli Feng,Chenglin Zhu,Chengting Feng,Chengyuan Yao,Daijiao Liu,DanNi Wan,Daxin Jiang,Dongjian Li,Dongqing Pang,Fei Tian,Feng Tian,Future Li,Gang Yu,Guanglong Yang,Haoyang Zhang,Hongyuan Wang,Jia Peng,Jiahao Song,Jialong Xue,Jiamin Fan,Jiangjie Zhen,Jianzheng Gao,Jincheng Wen,Jinghua Liang,Jinglan Gong,Jun Chen,Li Xie,Liang Zhao,Lifang Zhang,Lingli Ji,Lun Cai,Min Xu,Peilin Li,Peng Yang,Pengfei Tan,Qingjian Lin,Qinxin Du,Ruijie Xiong,Runze Li,Shenghua Hu,Shengqian Qin,Shi Qiu,Siqi Tu,Siyi Zhou,Tianjiao Deng,Wanying Lu,Weiming Niu,Wen Sun,WenWen Qu,Xiangyu Zhang,Xianwei Zhang,Xiaosu Su,Xing Chen,Xinyu Liu,Xuerui Yang,Yan Wu,Yang Li,Yang Yang,Yechang Huang,Yibo Zhu,Yifan Zhang,Yinuo Yan,Youjun Chen,Yu Fu,Yu Luo,Yu Zhou,Yujie Chen,Yumang Wang,Yunzhou Ju,Yuxiang Yang,Yuxin Li,Yuxin Zhang,Zekai Liu,Zengwei Yao,Zhaoxin Yuan,Zhenwei Mou,Zhiquan Zhang,Zhiyue Wu,Zichao Li,Zichao Zhou,Ziqi Ren,Zixuan Wang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 StepAudio 3 Realtime 音频-语言基础模型，以"持续聆听-对话-思考-执行"闭环实现深度推理与实时交互的统一，在多项语音基准上达到领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时口语交互要求同时具备深度推理、即时响应与流畅的轮次转换能力，而深度思考带来的高延迟与实时交流的低延迟需求之间存在固有矛盾，现有模型难以兼顾。

Method: 模型围绕"聆听-对话-思考-执行"连续闭环设计：Deep Perception 模块捕捉丰富声学线索以理解用户意图；Seamless Duplex 对音频流进行同步建模，以自然处理停顿、附和与打断；核心机制 Think-While-Speaking 在语音输出的同时并行执行私有推理，从而化解深度推理与延迟之间的冲突；并集成 Voice Agent，在不打断对话流的前提下异步执行工具调用。

Result: 推理模式下在 StepAudioChat 上取得 73.0 宏平均；借助 Think-While-Speaking，在实时说话的同时达到与专用推理模型相当的对话与推理表现；MMSU 基准 90.6，Artificial Analysis Full-Duplex Bench 总体 98.9，τ-Voice 宏任务成功率 56.0%。

Conclusion: StepAudio 3 Realtime 通过"边说边想"的并行推理机制与全双工建模，在实时性、推理深度与工具执行能力之间取得平衡，在关键维度上实现顶尖性能，为实时语音智能体提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Realtime spoken interaction demands deep reasoning, prompt responses, and fluid turn-taking. We present StepAudio 3 Realtime, an audio-language foundation model organized around a continuous listen-converse-think-act loop. Deep Perception captures rich acoustic cues to interpret user intent, while Seamless Duplex models synchronized audio streams to handle pauses, backchannels, and interruptions naturally. Crucially, we resolve the tension between deep deliberation and latency via Think-While-Speaking, executing private reasoning in parallel with spoken delivery. In reasoning mode, StepAudio 3 reaches a 73.0 macro average on StepAudioChat. With Think-While-Speaking, it achieves dialogue and reasoning performance comparable to dedicated reasoning models while speaking in real time. Furthermore, an integrated Voice Agent handles asynchronous tool execution without disrupting the dialogue flow. StepAudio 3 Realtime achieves top-tier performance across key dimensions: an exceptional 90.6 on the MMSU benchmark, 98.9 Overall on the Artificial Analysis Full-Duplex Bench, and a 56.0% macro task-success rate on $τ$-Voice.

</details>


### [61] [Robust Cross-Domain Speech-Based Alzheimer's Disease Detection via Iterative Adversarial Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14139)
*Luqi Sun,Shreeram Suresh Chandra,Aurosweta Mahapatra,Emily Mower Provost,Brian MacWhinney,Berrak Sisman*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代对抗自训练（IAST）方法，用于跨域语音阿尔茨海默病（AD）检测的无监督域适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音AD检测方法多在单一数据集上训练与评估，模型依赖数据集特有的伪特征而非疾病相关的语音线索，导致跨域性能严重下降。实际应用中需要模型对录音环境、说话人和数据采集条件的变化具有鲁棒性。

Method: 采用无监督域适应，在缺少目标域诊断标签的条件下学习鲁棒的域不变特征表示，并在此基础上提出迭代对抗自训练（IAST）方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，IAST在多种跨域设置下显著提升了泛化能力与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: IAST为语音AD检测的跨域鲁棒性提供了一种有效的无监督域适应方案。

Abstract: As Alzheimer's disease (AD) has increasingly become a major global public health issue, speech-based AD detection has attracted widespread attention. However, most existing methods are trained and evaluated on a single dataset, often leading to severe cross-domain performance degradation due to reliance on dataset-specific artifacts rather than disease-related speech cues. In real-world applications, reliable Alzheimer's disease detection requires models that are robust to variations in recording environments, speakers and data collection conditions. To address this challenge, this paper adopts unsupervised domain adaptation to learn robust, domain-invariant feature representations in the absence of target-domain diagnosis labels. On this basis, a novel unsupervised domain adaptation method, Iterative Adversarial Self-Training (IAST), is proposed. Results demonstrate that IAST significantly improves the generalization ability and robustness under various cross-domain settings.

</details>


### [62] [A New Transformer-Based Approach for Audio-Based Kinship Verification and a New Uncontrolled Mandarin Kinship Speech Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14145)
*Qiyang Sun,Langqing Zhang,Yupei Li,Björn Schuller*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出基于音频的亲属关系验证新架构CONVTRAP-TN，并构建了贴近日常录音条件的语音数据集ARKIN，同时进行了消融实验与多任务基线实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频亲属关系验证研究较少，现有带亲属标签的语音数据集稀缺，且要么来自网络极度嘈杂的野外数据，要么要求说话人在特定环境录制，无法反映用户在日常无约束条件下用个人设备录音的真实场景。

Method: 提出CONVTRAP-TN架构用于音频亲属关系验证，首次将成功的Transformer架构应用于该任务，并对其开展消融研究；同时采集了自定义语音数据集ARKIN，该数据集能准确反映日常录音条件。

Result: 在采集的ARKIN数据集上进行了一系列初步基线实验，包括说话人验证与识别、语音识别、年龄估计、亲属关系验证，以及跨数据集亲属关系验证实验，结果表明现有方法在不同数据集间缺乏鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CONVTRAP-TN将Transformer引入音频亲属关系验证，配合更贴近真实场景的ARKIN数据集，为该任务提供了新的模型与数据基础；跨数据集实验揭示了现有方法在跨数据集场景下鲁棒性不足的问题。

Abstract: Kinship verification is a task involving determining whether two individuals share a first-order kin relation. To tackle this task, we propose CONVTRAP-TN, a new architecture for audio-based kinship verification, and conduct an ablation study on the proposed model. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to apply the successful transformer architecture to the task of audio-based kinship verification. Furthermore, we also collect a custom speech dataset, ARKIN, which accurately reflects everyday recording conditions. We do this because only a few speech datasets with kinship labels currently exist, all of which either source extremely noisy in-the-wild data from the internet, or instruct speakers to record in specific environments. These settings fail to reflect real-world scenarios where users record on personal devices under unrestrained conditions. Additionally, we perform a series of preliminary baseline experiments on the collected dataset, including speaker verification and recognition, speech recognition, age estimation, and kinship verification, as well as cross-dataset kinship verification experiments to show that existing methods are not robust across datasets.

</details>


### [63] [AURA: Unified Multimodal Framework for Conversational Music Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14344)
*Quoc-Huy Trinh,Minh-Van Nguyen,Debesh Jha*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: AURA是一个统一的多模态对话式音乐编辑框架，利用MLLM将完整对话历史、可选图像和参考音频提炼为概念token，注入冻结的MusicGen骨干实现精确编辑，仅优化91M参数，在Slakh2100和MoisesDB上显著优于现有指令引导方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令引导音乐编辑器独立处理每个请求，无法支持用户逐步细化音轨的工作流，缺乏对话上下文理解能力。

Method: 使用多模态大语言模型解读完整对话历史、可选图像及参考音频，提炼为紧凑概念token；概念到音频模块将这些token与帧对齐参考特征注入冻结的MusicGen骨干，实现精确编辑并保留未受影响内容。仅优化91M参数，冻结1.9B骨干参数。

Result: 在Slakh2100和MoisesDB上，编辑正确性和内容保留相比现有指令引导方法有显著提升，域外添加与移除任务的FAD降低4-5倍。

Conclusion: AURA通过统一多模态对话框架和轻量参数微调，实现了高效且高保真的对话式音乐编辑，验证了概念token注入冻结骨干范式的有效性。

Abstract: Instruction-guided music editors typically process each request independently, limiting their ability to support workflows in which users progressively refine a track. We introduce AURA, a unified multimodal framework for conversational music editing. AURA uses a multimodal large language model to interpret the complete dialogue history, an optional image, and reference audio, distilling the editing intent into compact concept tokens. A concept-to-audio module injects these tokens and frame-aligned reference features into a frozen MusicGen backbone, enabling precise edits while preserving unaffected content. AURA optimizes only 91M parameters while retaining 1.9B frozen backbone parameters. Experiments on Slakh2100 and MoisesDB demonstrate substantial improvements in edit correctness and content preservation over existing instruction-guided methods, including a 4-5 times reduction in FAD for out-of-domain addition and removal.

</details>


### [64] [Differentiable Digital Signal Processing Mixture Model-Guided Diffusion for Synthesis Parameter Estimation from Harmonic Sound Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14427)
*Kengo Takemoto,Tomohiko Nakamura,Hiroshi Saruwatari*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文在DDSP混合模型(DDSPMM)基础上引入去噪扩散概率模型(DDPM)正则化，通过DDPM生成合成参数并用重构误差引导其反向扩散过程，从而获得时间上合理的合成参数轨迹，改进木管与弦乐合奏的参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDSP自编码器通过基频、响度和音色特征重构单音谐波声音。为处理混合音，作者此前提出DDSPMM，将混合信号表示为多个预训练DDSP自编码器解码器合成信号之和。但DDSPMM未显式建模合成参数的时序变化，容易产生过度的时间抖动，估计轨迹不自然。因此需要引入时序合理性约束。

Method: 将DDPM作为合成参数的生成模型进行训练。在参数估计阶段，把DDPM嵌入DDSPMM估计框架，以其反向扩散过程生成候选参数，并用观测混合信号与DDSPMM基于当前估计合成信号之间的重构误差来引导反向扩散过程，从而在保证重构精度的同时施加时序合理性。

Result: 在木管与弦乐合奏数据上的实验表明，基于DDPM的正则化通过为估计轨迹施加时间合理性，改进了合成参数估计性能，缓解了DDSPMM原有的过度时间波动问题。

Conclusion: 将DDPM作为先验嵌入DDSPMM估计流程，并利用重构误差引导反向扩散，是一种有效提升混合谐波声音合成参数估计时序合理性的方法。

Abstract: A differentiable digital signal processing (DDSP) autoencoder reconstructs a monophonic harmonic sound through three types of synthesis parameters: fundamental frequency, loudness, and timbre features. To handle mixtures of harmonic sounds within the DDSP approach, we have previously proposed a DDSP mixture model (DDSPMM). It represents a mixture as the sum of source signals synthesized by the decoders of pretrained DDSP autoencoders. Although DDSPMM enables direct estimation of synthesis parameters of each source from mixtures, it does not explicitly model temporal variations in the synthesis parameters and can produce excessive temporal fluctuations. In this paper, we propose a method for estimating synthesis parameters with temporally plausible trajectories by incorporating a denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM) into the DDSPMM-based estimation. The DDPM is trained as a generative model of synthesis parameters. During estimation, the proposed method guides the DDPM reverse diffusion process with the reconstruction error between the observed mixture and the mixture synthesized by DDSPMM from the current estimates. Experiments on woodwind and string instrument ensembles showed that the DDPM-based regularization improves synthesis parameter estimation by imposing temporal plausibility on the estimated trajectories.

</details>


### [65] [Exploiting Speech LLM Representations for Multilingual and Cross-Lingual Parkinson's Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14431)
*Sarthak Giri,Zi Haur Pang,Tatsuya Kawahara*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了语音大模型（Speech LLM）编码器与解码器内部表征在帕金森病（PD）检测中的有效性，发现编码器表征普遍优于解码器，病理线索在向语言模型空间投影时被逐步削弱；并提出基于SE的动态层聚合框架，通过融合多层信息优于最佳单层选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音大模型已在多类任务上表现优异，但其在病理性语音分析（尤其是帕金森病检测）中的潜力尚未被充分探索；同时，模型内部不同组件（编码器/解码器）与生成式输出的临床可靠性缺乏系统比较，且PD相关声学线索在Transformer各层间的分布规律不明确。

Method: 在多语言与跨语言设置下，提取Speech LLM编码器与解码器的内部表征用于PD检测，并与生成式输出进行对比；为利用跨层分散的信息，提出基于Squeeze-and-Excitation（SE）的动态层聚合框架，对各Transformer层表征进行加权融合。

Result: 在多数模型与设置中，编码器表征一致优于解码器表征；音频表征投影到语言模型空间后，病理线索被逐步衰减；生成式输出在临床任务上可靠性低于内部表征；SE动态层聚合在多项实验中超过最佳单层选择，表明PD相关声学线索分布于多个Transformer层而非集中于单层。

Conclusion: 语音大模型用于病理性语音分析时，应优先采用编码器内部表征而非解码器或生成式输出；PD相关声学线索在层间呈分布特性，采用SE动态层聚合可有效提升检测性能，为Speech LLM在临床语音分析中的应用提供了表征利用策略。

Abstract: Speech Large Language Models (Speech LLMs) have shown strong performance across diverse tasks, yet their utility for pathological speech analysis remains underexplored. In this work, we investigate the effectiveness of internal representations from encoder and decoder components of Speech LLMs for Parkinson's Disease (PD) detection across multilingual and cross-lingual settings. Our findings reveal that encoder representations consistently outperform their decoder counterparts in most models and settings and that pathological cues may be progressively attenuated as audio representations are projected into the language model space. We further show that generative outputs are less reliable for clinical tasks compared to internal representations. To leverage information spread across multiple layers, we propose a Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE)-based dynamic layer aggregation framework, which surpasses best-layer selection in multiple experiments, suggesting that PD-relevant acoustic cues are distributed across transformer layers rather than concentrated in one.

</details>


### [66] [Grounded in Sound: Reinforcement Learning with a Frozen Acoustic Judge to Curb ASR Insertion Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14455)
*Tingzhen Xiong,Rilin Chen,Weiwei Li,Wentao Zhang,Qicong Xie*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出声学保真度奖励：在GRPO训练中引入冻结的wav2vec2-CTC字符级声学评判器，抑制ASR后训练中的插入错误，在AMI会议语音上插入错误降低22-28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习用于ASR后训练时，奖励几乎只存在于文本空间：仅比较假设与参考文本，从不检验假设是否有音频支持。在高度规整的语音上，这使模型可以依赖强语言先验"猜词"而非真正"听音"；当声学条件退化时，该捷径失控，产生流畅但无音频依据的词，即插入错误。

Method: 设计一种声学保真度奖励：在GRPO奖励中加入一个单独预训练、永久冻结、非自回归的字符级wav2vec2-CTC声学评判器，仅在训练时使用，推理时移除，推理时单一模型执行贪心解码。在LibriSpeech上训练，并在含真实AMI会议语音的六级难度梯度（33,282条话语-条件实例）上评估。

Result: 相比调度匹配的WER-GRPO基线，近讲AMI-IHM插入错误降低28.3%，远场AMI-SDM降低22.3%，AMI-SDM的WER从35.89%降至34.71%，其余五级无显著WER差异；插入错误降低在会议级聚类bootstrap下依然成立。四项预设分析支持内容条件化的插入校准：在保留能量与语音活动的不可懂音频上输出坍缩85-90%；所评估的32-best CTC重打分配置无法复现该增益，而RL将其内化到单次贪心解码；仅策略置信度在四个评估设置中插入-AURC均更低。

Conclusion: 本文定位为机制性论文，并在一个具体实例上验证：7B语音大模型搭配0.3B CTC评判器。表明将声学接地信号引入RL奖励可有效校准插入行为，且该能力可被内化到推理时的单模型贪心解码中。

Abstract: When reinforcement learning (RL) is used for post-training automatic speech recognition (ASR), the reward almost always lives in the text space: it compares a hypothesis with the reference and never checks whether the hypothesis is supported by the audio. On highly regular speech this licenses a shortcut - guessing from a strong language prior rather than listening. Once the acoustics degrade, the shortcut runs unchecked and emits fluent but ungrounded words, i.e., insertion errors. We propose an acoustic-fidelity reward: a GRPO reward augmented with a separately pretrained, permanently frozen, non-autoregressive character-level wav2vec2-CTC acoustic judge, used strictly at training and absent at inference, where a single model decodes greedily. Trained on LibriSpeech and evaluated across a six-tier difficulty gradient including real AMI meeting speech (33,282 utterance-condition instances), the method reduces insertion errors by 28.3% on close-talking AMI-IHM and 22.3% on far-field AMI-SDM, while lowering WER on AMI-SDM from 35.89% to 34.71% and showing no detectable WER difference on the other five tiers, against a schedule-matched WER-GRPO baseline. The insertion reduction holds under a meeting-level clustered bootstrap. Four prespecified analyses support content-conditioned insertion calibration: output collapses 85-90% on unintelligible audio that preserves energy and voice activity; the gain is not recovered by the evaluated 32-best CTC rescoring configuration, yet RL internalizes it into a single greedy decoding run; and policy-only confidence yields lower insertion-AURC in all four evaluated settings. We frame this as a mechanism paper, demonstrated in one instantiation: a 7B speech LLM with a 0.3B CTC judge.

</details>


### [67] [Bridging the Modality Gap in Long-Form Clinical Audio: A Comparative Study of Lightweight and Heavyweight End-to-End SOAP Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14467)
*Ziyu Zhang,Mingchen Shao,Wenjie Tian,Tianlun Zuo,Longhao Li,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: ASLP团队提出面向BeTraC 2026挑战赛的全端到端多模态系统，直接从长时医患对话音频生成结构化SOAP病历，绕过中间转录文本；通过141万样本多任务语料与领域预训练、监督微调、奖励优化三阶段流程，在3B与30B两种规模下均超越级联ASR+LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时医患对话的临床文档自动生成对现代音频-语言模型仍是难题：级联ASR系统表现尚可，但端到端模型在长音频上易出现信息丢失与幻觉。作者希望验证绕过中间转录、直接进行多模态优化的可行性。

Method: 构建141万样本的多任务语料；采用全E2E多模态架构，直接从音频生成结构化SOAP笔记；训练采用多阶段流水线：领域预训练、监督微调、奖励优化；在轻量（3B）与重量（30B）两种参数约束下评估架构。

Result: 各训练阶段逐步提升性能；参数扩展至30B显著改善概念抽取与摘要质量；E2E系统在两种规模下均持续优于代表性级联ASR+LLM基线。

Conclusion: 直接多模态优化对临床文档生成有效，全端到端方案优于级联范式；模型规模扩展与多阶段训练是提升性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Automating clinical documentation from long-form doctor-patient conversations remains challenging for modern audio-language models. While cascaded ASR systems perform well, end-to-end (E2E) models often struggle with information loss and hallucinations on extended audio. For the BeTraC 2026 challenge, the ASLP team presents a fully E2E multimodal system that generates structured SOAP notes directly from audio, bypassing intermediate transcripts. We constructed a 1.41-million-sample multi-task corpus and applied a multi-stage pipeline: domain pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and reward optimization. Evaluating the architecture under both Lightweight (3B) and Heavyweight (30B) constraints reveals that each training stage progressively enhances performance. Furthermore, scaling to 30B parameters substantially boosts concept extraction and summarization quality. Ultimately, our E2E systems consistently outperform representative cascaded ASR+LLM baselines, proving the efficacy of direct multimodal optimization for clinical documentation.

</details>


### [68] [CCMAN: Cognitive Instability-Aware Cross-Modal Attention Network for Interpretable Temporal Biomarkers of Verbal Fluency Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14764)
*Madhurananda Pahar,Caitlin Illingworth,Dorota Braun,Daniel Blackburn,Heidi Christensen*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出CCMAN跨模态注意力网络，利用迁移学习与时间建模从言语流畅性任务中提取可解释的言语动态不稳定生物标志物，实现认知衰退的早期检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临床认知评估难以规模化且具侵入性；言语流畅性任务信息量大，但现有自动化方法多在整段录音上聚合特征，忽略了言语的时间动态信息。

Method: CCMAN为迁移学习框架：先在多个记忆探测任务上学习任务无关的认知言语表征，再在约一分钟的语义与音位言语流畅性任务上微调；通过双向交叉注意力融合语义、声学与语言学信息，采用门控多模态融合与基于Transformer的时间建模，导出可解释的认知衰退生物标志物。

Result: 在843名参与者（498健康对照、245轻度认知障碍、100痴呆）共165.44小时语音上实验：二分类与多分类语义流畅性Macro-F1为0.81和0.59，音位流畅性为0.77和0.53，优于静态与时间基线。统计显示语义漂移方差与停顿方差在MCI和痴呆中显著升高，而语义漂移均值无显著差异；停顿时长随任务推进逐渐增加，痴呆斜率最陡。在独立PROCESS-2基准上Macro-F1较基线最高提升9%。

Conclusion: 言语时间不稳定性能作为动态言语生物标志物，支持鲁棒、可解释且具泛化性的认知衰退早期检测。

Abstract: Early detection of cognitive decline from speech offers a scalable and non-invasive alternative to conventional clinical assessment. Verbal fluency tasks are particularly informative, but most automated approaches aggregate features across an entire recording, overlooking temporal speech dynamics. We propose the Cognitive Instability-Aware Cross-Modal Attention Network (CCMAN), a transfer learning framework that learns task-agnostic cognitive speech representations from multiple memory-probing tasks before fine-tuning on a minute-long semantic and phonemic verbal fluency task. CCMAN integrates semantic, acoustic, and linguistic information through bidirectional cross-attention, gated multimodal fusion, and transformer-based temporal modelling to derive interpretable biomarkers of cognitive decline. Experiments were conducted on 165.44 hours of speech from 843 participants (498 healthy controls, 245 with mild cognitive impairment, and 100 with dementia). CCMAN achieved Macro-F1 scores of 0.81 and 0.59 for binary and multiclass semantic fluency classification, and 0.77 and 0.53 for phonemic fluency, consistently outperforming strong static and temporal baselines. Statistical analyses showed that semantic drift variance and pause variance, but not mean semantic drift, were significantly elevated in both MCI and dementia relative to healthy controls, while pause duration increased progressively over the task with the steepest slope in dementia, supporting global and progressive temporal speech instability as interpretable biomarkers. Evaluation on the independent PROCESS-2 benchmark further demonstrated the generalisability of the proposed framework, improving the baseline Macro-F1 by up to 9%. These findings support temporal speech instability as a dynamic speech biomarker for robust, interpretable, and generalisable early detection of cognitive decline.

</details>


### [69] [POLARIS: Training-Free Audio Fingerprinting with Saliency-Based Landmarks and Delaunay Grouping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14820)
*Jiheng Li*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出免训练音频指纹系统POLARIS，基于局部归一化显著场选关键点并用Delaunay三角剖分分组为稀疏指纹，查询时按需扩展两跳邻域，在两个基准上取得最佳免训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频指纹需在查询失真下保持鲁棒性，同时避免因扩充参考索引而增大存储与检索开销；训练-free方法在鲁棒性与效率间难以兼顾。

Method: 从局部归一化显著场中选取关键点，用Delaunay三角剖分将关键点分组为稀疏指纹；仅在查询阶段加入两跳Delaunay邻域指纹，参考索引不变；采用自适应配置，仅在原始指纹无法产生高置信匹配时才触发扩展。

Result: 在PEX Hard Medium合成失真基准（排除音高与速度偏移查询）及真实重录音乐新基准上，POLARIS均取得所评估免训练方法中的最佳性能；真实录音上自适应配置还优于神经NMFP基线，查询时间相当且逻辑参考载荷更小。

Conclusion: 查询端按需邻域扩展与自适应策略可在不增大参考索引的前提下提升鲁棒性，使免训练指纹系统在真实重录场景中达到甚至超越神经基线，代码、数据集与复现说明已开源。

Abstract: This work presents POLARIS, a training-free audio fingerprinting system that selects landmarks from a locally normalized saliency field and groups them into sparse fingerprints using Delaunay triangulation. To deal with query distortion, POLARIS adds fingerprints from two-hop Delaunay neighborhoods only at query time, without enlarging the reference index. An adaptive configuration applies this expansion only when the original fingerprints do not produce a confident match. We evaluate POLARIS on synthetic distortions from the public PEX Hard Medium benchmark, excluding queries with pitch or tempo shifts, and on a new benchmark of real re-recorded music. POLARIS achieves the best performance among the evaluated training-free methods on both benchmarks. On the real recordings, its adaptive configuration also outperforms the neural NMFP baseline with a comparable measured query time and a smaller logical reference payload. Code, dataset, and instructions for reproducing all experiments are available at https://github.com/JihengLi/POLARIS.git.

</details>


### [70] [Tracing the Origins: Legacy Codec Identification in Neural Audio Transcoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14916)
*Wonje Heo,Shinee Youn,Yooshin Kim,Chuck Chae,Donghoon Shin*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该研究定义并填补了RVQ神经音频编解码器带来的取证空白，提出基于Transformer的框架，利用RVQ序列的层次与时序依赖解耦传统压缩与神经转码的叠加伪影，实现97%+的编解码器识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RVQ神经音频编解码器以离散token表示实现极低码率的高保真音频传输，但这种非线性神经转码破坏了传统取证，掩盖了遗留压缩的底层痕迹，形成取证空白。

Method: 提出Transformer-based框架，建模RVQ序列的层间因果关系与动态取证显著性，有效解耦遗留到神经转码过程中叠加的伪影。

Result: 在32-128 kbps范围内实现97%+的编解码器识别准确率，并具备稳健的联合识别性能。

Conclusion: 传统编解码器痕迹在神经转码后仍然持续存在，证明了神经编解码器感知音频取证的可行性与必要性。

Abstract: Residual Vector Quantization (RVQ)-based neural audio codecs (NACs) enable high-fidelity audio distribution at unprecedentedly low bitrates through discrete token-based representations. However, this shift disrupts traditional forensics, as non-linear neural transcoding obscures the underlying traces of legacy compression. This study defines the forensic gap and proposes a Transformer-based framework designed to leverage the hierarchical and temporal dependencies inherent in RVQ sequences. By modeling inter-layer causal relationships and dynamic forensic significance, our model effectively disentangles superimposed artifacts from legacy-to-neural transcoding. Experimental results achieve 97%+ accuracy for codec identification and robust joint identification performance across 32-128 kbps. These results demonstrate that traditional codec traces persist even after neural transcoding, supporting the feasibility and necessity of neural-codec-aware audio forensics.

</details>


### [71] [CAL-MOS: Bridging Layers with Adapters for Robust MOS Prediction Across Speech Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14956)
*Alef Iury Siqueira Ferreira,Pedro Lustosa Rege Botelho,Fernanda Silva,Daniel Casanova,Rafael Faustino,Frederico Oliveira,Arlindo Galvão Filho,Anderson da Silva Soares*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文系统评测了十种语音基础模型在四个MOS数据集上的语音质量评估表现，比较全量微调、冻结编码器的末层探针与朴素跨层加权聚合三种方案，并提出逐层校准的聚合变体以提升多层融合稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非侵入式SQA预测器日益依赖语音基础模型（SFM），但SFM暴露多层表示，哪些深度的信息对MOS预测最有效、以及如何跨骨干与数据集可靠地组合多层信息尚不明确。

Method: 在四个MOS数据集上基准测试十个SFM，采用三种范式：全量微调、冻结编码器的末层探针、朴素跨层加权聚合；并评估一种逐层校准聚合变体，即在池化前为每层引入适配器。

Result: 最优层强烈依赖骨干模型与数据集；朴素加权融合在不同设置下不稳定；逐层校准聚合提升了多层融合的鲁棒性，在保持骨干冻结的情况下缩小了与全量微调的差距。

Conclusion: 多层表示的有效性具有骨干与数据依赖性，朴素融合不可靠；逐层校准的聚合方式为冻结骨干下的多层SQA提供了更稳健的方案。

Abstract: Speech Quality Assessment (SQA) is essential for modern speech technologies, and recent non-intrusive SQA predictors increasingly rely on Speech Foundation Models (SFMs). However, because SFMs expose representations from many layers, it remains unclear which depths are most informative for MOS prediction and how multi-layer information should be combined reliably across backbones and datasets. We benchmark ten SFMs on four MOS datasets under three regimes: full fine-tuning, last-layer probing with a frozen encoder, and naive cross-layer weighted aggregation. We find that the best layer is strongly backbone- and dataset-dependent, and that naive weighted fusion can be unstable across settings. We further evaluate a layer-calibrated aggregation variant that applies per-layer adapters before pooling, which improves the robustness of multi-layer fusion and narrows the gap to full fine-tuning while keeping the backbone frozen.

</details>


### [72] [Rethinking Procedural Audio Pre-training: Source Scaling and Objective Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15067)
*Jiajun Peng,Fengrui Liu,Xinyu Liu,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文重新审视程序化音频的表征学习设计原则，将数据规模分解为公式类覆盖度C与类内渲染多样性I，并发现二者收益不同且依赖学习形式与下游任务；同时程序化音频偏好低掩码率（10%–25%），与自然音频（AudioSet-28K的50%–75%）不同，据此提出源感知的程序化预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化音频已成为可迁移音频表征学习的可行来源，但其设计原则尚不明确，尤其缺乏对"如何扩展程序化数据源"以及"在自然音频上发展的训练选择能否原样迁移到程序化数据"这两个问题的系统探讨。

Method: 使用受控的程序化数据源，将规模分解为公式类覆盖度（formula-class coverage C）与类内渲染多样性（within-class rendering diversity I）；在FDSL与AudioMAE上进行实验；并开展匹配的AudioMAE研究比较不同掩码比例（10%–25% vs 50%–75%）的表现，辅以共享码本分析考察patch多样性与时间可预测性。

Result: 公式类覆盖度与类内渲染多样性两种规模形式带来不同收益，且依赖于学习形式与下游任务；AudioMAE中程序化音频偏好低掩码率（10%–25%），而AudioSet-28K偏好50%–75%；共享码本分析显示程序化音频具有更低的patch多样性和更强的时间可预测性。

Conclusion: 程序化预训练应具备源感知特性，将数据源扩展与学习配置（如掩码率）联合考虑，而非直接沿用自然音频的训练选择。代码已开源。

Abstract: Procedural audio has emerged as a viable source for transferable audio representation learning, but its design principles remain unclear.We revisit two questions: how a procedural source should be scaled, and whether training choices developed on natural audio should transfer unchanged to procedural data.Using a controlled source, we separate scale into formula-class coverage C and within-class rendering diversity I.Experiments with FDSL and AudioMAE show that these two forms of scale provide different benefits and depend on the learning formulation and downstream task. A matched AudioMAE study further shows that procedural audio favors low mask ratios (10%--25%), whereas AudioSet-28K favors 50%--75%. Shared-codebook analysis reveals lower patch diversity and stronger temporal predictability in procedural audio. These results motivate source-aware procedural pre-training, where source scaling and learning configuration are considered jointly.Code is available at https://github.com/Cross-Innovation-Lab/Formula-Bank.

</details>


### [73] [Cross-Lingual F5-TTS 2: A Simplified Framework for Language-Agnostic Voice Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15184)
*Qingyu Liu,Rixi Xu,Yushen Chen,Zhikang Niu,Haitao Li,Pengcheng Zhu,Bowen Zhang,Jian Zhao,Yunting Yang,Qinyuan Cheng,Xipeng Qiu,Berrak Sisman,Kai Yu,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 Cross-Lingual F5-TTS 2，去除强制对齐依赖，通过预训练模型自构同说话人提示-目标对进行微调，并加入静音感知增强使音节级语速预测器更稳健，实现无需提示文本的跨语言零样本语音克隆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本 TTS 多需提示音频的转写文本，限制了跨语言（尤其未见语言）克隆；现有 Cross-Lingual F5-TTS 虽去除该依赖，但依赖强制对齐（边界敏感、成本随语种增加），且语速预测器在提示音首尾含静音时估计不可靠。

Method: 不用强制对齐切分真实语音，而是借助预训练 F5-TTS 构造同说话人的提示-目标对，并在这些构造对上微调同一模型；通过静音感知增强提升音节级语速预测器对首尾静音的鲁棒性。

Result: 在保持可懂度的同时，说话人相似度高于 F5-TTS 与 Cross-Lingual F5-TTS。

Conclusion: 简化的无强制对齐框架支持仅需短微调即可适配，实现无需转写文本的跨语言语音克隆，相关资源已公开。

Abstract: Zero-shot text-to-speech (TTS) can clone a speaker's voice from a short audio prompt, yet most TTS systems still require the audio prompt transcript during inference. This dependency prevents cross-lingual voice cloning when the audio prompt transcript is unavailable, particularly for unseen languages. Cross-Lingual F5-TTS removes this dependency and enables transcript-free cross-lingual voice cloning, but it prepares its training data with forced alignment. Forced alignment is sensitive to boundary errors, and its cost grows as more languages are covered. Its speaking rate predictor is also unreliable at estimating duration when the audio prompt begins or ends with silence. In this paper, we present Cross-Lingual F5-TTS 2, a simplified framework for transcript-free cross-lingual voice cloning without forced alignment. Instead of using forced alignment to segment real utterances, we build same-speaker prompt and target pairs using a pretrained F5-TTS model and fine-tune the same model on these constructed pairs. This simplifies data preparation and preserves the acoustic modeling capability of the pretrained model, enabling adaptation with only a short fine-tuning stage. We further make the syllable-level speaking rate predictor robust to leading and trailing silence through silence-aware augmentation. Experiments show that Cross-Lingual F5-TTS 2 reaches higher speaker similarity than F5-TTS and Cross-Lingual F5-TTS while maintaining intelligibility. All related resources are publicly available.

</details>


### [74] [Augmenting Large Audio-Language Models with Frame-Level Grounding for Fine-Grained Temporal Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15215)
*Yanfeng Shi,Yan Song,Junhui Li,Tinggan Huang,Wu Guo,Haoyu Song,Ian McLoughlin*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文针对大型音频-语言模型在细粒度时间感知、尤其是精确事件定位上的不足，提出将LALM与专用帧级定位模型结合：冻结LALM负责编码事件查询与音频上下文，定位模型融合查询表示与细粒度音频特征进行帧级定位，在多个时间定位基准上取得稳健提升并可提供时间证据支持下游推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LALM虽在通用音频理解上进展显著，但在细粒度时间感知（尤其精确事件定位）上仍受限。已有方法主要通过对LALM后训练，以时间戳token的形式生成事件边界，但这种生成式范式缺乏时间戳预测与细粒度声学证据之间的显式对应，导致定位精度与可靠性不足。

Method: 在LALM之外引入一个专用的帧级定位（grounding）模型，同时利用LALM的语义建模能力表示事件查询。具体而言，冻结的LALM以音频为上下文编码事件查询；定位模型将得到的查询表示与细粒度音频特征相结合，在帧级别定位目标事件。

Result: 在多个不同的时间定位基准上开展大量实验，结果显示相较于现有方法取得显著且一致的性能提升。进一步评估表明，该定位模型能够提供时间证据以支持下游推理。

Conclusion: 通过将事件查询的语义表示与帧级声学特征显式对齐，所提方法弥补了生成式时间戳范式缺乏声学证据对应的缺陷，提升了音频事件时间定位的精度与可靠性，并可作为时间证据服务于下游推理任务。

Abstract: Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) have substantially advanced general audio understanding, yet they remain limited in fine-grained temporal perception, particularly in precise event localization. Existing approaches primarily post-train LALMs to predict event boundaries as timestamp tokens. However, this generative formulation lacks explicit correspondence between the timestamp predictions and fine-grained acoustic evidence, limiting the precision and reliability of temporal localization. To address this issue, we augment the LALM with a dedicated frame-level grounding model while leveraging its semantic modeling capability to represent the event query. Specifically, the frozen LALM encodes the event query with audio as context, and the grounding model combines these query representations with fine-grained audio features to localize the target event at the frame level. Extensive experiments across diverse temporal grounding benchmarks demonstrate strong and consistent improvements over existing methods. Further evaluation shows that the grounding model can provide temporal evidence to support downstream reasoning.

</details>


### [75] [MAST: Label-Efficient, Robust, and Generalizable Sound Detection for Biodiversity Monitoring via Masked Audio Pretraining and Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15221)
*Tianyi Xu,Daniel Pimentel-Alarcón,Zuzana Buřivalová,Claudia Solís-Lemus*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 MAST：结合掩码音频预训练与轻量检测器的标签高效声事件检测框架，通过框级对比损失和两阶段自训练伪标签，在热带雨林与地中海鸟类两个域上提升了跨站点时频定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动声学监测可大尺度测量生物多样性，但动物发声的时频标注昂贵、依赖站点且难以规模化；同时需应对时序与跨站点分布偏移，因此需要标签高效的鲁棒声检测方法。

Method: 在两阶段流程中：先以掩码重建在无标注录音上预训练 ViT 编码器并迁移至检测骨干；在梅尔频谱上用轻量检测器，并引入框级对比损失拉近匹配事件区域、推离噪声负样本；随后采用两阶段伪标签课程，利用大规模无标注音频进行迭代自训练。

Result: 在印尼热带雨林与西班牙地中海鸟类两个生态差异显著的域上，掩码预训练与对比学习在时序及跨站点偏移下稳定提升时频检测；自训练进一步提升分布外性能。雨林域跨站点偏移下，MAST 加自训练较最强基线提升 +0.22 mAP 与 +0.24 F1；鸟类域跨站点偏移下提升 +0.12 mAP 与 +0.10 F1。

Conclusion: MAST 可将自监督音频表征从片段级任务扩展至跨多样生物声学场景的鲁棒框级定位，为标签受限下的生物多样性监测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Passive acoustic monitoring can measure biodiversity at larger scales, but time--frequency annotation of animal vocalizations is expensive, site-specific, and difficult to sustain at scale. We present a label-efficient sound detection framework that combines masked audio pretraining with a lightweight detector on mel spectrograms, then further improves robustness through iterative self-training on unlabeled audio. We first pretrain a ViT-based encoder on unlabeled recordings via masked reconstruction and transfer the encoder to a detection backbone. To better separate animal sounds from confounding background, we add a box-level contrastive loss that pulls matched event regions together while pushing noisy negatives apart. We then apply a two-stage pseudo-labeling curriculum to exploit large unlabeled pools without additional annotation. We evaluate the performance on two ecologically distinct domains: tropical rainforest soundscapes (Indonesia) and bird vocalizations in Mediterranean habitats (Spain). On both domains, masked audio pretraining and contrastive learning consistently improve time--frequency detection under temporal and cross-site distribution shift, and self-training yields further gains in out-of-distribution performance. On the rainforest domain, MAST with self-training achieves +0.22 mAP and +0.24 F1 over the strongest baseline under cross-site shift. On the bird domain, self-training achieves +0.12 mAP and +0.10 F1 over the strongest baseline under cross-site shift. Overall, our results show that MAST can effectively extend self-supervised audio representations from clip-level tasks to robust box-level localization across diverse bioacoustic settings, providing a practical path for biodiversity monitoring with limited labels.

</details>


### [76] [Generating the Unheard: Phylogeny-Guided Latent Generation for Ancestral Sound Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15240)
*Tianyi Xu,Shrinaath Narasimhan,Evan Gorstein,Santiago Perea,Yunyi Shen,Claudia Solís-Lemus*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出首个祖先鸟类发声生成框架：将鸟鸣编码至VAE潜空间，学习与系统发育距离对齐的低维性状投影，在该性状空间进行祖先推断，并通过锚定逆提升恢复可解码潜变量，从而为每个内部节点输出全新的波形，实现真正的祖先音频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有祖先状态重建方法只能推断低维性状（如形态特征），无法生成音频等丰富感知信号。主要原因在于推断得到的表征要么维度过低无法解码，要么位于非生成性特征空间，因此尚无方法能生成祖先音频。

Method: 构建端到端流水线：1）将鸟类录音编码进VAE潜空间；2）学习与系统发育距离对齐的低维性状投影；3）在该性状空间执行祖先推断；4）通过锚定逆提升（anchored inverse lift）把推断性状恢复为可解码潜变量；5）为每个祖先节点合成新波形。整个流程始终保持在可解码潜空间内。

Result: 在系统发育上相距较远的两个鸟类别群（21种霸鹟科Tyrannidae与19种山雀科Paridae）上实验表明，该方法是唯一在两个数据集上同时实现真实生成、系统发育一致性与自然音质的方案。

Conclusion: 该框架首次实现了合理的祖先发声生成，每个内部节点都可获得真正的新音频，而非检索式替代方案所能提供的样本，为祖先感知信号重建开辟了新路径。

Abstract: What did an ancestral bird species sound like? Existing ancestral state reconstruction methods can infer low-dimensional traits such as morphological characters at internal nodes of a phylogenetic tree, but no one has tried to produce rich perceptual signals such as audio. Some of the challenges include inferred representations that are either too low-dimensional to decode or lie in non-generative feature spaces, so no method to date can produce ancestral audio. We introduce the first framework that generates plausible ancestral vocalizations. Our pipeline encodes bird recordings into a VAE latent space, learns a low-dimensional trait projection aligned with phylogenetic distances, performs ancestral inference in this trait space, and recovers decodable latents through an anchored inverse lift before emitting novel waveforms for each ancestral node. Because the entire pipeline stays within a decodable latent space, every internal node receives a genuinely new audio output representing plausible intermediate ancestral sounds unavailable to retrieval-based alternatives. Experiments on two phylogenetically distant bird clades, 21-species Tyrannidae and 19-species Paridae, show that our method is the only approach that simultaneously achieves genuine generation, phylogenetic consistency, and naturalistic audio quality across both datasets.

</details>


### [77] [Listening for Airway Stenosis: A Foundation Model-Based Method for Rapid and Accessible Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15453)
*Jean Groeninger,Zihao Zhao,Juliana de Castilhos,Sven Nebelung,Daniel Truhn*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本研究探索利用声学基础模型（AFMs）对患者语音录音进行气道狭窄的快速、低负担检测，在748人队列上取得AUROC 0.952、准确率0.924的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气道狭窄可导致严重呼吸系统并发症，但现有检测依赖专科检查与医学影像，成本高、可及性受限，亟需快速、便捷的筛查手段。

Method: 基于Bridge2AI-Voice数据集中的748名受试者（134例气道狭窄、614例对照），系统评估声学基础模型（AFMs）能否提取与气道狭窄相关语音模式对应的声学表征，并进行五折交叉验证与录音类型（连续语音、持续发声、呼吸等）的贡献分析。

Result: 最佳模型达到AUROC 0.952、准确率0.924（五折交叉验证均值）；分析表明模型主要依赖连续语音（connected-speech）录音，而非持续发声、呼吸等孤立声学任务。

Conclusion: 基于语音的声学AI可实现对气道狭窄的快速、低负担、广泛可及的筛查，有望补充现有诊断流程，并显示AFMs从通用语音应用向临床诊断任务迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Airway stenosis can cause severe respiratory complications, yet its detection often relies on specialized examinations and medical imaging. This study explores the potential of acoustic AI for rapid and accessible airway stenosis detection using readily acquired patient voice recordings. We systematically investigate whether acoustic foundation models (AFMs) can extract acoustic representations associated with airway stenosis-related speech patterns. Experiments are conducted on a cohort of 748 participants from the Bridge2AI-Voice dataset, 134 with airway stenosis and 614 without. The best-performing model achieves an AUROC of 0.952 and an accuracy of 0.924 (means over five-fold cross-validation), highlighting the potential of AFMs to transfer beyond general-purpose speech applications to clinical diagnostic tasks. Further analysis reveals that the model primarily relies on connected-speech recordings rather than isolated acoustic tasks, such as sustained phonation and breathing. Overall, these results suggest that voice-based acoustic AI could complement existing diagnostic workflows by enabling rapid, low-burden, and widely accessible screening for airway stenosis.

</details>


### [78] [Building a Dataset for Music Sample Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15465)
*R. Oguz Araz,Xavier Lizarraga,Xavier Serra,Dmitry Bogdanov*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文从音乐数据库中挖掘采样标注，构建了近三个数量级于现有基准的样本识别数据集，并提出基于连通分量的防泄漏划分流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 样本识别（SI）任务研究不足，且缺乏大规模公开可用数据。

Method: 挖掘采样标注并划分训练/验证/测试集；从标注构建图并按连通分量划分以避免同一曲目跨集合；因单个超大分量含半数标注，对其裁剪以实现泄漏感知且均衡的划分。

Result: 数据集规模约为现有SI基准的近三个数量级，训练、验证、测试集分别为114k、6k、10k曲目；发现朴素划分会导致泄漏并予以解决。

Conclusion: 数据集仅限非商业科研用途共享，数据分析与划分代码已公开，期望推动SI研究。

Abstract: Sample identification (SI) is the task of matching an element of a musical work to its musically transformed versions used to create new works. The task has received little attention and lacks large-scale publicly available data. In this work, we mine sampling annotations from a music database and split them for training and evaluation. The resulting dataset is nearly three orders of magnitude larger than the existing SI benchmarks, with training, validation, and test sets of 114 k, 6 k, and 10 k tracks. We find that naively splitting the annotations places the same tracks in different sets. To avoid this, we construct a graph from the annotations and split it over connected components. We further find that a single mega-component contains half of the annotations, making component-wise splitting incompatible with balanced splits; we trim it, yielding a leakage-aware pipeline. We share the dataset for non-commercial scientific research purposes only and make the data-analysis and splitting code publicly available. We hope that our work fosters research on SI.

</details>


### [79] [Graph Attention Design Choices Matter: A Controlled Study of LoRA-Adapted Audio Anti-Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15650)
*Haoyu Wang,Jing Yang,Chenyu Liu,Yushan Du,Yifan Liao,Ningning Pan,Gongping Huang,Yu Zhao,Gang Li,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 在统一实验设定下对图注意力层的三个设计维度进行受控研究，发现各维度贡献非可加：LearnT 分支带来最佳平均 EER（相对提升 16.1%），多温度路由分支单独无效但能降低跨种子标准差，而两个单独有效的分支组合后反而使性能相对退化 25.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频反欺骗系统常同时结合自监督学习、参数高效微调与图注意力后端，但性能提升往往与骨干、微调策略、训练协议的同步变化相纠缠，难以分离图注意力设计本身的独立贡献。因此需要一个统一设定下的系统受控研究。

Method: 将图注意力层分解为三个可独立测试的设计维度：打分对称性、温度可学习性、路由粒度，分别实例化为基于 concat 的打分分支、带可学习温度的 LearnT 分支、多温度路由分支；每个维度实现为可独立门控的残差分支，从而在同一实验设定下评估各变体及其组合。实验使用五个评估集与五个随机种子。

Result: LearnT 分支取得最佳平均 EER，相对基线提升 16.1%；多温度路由分支单独不改善平均性能，但与 concat 打分分支组合时显著降低跨种子标准差；两个单独有效的分支组合使用时性能反而退化，相对基线恶化 25.6%，表明设计维度间存在强非可加交互。

Conclusion: 在参数受限的微调条件下，图注意力层的改进更多取决于容量分配与分支交互，而非单纯增加可学习参数。

Abstract: Audio anti-spoofing systems increasingly combine self-supervised learning, parameter-efficient fine-tuning, and graph-attention-based backends. However, performance gains in such systems are often entangled with concurrent changes in the backbone, fine-tuning strategy, and training protocol, making the independent contribution of graph attention design difficult to isolate. To address this issue, we conduct a systematic controlled study of the graph attention layer under a unified experimental setting. We decompose the layer into three independently testable design dimensions: scoring symmetry, temperature learnability, and routing granularity. These are instantiated as a concat-based scoring branch, a LearnT branch with learnable temperature, and a multi-temperature routing branch, respectively. Each dimension is implemented as an independently gated residual branch, enabling the evaluation of both individual variants and their combinations under the same experimental setting. Experiments on five evaluation sets with five random seeds show that the LearnT branch achieves the best average equal error rate (EER), yielding a 16.1% relative improvement over the baseline. In contrast, the multi-temperature routing branch does not improve average performance on its own, but substantially reduces cross-seed standard deviation when combined with the concat-based scoring branch. Moreover, two individually effective branches degrade performance when used together, resulting in a 25.6% relative deterioration compared with the baseline. This finding reveals strong non-additive interactions among graph attention design dimensions. Overall, the results suggest that, under parameter-constrained fine-tuning, improvements in graph attention layers depend more on capacity allocation and branch interaction than on simply adding more learnable parameters.

</details>


### [80] [Sectional Structure and Emotional Dynamics in Chinese Pop Songs: An Empirical Analysis of Valence-Arousal Trajectories across 100 Songs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15675)
*Jingyi Lyu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本研究通过对100首中文流行歌曲的1046个手动标注段落与连续VA轨迹对齐，从段落类型、相邻转换、重复段落及全曲轨迹四个层面分析歌曲段落结构中的情感组织规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态音乐情感识别（MER）研究对歌曲段落结构的考虑有限，缺乏对段落层面情感变化规律的系统刻画。

Method: 将100首中文流行歌曲的1046个人工标注段落与连续Valence-Arousal（VA）轨迹对齐，从段落类型、相邻段落转换、重复段落和全曲轨迹四个角度进行统计分析。

Result: 段落间情感差异在Arousal维度上更明显：Verse为低唤醒基线，Pre-chorus呈渐进累积，Chorus为显著高唤醒到达，Interlude、Bridge、Outro分别呈过渡、分化和收束功能；全曲段落配置多样但高频局部转换相对集中；高唤醒位置多出现在歌曲后段，但并非固定于最后的Chorus。

Conclusion: 提出"局部循环—全局累积"的经验性情感组织框架：局部段落循环伴随情感回落，整体轨迹呈后段VA上升，且高唤醒位置趋于后移。

Abstract: Music Emotion Recognition (MER) aims to identify and represent emotional information in music through computational methods and is an important research area within Music Information Retrieval (MIR). To address the limited consideration of song sectional structure in existing dynamic MER research, this study examines 100 Chinese pop songs by aligning 1,046 manually annotated sections with continuous Valence-Arousal (VA) trajectories and analyzing them from the perspectives of section type, adjacent section transitions, repeated sections, and whole-song trajectories. The results show that emotional differences across sections are reflected more strongly in Arousal. Verse typically forms a relatively low-activation baseline, Pre-chorus exhibits a progressive buildup, and Chorus produces a more pronounced high-arousal arrival, while Interlude, Bridge, and Outro tend to show transitional, divergent, and closing functions, respectively. Although whole-song sectional configurations are diverse, high-frequency local transitions are relatively concentrated. High-arousal positions occur more often in the later part of a song, but are not fixed to the final Chorus. Based on these findings, this study summarizes the emotional organization of the selected pop songs as an empirical framework of "local cycles-global accumulation", in which local sectional cycles are accompanied by emotional pullbacks, while the overall trajectory exhibits a later-stage rise in VA and a tendency for high-Arousal positions to occur later in the song.

</details>

### [81] [Physical Counterexamples to the Wigner Shannon Entropy Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13312)
*Zixuan He*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了具有处处非负Wigner函数的物理量子态，其Shannon熵可低于真空值1+lnπ，给出有限能量反例、有限Fock支撑族、相干阈值、熵下降机制及最优熵亏损的精确标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Wigner函数非负性是否隐含Shannon熵不低于真空值1+lnπ这一猜想，并刻画在平均光子数约束下熵亏损的最优标度。

Method: 构造显式有限能量反例与秩二有限Fock支撑态族；对固定Fock能级n和相干比例λ做小t展开分析熵差；研究真空—单光子核与真空—三光子构造，确定Wigner非负所需最小热混合权重的对数渐近；在平均光子数≤E约束下对所有单模Wigner非负态优化熵亏损。

Result: 熵差满足h(W)-(1+lnπ)=(2n-2^nλ^2)t^2+O(t^4)，对每个n≥3在显式相干阈值以上给出有限支撑反例；完全相干真空—单光子核可得h(W)-(1+lnπ)=-4t^6/3+o(t^6)；最大熵亏损具有精确标度E^γ/[ln(1/E)]^β，其中γ≈0.7412033679，β≈0.5861054961。

Conclusion: Wigner非负性不保证熵不低于真空值；当E→0时真空熵被恢复，但最优熵亏损的衰减远慢于平均能量的任何线性修正。

Abstract: We construct physical quantum states with everywhere positive Wigner functions whose Shannon entropy lies below the vacuum value $1+\lnπ$. Besides an explicit finite-energy counterexample, we obtain rank-two finite-Fock-support families with analytic positivity and entropy bounds. For fixed Fock level $n$ and coherence fraction $0\leλ<1$, the entropy difference satisfies $h(W)-(1+\lnπ)=(2n-2^nλ^2)t^2+O_{n,λ}(t^4)$, yielding finite-support counterexamples for every $n\ge3$ above an explicit coherence threshold. We also show that the absence of a negative quadratic term does not preclude entropy descent: a fully coherent vacuum--one-photon core with a vanishing positive thermal repair gives $h(W)-(1+\lnπ)=-4t^6/3+o(t^6)$. For the vacuum--three-photon construction, we determine the logarithmic asymptotic of the minimum fixed-thermal mixing weight required for Wigner nonnegativity. Finally, optimizing over all one-mode Wigner-nonnegative states with mean photon number at most $E$, we prove that the maximal entropy deficit has the sharp scale $E^γ/[\ln(1/E)]^β$, where $γ\simeq0.7412033679$ and $β\simeq0.5861054961$. Thus the vacuum entropy is recovered as $E\to0$, but the optimal deficit decays much more slowly than any universal linear correction in the mean energy.

</details>


### [82] [Structured Quantum Kernels for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13360)
*Zhihui Wang,Sujit Roy,Ata Akbari,Manil Maskey,Rahul Ramachandran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出AeRot量子核，将l2归一化延迟窗口的振幅编码与分组单比特旋转层融合，使编码电路具备延迟窗口感知的归纳偏置。在Lorenz-63核岭回归上优于调优RBF与Matern-5/2，优势集中在鞍点逼近的不稳定区间，源于折叠分支歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子核虽理论特征空间指数级大，但表达力过强的电路会使Gram矩阵无信息量，带宽调优又使其退化为经典RBF，学界普遍认为量子核在经典数据上无增益。作者转而提出一个架构层面的问题：编码电路的结构能否携带调优经典核所缺乏的归纳偏置。

Method: 设计AeRot量子核：对l2归一化的延迟窗口做振幅编码，并叠加分组单比特旋转层，将连续时间块分配给各量子比特，使电路具备延迟窗口感知能力。在Lorenz-63系统上做核岭回归，对带宽进行交叉验证，运行100个随机种子，与调优RBF及Matern-5/2对比。另做窗口尾部的线性稳定性分析，并按不稳定性分位数统计胜率，辅以Gram矩阵结构与目标-核对齐诊断。

Result: AeRot优于调优RBF与Matern-5/2，优势在物理预测时域>=0.15 tu时显现，0.25 tu处平均R^2提升+0.137（5量子比特、窗口32）。稳定性分析将优势定位于鞍点逼近的不稳定区间：最不稳定十分位窗口胜率达83%，胜率随尾部不稳定性单调上升，并在局部稳定边界出现符号翻转。结构诊断显示Gram矩阵与调优RBF结构性不同，且在每个时域上目标-核对齐严格更高。

Conclusion: 该增益是架构性的：时间结构化编码在折叠吸引子上产生的有限样本归纳偏置效应，不附带计算分离性主张。这是首次将量子核优势机制性地定位到经典系统的特定动力学区间。困难本质为折叠分支歧义——轨迹接近鞍点时局部发散，欧氏核难以分辨其将进入哪一叶。

Abstract: Quantum kernels promise exponentially large feature spaces, but expressive circuits render their Gram matrices uninformative, bandwidth tuning collapses them toward classical RBF, and the practical consensus is that quantum kernels add nothing on classical data. We answer another productive question, an architectural one: whether the structure of an encoding circuit can carry an inductive bias that tuned classical kernels lack. We introduce AeRot, a quantum kernel fusing amplitude encoding of l2-normalized delay windows with a grouped single-qubit rotation layer that assigns contiguous temporal blocks to each qubit, making the circuit delay-window-aware. On Lorenz-63 kernel ridge regression with cross-validated bandwidths over 100 seeds, AeRot outperforms tuned RBF and Matern-5/2, the advantage emerging at physical horizons >= 0.15 tu and reaching +0.137 mean R^2 at 0.25 tu (5 qubits, window 32). Linear-stability analysis of the window tail localises the advantage to the unstable saddle-approach regime: AeRot wins 83% of windows in the most unstable decile, with the win rate rising monotonically with tail instability and a sign flip at the local stability boundary. The difficulty is fold-branch ambiguity: trajectories approaching the saddle are locally diverging, and Euclidean kernels struggle to resolve which lobe the trajectory will commit to. Structural diagnostics confirm Gram matrices structurally distinct from tuned RBF, with strictly higher target-kernel alignment at every horizon. The gain is architectural: a finite-sample inductive-bias effect of temporally structured encoding on a folded attractor, with no computational-separation claim attached. To our knowledge this is the first mechanistic localisation of quantum-kernel advantage to a specific dynamical regime of a classical system.

</details>


### [83] [High-rate multipartite quantum secret sharing with composable security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13367)
*Russell M. J. Brooks,Joseph Ho,Joseph Niblo,Janka Memmen,Anna Pappa,Jens Eisert,Nathan Walk,Alessandro Fedrizzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验演示了一种离散变量 (n,n)-阈值量子秘密共享协议，利用两个域工程化纠缠光子源生成4-qubit GHZ态，在总长20km的四臂星形光纤网络中分发，并基于有限密钥分析给出对一般攻击（含参与者攻击）的可组合安全性下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来量子通信网络需支持多用户间纠缠，量子秘密共享是其典型应用。然而传统基于GHZ态的协议易受参与者攻击，即非可信参与方试图在不合作的情况下获取秘密，因此需要具备可组合安全性且能抵御此类攻击的协议。

Method: 实验采用两个域工程化纠缠光子对源，产生4-qubit GHZ态，速率超过每秒5×10^3次四重符合事件；随后在总长约20km光纤构成的四臂星形网络中分发该态。基于测量的事件率和误码统计，结合优化基概率，推算24小时随机化运行下的可组合有限密钥下界。

Result: 实现的最大渐近秘密密钥率为750±10 bits/s；在假设源与器件统计保持稳定的条件下，24小时运行可得到8.7 Mbits的可组合有限密钥下界。

Conclusion: 该工作实验验证了具有有限密钥可组合安全性的(n,n)-阈值量子秘密共享协议，可在多用户光纤网络中抵御包括参与者攻击在内的一般攻击，为未来多用户量子通信网络提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Future quantum communication networks will conceivably support cryptographic tasks that require entanglement among more than two users. Quantum secret sharing is a prime example where entanglement provides a direct means to coordinate untrusted parties with security from eavesdropping in a multi-party setting. However, the canonical GHZ-based protocols can be vulnerable to participant attacks, in which untrusted parties try to learn the secret without collaborating. Here, we experimentally evaluate a discrete-variable $(n,n)$-threshold quantum secret-sharing protocol whose finite-key analysis provides composable security against general attacks, including participant attacks. Using two domain-engineered entangled photon pair sources, we generate 4-qubit GHZ states at rates above $5\times10^3$ fourfold events per second and a maximum asymptotic secret key rate of $750 \pm 10$ bits per second. We then distribute the state through a 4-arm star network comprising 20km of fibre in total. From the measured event rates and error statistics, we infer that a randomised 24-hour execution with the optimised basis probability would yield a composable finite-key lower bound of 8.7 Mbits, under the assumption that the measured source and device statistics remain stationary.

</details>


### [84] [Moment-Selective Quantum Designs in Aperiodic Temporal Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13371)
*Yang Peng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出利用层级结构非周期驱动实现"矩选择性"时间设计的通用机制：在有限对称群约束的特殊控制点，低阶矩已达Haar统计而特定高阶矩偏离非零；微小静态失谐使该偏差寿命呈指数增长，寿命指数与失谐平方成反比，并在量子比特系统中以Fibonacci与silver-mean构造验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机化/时间设计（t-design）通常追求整体逼近Haar分布，难以在保留特定高阶矩偏差的同时使低阶矩精确满足Haar统计。作者希望找到一个可控机制，实现"选择性"地保留某一高阶非Haar矩，并利用该偏差的长寿命特性服务于量子控制与随机化应用。

Method: 构造层级结构（hierarchical）的非周期驱动序列；在受有限对称群约束的特殊控制设置下，分析各阶矩与Haar统计的匹配情况。对偏离设置施加小的静态失谐，推导长寿命指数与失谐平方的反比标度。以量子比特为平台，采用精确的Fibonacci与silver-mean构造，分别结合四面体（tetrahedral）与二十面体（icosahedral）共振，实现首个保留的非Haar矩阶数为3和6。并对比随机控制涨落与相干失谐产生的不同截断行为，提出可有限时间测量的长寿命矩信号。

Result: 在所构造方案中，低阶矩精确复现Haar统计，而选定高阶矩保持非零偏差；微小静态失谐使该偏差寿命呈指数延长，寿命指数与失谐平方成反比。量子比特实现中，四面体与二十面体共振对应首个保留非Haar矩分别为3阶与6阶。随机控制涨落产生的截断机制与相干失谐 qualitatively 不同。作者给出无需指数长时间观测即可测得的有限时间特征信号。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种通用的矩选择性时间设计框架：通过层级非周期驱动与有限对称群约束，可精确控制哪些矩满足Haar统计、哪些高阶矩被保留；静态失谐为这些保留矩提供可调的指数长寿命，且有限时间可探测。该机制将时间设计与对称群共振、非周期序列（Fibonacci、silver-mean）及噪声截断机制联系起来，为量子随机化与精密控制提供新工具。

Abstract: We introduce a general mechanism for engineering moment-selective temporal designs with hierarchically structured aperiodic drives. At special control settings where the driven dynamics is constrained by a finite symmetry group, lower-order moments can already reproduce Haar statistics while a selected higher-order deviation remains nonzero. A small static detuning from such a setting generically produces an exponentially long lifetime whose exponent scales inversely with the square of the detuning. We illustrate this mechanism in qubit systems through exact Fibonacci and silver-mean constructions with tetrahedral and icosahedral resonances, for which the first retained non-Haar moments occur at orders $3$ and $6$, respectively. We further show that stochastic control fluctuations produce a qualitatively different cutoff from coherent detuning. Finally, we propose a finite-time signature of the long-lived moment that can be measured without requiring observations over exponentially long times.

</details>


### [85] [Handling Imperfections in Subcircuit Compilation for Linear Optical Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13379)
*Tobias Forster,Yannick Stade,Andreas Fyrillas,Jean Senellart,Lukas Burgholzer,Robert Wille*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出首个高效的子电路编译方法，通过处理硬件缺陷（如分束器反射率、相位噪声等）来优化符合率，在各类基准上相较于现有方法将符合率最多提升33.2%，并显著增强了对相位噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性光学量子计算（LOQC）中，线性光学电路规模与制造精度不断提升，但电路仍存在不完美之处，导致光子损耗或相位噪声，且这些缺陷在电路各处分布不均，即使微小缺陷也会严重影响计算精度与效率。对于仅需部分光学电路的子电路计算，存在选择执行位置的优化机会，但现有编译器将子电路计算散布至整个电路、连接相同的输入输出端口，未能充分利用这些机会。

Method: 作者提出首个高效的子电路编译方法。鉴于子电路规模小于整电路，可将其实现在局部化，并有意地路由光子以连接优质的输入输出端口。该方法需权衡大量选项，并考虑广泛的硬件参数：特别是非理想分束器的反射率会在路由过程中引入光子损耗，需与输入输出端口因非理想透射率造成的损耗进行权衡。现有编译器未考虑这些效应，而本文方法通过处理硬件缺陷来优化符合率（即成功测量所有目标光子的概率）。

Result: 评估结果表明，该方法在抗相位噪声鲁棒性上显著优于现有方法，并在所有基准测试中将符合率最高提升33.2%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模并优化硬件缺陷（分束器反射率、透射率与相位噪声）对符合率的影响，本文提出的子电路编译方法在鲁棒性与符合率两方面均取得显著提升，弥补了现有编译器忽略硬件不完美性的不足。

Abstract: Linear Optical Quantum Computing (LOQC) is emerging as a promising technology in large-scale quantum computing. Corresponding devices, including linear optical circuits, are rapidly growing in size and fabrication precision. However, even with improved quality, the circuits still exhibit imperfections leading to photon loss or phase noise that vary across the circuit. Already small deficiencies can have a vast impact on both accuracy and efficiency. For computations that do not require the entire linear optical circuit, so-called subcircuits, one can choose where to perform the computation, opening optimization opportunities that are addressed by subcircuit compilation. Current compilers spread the subcircuit's computation across the entire circuit, connecting the same input and output ports, thereby failing to fully exploit the optimization opportunities. However, given the subcircuit's reduced size compared to the entire circuit, one can keep its implementation localized and intentionally route photons to connect good input and output ports. This, however, is a highly non-trivial task, as numerous options must be weighed against one another, and the quality of the solution is influenced by a wide range of hardware parameters. In particular, the reflectivities of imperfect beam splitters can lead to photon losses during routing, which must be traded off against losses at the input and output ports due to imperfect transmission rates. Since current compilers do not account for these effects, we propose the first efficient subcircuit compilation method that optimizes for the coincidence rate, the probability of successfully measuring all desired photons, by handling hardware imperfections. Our evaluations demonstrate significant improvement over existing methods in robustness against phase noise and significantly increased coincidence rates across all benchmarks of up to plus 33.2%.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用目标态错误综合子（syndrome）的空间结构，构造一类幺正时序电路，在某些情形下用严格局域门系统性地抑制错误传播。方法应用于两类序：对称破缺序给出3D构造示例；拓扑序在4D环面码上展示。并讨论该稳定性对非Clifford门的需求，以及如何用测量-反馈回路稳定低维态。

Result: 提出了可在Pauli噪声下制备特定非平凡序的时序电路类；在3D对称破缺序与4D环面码拓扑序上实现了错误传播抑制；指出抗错稳定性必然需要非Clifford门；测量-反馈回路可用于稳定低维态。
