Here, we show that introducing a band gap in the environmental spectral density of both bosonic and fermionic baths provides a direct route to tuning non-Markovianity over a wide range. We demonstrate this effect by means of numerical simulations across bosonic and fermionic environments and discuss experimental tests of our predictions.

</details>


### [125] [Quantum synchronization: A tutorial guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14375)
*Sourabh Lahiri,Shamik Gupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于经典与量子动力系统中同步现象的教程综述，系统介绍从振幅-相位动力学到量子极限环振荡器同步的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步是相互作用非线性振子间相位与频率形成持续关系的集体现象；在量子系统中描述同步需要将经典极限环动力学与量子涨落、耗散及相位概念相结合，因此需要一套系统的分析框架。

Method: 从振幅-相位动力学出发，依次引入锁相、Adler方程、Arnold舌、Kuramoto模型等概念分析耦合振子及其稳定性；随后用Lindblad主方程和相空间方法建立量子极限环振荡器的描述，并给出两个及多个耦合振子的实例。

Result: 构建了涵盖经典与量子同步的统一分析框架，包括噪声、同步度量以及极端量子区域（含自旋系统同步）的讨论，并强调解析推导与识别同步态、判定稳定性、表征量子同步的实用方法。

Conclusion: 该综述是自包含指南，学生可借此学习基本概念与技术，研究人员也可直接获取处理新同步问题所需的解析工具与方法论洞见。

Abstract: Synchronization is a collective phenomenon in which interacting nonlinear oscillators develop a persistent relation between their phases and frequencies. In quantum systems, its description requires connecting the classical dynamics of limit-cycle oscillators with quantum fluctuations, dissipation, and the notion of phase. In this tutorial review, we offer a systematic framework for analyzing synchronization in classical and quantum dynamical systems. Starting from amplitude-phase dynamics, we introduce successively topics of relevance, such as phase locking, the Adler equation, Arnold tongues, and the Kuramoto model, and use these concepts to analyze coupled oscillators and their stability. We then formulate the corresponding description for quantum limit-cycle oscillators using Lindblad master equations and phase-space methods, with explicit examples of two and many coupled oscillators. We further discuss noise, synchronization measures, and the extreme quantum regime, including synchronization in spin systems. The emphasis throughout is on analytical derivations and a practical methodology for identifying synchronized states, determining their stability, and characterizing synchronization in quantum systems. The review is designed as a self-contained guide from which we hope that students can learn the essential concepts and techniques, while researchers can readily find the analytical tools and methodological insights needed to approach new synchronization problems.

</details>


### [126] [Certification cost of quantum models: measurement correlation, not parameter count](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14424)
*Pavel Sulimov,Claude Lehmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究训练后变分量子模型的Fisher几何度量所需置信预算，证明在坐标式参数偏移下以相对Frobenius误差ε认证经验Fisher矩阵需Θ(Bp²V/(ε²G))次电路执行，并指出该三次代价实为有限尺寸窗口，取决于读出光锥是否随寄存器增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 报告训练后变分量子模型的Fisher几何是常规操作，但给出能确立该几何的采样预算却并非如此。作者希望量化认证经验Fisher矩阵所需的电路执行代价，并厘清该代价的标度律本质。

Method: 在坐标式参数偏移协议下，将经验Fisher矩阵认证到相对Frobenius误差ε；证明均匀分配在该类中是最优的；通过624个矩阵乘积态单元的数值实验检验nV与nG随寄存器的标度关系；比较乘积族、brickwork纠缠器、分块纠缠器及固定/比例锥宽等多种电路族；并在ibm_marrakesh、ibm_fez、ibm_kingston硬件上测量硬件乘数与读出权重收益，同时评估基于廉价电路拟合的差异模型及其共形校准。

Result: 理论上给出Θ(Bp²V/(ε²G))的执行代价，同一常数复现了两个指数相差整整一个p次幂的电路族代价；指数在去除有限p前因子后，于624个矩阵乘积态单元中逐族保持到0.001。乘积族至256量子位给出1.966（95% CI 1.934–1.997）；brickwork纠缠器从十量子位以下的2.853降至六十四以上的1.715；分块纠缠器固定锥宽为1.984，比例锥宽为3.034。硬件乘数分别为2.07×（1.41–3.02）、2.38×与1.91×。优读权重将实测采样预算降低2.67×（1.33–4.00）。差异模型在校准网格内可迁移均值，但在六个更大尺寸上失效：名义90%区间仅覆盖36%，仅split conformal接近名义水平。

Conclusion: Fisher几何认证的三次代价是一个有限尺寸窗口，取决于读出光锥是否随寄存器增长；固定器件连通性固定了光锥，因此来自小规模仿真的三次预算会高估大规模机器上的成本。硬件乘数与成本最优读出权重显著影响实测预算，而基于廉价电路的差异模型外推不可靠，需借助split conformal等校准手段。

Abstract: Reporting the Fisher geometry of a trained variational quantum model is routine; quoting the shot budget that would establish it is not. Certifying an empirical Fisher matrix to relative Frobenius error $\varepsilon$ under coordinate-wise parameter shift costs $Θ(B p^{2} V/(\varepsilon^{2} G))$ circuit executions, where $V$ is the measured readout variance and $G$ the measured squared gradient norm, with uniform allocation optimal in that class. One constant reproduces the cost of two circuit families whose exponents differ by a full power of $p$. The exponent is an identity in how $nV$ and $nG$ scale with the register, holding family by family to $0.001$ across 624 matrix-product-state cells once the finite-$p$ prefactor is removed. The cubic cost is therefore a finite-size window, set by whether the readout light cone grows with the register. A product family to 256 qubits gives $1.966$ (95% CI $1.934$--$1.997$); a brickwork entangler falls from $2.853$ below ten qubits to $1.715$ beyond sixty-four; a blocked entangler gives $1.984$ at a fixed cone width against $3.034$ at a proportional one. Fixed device connectivity fixes the cone, so a cubic budget from a small simulation overestimates a large machine, on top of hardware multipliers $2.07\times$ ($1.41$--$3.02$), $2.38\times$ and $1.91\times$ on ibm_marrakesh, ibm_fez and ibm_kingston. Cost-optimal readout weights cut the measured shot budget by $2.67\times$ ($1.33$--$4.00$) on hardware, flat from four to twelve qubits. A discrepancy model fitted on cheap circuits transfers its mean inside the calibration grid and, at six larger sizes named before the data, does not: nominal 90% intervals cover 36%, and split conformal is the only rung that stays near nominal.

</details>


### [127] [Indistinguishability Theory for Identical Particles with Invariant Degrees of Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14428)
*Valery Shchesnovich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过引入"内部-可见"自由度划分和不可区分性函数，给出玻色/费米对称化公设的推广形式，刻画可见态的对称谱，并揭示不可区分性投影测度的层级上界及其实验可行测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对称化/反对称化公设仅适用于全同粒子内部模式完全不可区分的情形，无法描述部分可区分粒子的可见态；需要建立一个能自洽描述部分可区分性及其对称谱的普适框架。

Method: 将全同粒子自由度划分为受酉演化的"可见"部分与动态不变的"内部"部分；用对称群上的不可区分性函数刻画内部标记态的置换对称性，并据此给出可见态的显式表达（推广对称化公设）。进一步用对称谱上的投影测度刻画独立发射单粒子的不可区分性，并借助边缘投影测度推导层级式上界。

Result: 得到以不可区分性函数表示的可见态显式形式；给出独立稳定源单粒子不可区分性由对称谱投影测度完全刻画；证明不可区分性投影测度可由边缘投影测度给出逐级上界；提出通过直接实验读取边缘投影测度来获得任意大量单光子不可区分性上界的可行方案；并分析相干叠加与凸混合的不可区分性，解决了一个近期提出的问题。

Conclusion: 该框架将部分可区分性纳入统一的置换对称性描述，为全同粒子不可区分性的度量、上界估计和实验测定提供了自洽且可操作的理论工具。

Abstract: Identical particles have dynamically invariant labels in all setups where their degrees of freedom can be partitioned into two parts, which we call internal and visible, with the visible part subject to unitary evolution, while the dynamically invariant internal part accounts for the partial distinguishability of particles. We give the explicit form of the visible state in terms of the indistinguishability function, generalizing the standard symmetrization/antisymmetrization postulate for bosons/fermions with internal modes. The indistinguishability function on the symmetric group accounts for the permutation symmetry of the dynamically invariant label state and gives a self-contained description of the symmetry spectrum of the visible state. It is shown that the indistinguishability of single particles emitted independently from a stable source is completely characterized by the projective measures on the symmetry spectrum. We reveal a hierarchy of successive upper bounds on the projective measure of indistinguishability in terms of marginal projective measures. We point out an experimentally feasible way to get the upper bounds on the indistinguishability of an arbitrarily large number of single photons by direct experimental readout of their marginal projective measures. We also analyze the indistinguishability of coherent superpositions and convex mixtures and resolve a recently posed problem.

</details>


### [128] [From breakfast, lunch, and dinner to the Bell-inequality tetrahedron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14436)
*Francesco Giacosa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文借助"双胞胎"教学模型直观讨论并可视化贝尔不等式，展示局域模型、量子模型与无信号模型在混合矩空间中的几何关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝尔不等式的概念较为抽象，需要一种简单、具象的教学化解释与可视化方式，帮助理解纠缠粒子关联与局域性、无信号约束之间的关系。

Method: 构造一对"双胞胎"系统（源自 2605.03104），两人对相同"问题"（如是否喜欢早/午/晚餐）给出完全相同的回答，类比远距离纠缠粒子对同一测量方向得到相同结果。在混合矩空间 $(X,Y,Z)$ 中（$X$ 表示早餐-午餐相关性，相同回答为 $1$，相反为 $-1$）分析三种区域：贝尔局域模型构成四面体，量子空间由嵌入该四面体的椭球体（elliptope）界定，无信号区域为立方体 $[-1,1]^3$。

Result: 在 $(X,Y,Z)$ 空间中，局域模型集合含于一个四面体内，量子可实现集合由椭球体给出并包含该四面体，而完整无信号集合恰为立方体 $[-1,1]^3$。该双胞胎情境可应用于希格斯玻色子衰变为费米子的过程。

Conclusion: 该双胞胎类比为贝尔不等式提供了一种简洁直观的教学与可视化框架，清晰刻画了局域、量子与无信号三种集合的嵌套几何结构，并可推广至粒子物理中的具体衰变情境。

Abstract: A simple pedagogical discussion and visualization of certain Bell inequalities is presented with the help of a system involving two \textquotedblleft twins\textquotedblright, originally discussed in 2605.03104. These twins answer identically when the same `question' is posed, here exemplified with: Did you like breakfast/lunch/dinner? In analogy, for two entangled particles at distant locations, the `twin-like' quantum state is such that the same outcome for the same measurement (i.e. spin direction) is obtained. In the space $(X,Y,Z)$ of mixed moments (i.e. $X$ refers to breakfast-lunch correlation, being $1$ for same answers and $-1$ for opposite ones), the space of Bell local models is contained in a tetrahedron, while the quantum space is bound by an elliptope that embeds the tetrahedron. The whole no-signalling region in the $(X,Y,Z)$ space is the cube $[-1,1]^{3}.$ The twin scenario applies e.g. to the decay of the Higgs into fermions.

</details>


### [129] [Squeezing measurement with spectrally broadened in microstructured fiber local oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14454)
*A. Chudakov,V. Severin,A. Poshevkina,A. Finochenko,K. Kuznetsov,A. Levchenko,E. Serebryannikov,A. Fedotov,D. Kalashnikov,P. Sharapova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出并通过实验验证了一种利用微结构光纤展宽本振（LO）带宽的技术，以改善其与多模压缩光模式的光谱匹配，并在type-0和type-II PDC产生的压缩光模式中测量了压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量（CV）量子计算因使用压缩态而能提升系统维度，受到广泛关注。多模压缩光由参量下转换（PDC）产生，具有宽带特性，涵盖多个频率模式。然而，其宽带结构要求本振（LO）与压缩光模式精确匹配，才能实现正确的零差探测与处理。由于LO的光谱带宽通常远窄于压缩光模式带宽，这种匹配极具挑战性，因此需要一种扩展LO带宽的方法。

Method: 作者提出并实验演示了一种通过让本振光经过微结构光纤来展宽其带宽的技术。通过在type-0和type-II PDC产生的多模压缩光模式中进行压缩测量，对该方案进行了验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，展宽后的本振开始在光谱上与type-II PDC产生的模式相匹配，从而验证了所提概念的有效性。

Conclusion: 该技术有望为多种连续变量量子系统与协议的实现提供帮助。

Abstract: Recently, continuous variables (CV) quantum computing, i. e. computing employing squeezed states, has attracted significant attention, as it enables to increase the dimensionality of the system. In CV protocols, homodyne detection plays a key role in the realization of the required gates and operations. Multimode squeezed light generated via parametric down-conversion (PDC) benefits from the broadband bandwidth, which encompasses multiple frequency modes. However, its broadband structure simultaneously requires the precise matching of the local oscillator (LO) with the squeezed light modes for their correct homodyne detection and processing. Achieving such matching is usually a challenging task, since the spectral bandwidth of the local oscillator is typically significantly narrower than the bandwidth of the squeezed-light modes. In this work, we propose and experimentally demonstrate a technique for expanding the bandwidth of the LO by passing it through the microstructured fiber. We validate the proposed concept by measuring squeezing in the modes of multimode squeezed light generated in type-0 and type-II PDC. We show that the broadened LO starts to spectrally match the modes generated via type-II PDC. The presented technique might be beneficial for implementing various CV systems and protocols.

</details>


### [130] [Arithmetic of Bohr Frequencies Governs Uniform Mixing in Randomly Timed Quantum Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14463)
*Musung Kang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 随机化读出时间可使均匀耦合XY自旋链的模干涉被平均；本文完整刻画了在何种自旋数N下，对任意初始格点都能得到严格均匀的格点布居。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子行走中模式干涉会破坏格点布居的均匀性，随机化读出时间是一种自然的平均手段。作者希望确定这种平均在均匀耦合XY链上何时能对每个初始态都实现严格均匀布居，并理解其算术与谱结构根源。

Method: 将随机时间平均化为对模干涉的余弦相干项求平均；证明均匀性唯一地由各模的镜像宇称固定相干值；把由相等Bohr频率造成的"宇称碰撞"转化为单位根消失和（vanishing sums of roots of unity）的分类问题；在可行情形下用环面上的相位分布构造证明。

Result: 对N≥2个自旋，当N+1为2的幂、素数或两倍素数时可实现完全均匀；当15|(N+1)、21|(N+1)，或6|(N+1)且N≥11时不可能。均匀性唯一决定平均余弦相干；相等Bohr频率会强制不相容的相干取值，此类宇称碰撞用单位根消失和刻画并完成分类。

Conclusion: 随机读出时间下的均匀性是数论性质与谱简并共同决定的现象：可行性由N+1的算术结构给出充要条件，不可行性源于宇称碰撞导致的相干约束冲突。

Abstract: Randomizing the readout time averages the mode interference in a quantum walk. We ask when this makes the site populations of a uniformly coupled $XY$ chain exactly uniform for every initial site. For $N\ge2$ spins, this is possible when $N+1$ is a power of two, a prime, or twice a prime. It is impossible when $15\mid(N+1)$, $21\mid(N+1)$, or $6\mid(N+1)$ with $N\ge11$. Uniformity uniquely fixes the averaged cosine coherences according to the mirror parities of the modes. Equal Bohr frequencies can impose incompatible coherence values. We classify these parity collisions using vanishing sums of roots of unity and prove existence in the positive cases through a phase distribution on a torus.

</details>


### [131] [NAQsim: Full-Stack Architecture Simulation Framework for Fast and Space-Efficient Neutral Atom Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14478)
*Yosuke Ueno,Shinichi Sunami,Toshihide Hinokuma,Yasunari Suzuki,Akihisa Goban,Hayata Yamasaki,Teruo Tanimoto,Ilkwon Byun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出开源仿真框架 NAQsim，用于基于横向门的中性原子容错量子计算（FTQC）架构的全栈评估，并通过该框架识别出"patch rotation"瓶颈，提出近无旋转架构 D3-ROT，在典型基准负载上实现 2.27 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子平台在 FTQC 设计中展现出潜力，但其物理操作速度比超导量子比特等其他平台慢数个数量级。因此架构设计者需要识别快速高效的 FTQC 架构，而这依赖于能够在软件、经典硬件与量子器件栈之间进行探索的可靠建模工具。

Method: 作者提出 NAQsim——一个面向基于横向门的中性原子 FTQC 架构的开源仿真框架，其核心能力是支持细粒度的全栈架构评估。作为首个用例，作者利用该工具识别出 patch rotation 这一性能瓶颈，并进行全栈协同优化，最终推导出近无旋转架构 D3-ROT。

Result: 在实用的 FTQC 基准负载上，D3-ROT 仅针对 patch rotation 这一单一瓶颈即实现了 2.27 倍加速，且面积（footprint）开销极小。

Conclusion: 结果表明 NAQsim 为横向表面码架构及其他架构开辟了更广泛的全栈优化空间，有望推动中性原子 FTQC 架构的高效设计。

Abstract: Technological advances in neutral-atom platforms have opened a new path toward designing efficient protocols for fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC). However, each physical operation is still orders of magnitude slower than on other platforms, such as superconducting qubits. Therefore, architects must identify fast and efficient FTQC architectures, which require reliable modeling tools to explore various design choices across the software, classical hardware, and quantum device stacks of neutral-atom platforms.
  In this paper, we propose NAQsim, an open-source simulation framework for neutral-atom FTQC architectures based on transversal gates. As its key feature, NAQsim enables detailed full-stack architecture evaluation that opens opportunities to explore previously overlooked performance bottlenecks. As a first use case for NAQsim, we identify one such bottleneck, patch rotations, perform full-stack co-optimization, and finally derive a near-rotation-free architecture (D3-ROT). For practical FTQC benchmark workloads, D3-ROT achieves a 2.27x speedup from this single bottleneck alone, with minimal footprint overhead. These results point to a much broader space of full-stack optimizations that NAQsim makes accessible for transversal surface-code architectures and beyond.

</details>


### [132] [Exact computation of quantum wave functions for nonlinear potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14480)
*Winfried Loghmiller,Jean-Jacques Slotine*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于经典最小作用量原理精确求解薛定谔方程：先解Hamilton-Jacobi方程得作用量，再沿所有驻定作用量路径计算经典传播密度，进而仅用经典量构造精确核与波函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作表明薛定谔方程可仅依据经典最小作用量精确求解，但该方法依赖"传播的经典平方根密度沿每条驻定作用量路径为调和函数"这一条件；本文旨在将这一条件推广到任意非线性势，从而原则上替代量子微扰论的近似方法。

Method: 沿每条驻定作用量路径传播经典平方根密度，其调和性可由传播作用量的拉普拉斯量为纯时间变化函数来保证。对任意非线性势，引入调和坐标变换及相应的传播经典密度与量子波的时间尺度变换，验证该条件仍不失一般性成立；所得时间尺度化薛定谔方程的量子波与原方程等价，推广了Duru-Kleinert的经典结果。在1至3维系统中，该调和坐标变换把非线性势转化为线性振子的二次势，而后者已有精确解析作用量与波解。

Result: 该构造对双缝、量子隧穿、EPR实验等基本情形成立，此处还讨论了希格斯玻色子的相对论传播子；其密度本质上不同于使用已有整体量子波范数的Madelung密度。对1至3维系统，调和坐标变换将非线性势化为线性振子的二次势，使四次势、非线性摆等此前无精确解的基本情形也能绕过氢原子波函数直接构造量子波。

Conclusion: 通过调和坐标变换与时间尺度化，可在任意非线性势下验证传播经典密度的调和条件，所得量子波与原薛定谔方程等价，原则上可取代量子微扰论的近似，并为四次势、非线性摆等无解析解系统提供精确构造途径。

Abstract: Recent work shows that the Schroedinger equation can be solved exactly based only on classical least action rspa.2025.0413. The computation is based on first solving a Hamilton-Jacobi equation for the action, computing the classical propagated density along all stationary action paths, and finally constructing the exact kernel and wave function based on these classical quantities alone. The method requires that the propagated classical square root density along each stationary action path is harmonic (where a sufficient condition is that the Laplacian of the propagated action is purely time-varying), as is the case for basic examples such as the double-slit, quantum tunneling, the Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen experiment, or also as discussed here the relativistic propagator of a Higgs boson. This density is fundamentally different from using the Madelung density, which is the norm of an existing overall quantum wave.
  In the case of arbitrary nonlinear potentials, this condition can still be verified without loss of generality, by using a harmonic coordinate transformation and an associated time scaling for the propagated classical density and quantum wave, as this paper details. The resulting quantum wave of the time-scaled Schroedinger equation is equivalent to the quantum wave of the original Schroedinger equation, extending a standard result of Duru and Kleinert. Hence, in principle it can replace the approximations of quantum perturbation theory.
  For 1 to 3-dimensional systems, this harmonic coordinate change transforms a nonlinear potential into the quadratic potential of a linear oscillator, for which an exact analytic action and wave solution are already known. This makes the construction of the quantum wave beyond the hydrogen wave straightforward for basic cases where no exact solution has been derived, such as the quartic potential or the nonlinear pendulum.

</details>


### [133] [Robustness of Hidden-Variable Theories and Matrix Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14519)
*Giulio Malavolta,Harold Nieuwboer,Akshay Ramachandran,Michael Walter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文否证了Aaronson关于Schrödinger隐变量理论鲁棒性的猜想，构造了反例：两个指数接近的酉矩阵对应的联合分布差异显著；并提出修正理论恢复鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson受量子基础与复杂性理论启发，基于Schrödinger的提议形式化了一种隐变量理论，其中联合概率分布通过Sinkhorn算法（对酉矩阵元素模的行列缩放）构造。他猜想该映射具有鲁棒性——输入的多项式级小扰动仅导致分布的多项式级小扰动——这对复杂性理论应用至关重要。检验并澄清该猜想的真伪，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 作者构造反例：选取一个纯态和两个指数接近的酉矩阵，分析Sinkhorn算法赋予的联合概率分布差异。同时提出对Schrödinger理论的修正版本，以在保留其他理想性质的同时满足鲁棒性。

Result: 反例表明：两酉矩阵指数接近，但对应联合分布至少在一个元素上相差反线性量级，且总变差距离为常数，故原猜想不成立。修正理论则满足鲁棒性且保留其他良好性质。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了隐变量理论可同时满足的Aaronson各公理：原Schrödinger理论因鲁棒性失效而不满足全部公理，而修正版本实现了鲁棒性与其余性质的兼顾。

Abstract: Motivated by quantum foundations and complexity theory, Aaronson formalized a hidden-variable theory inspired by a proposal by Schrödinger. To any quantum state and unitary, this Schrödinger theory assigns a joint probability distribution via the Sinkhorn algorithm: rescale the columns and rows of the entrywise modulus of the unitary so that the marginals match the Born rule for the initial and final quantum states, respectively. He conjectured that this map is robust, i.e., inverse polynomially small perturbations in the inputs lead to inverse polynomially small perturbations of the joint distribution, which is important for complexity theoretic applications. We present a counterexample to this conjecture: We construct a pure state and two unitaries that are exponentially close, yet the joint probability distributions assigned by the Schrödinger theory differ by at least an inverse-linear term in at least one entry, and by a constant in total variation distance. We also propose a modified version of Schrödinger's theory that satisfies robustness, while retaining all of its other desirable properties. This yields a complete picture of which axioms of Aaronson can be simultaneously satisfied by hidden-variable theories.

</details>


### [134] [Preparation of Multimode NOON States via Floquet-Engineered Conditional Chiral Excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14524)
*Mengxue Li,Bo Tian,H. Z. Shen,Haodi Liu,Gangcheng Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 Floquet 工程化的条件路由框架，用 d 能级控制器调控 d 个玻色模式，实现任意奇数 d 的 NOON 态制备与可编程复振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NOON 态具有海森堡极限相位灵敏度，对量子增强计量至关重要，但多模、任意奇数维 NOON 态的可控生成仍缺乏系统方案。

Method: 构建 Floquet 工程化的条件路由框架：一个 d 能级量子控制器介导 d 个玻色模式的动力学。对任意奇数 d=2s+1，通过调节控制器初态相干控制手性激发流方向，不同本征态对应不同循环路由分支；选用 s 个谐波构造精确的 qudit 控制循环置换，用于制备奇数 d 模 NOON 态，并可编程复振幅。以三模、五模为具体实现，分析系统误差与耗散影响。

Result: 三模情形仅需单个谐波，呈现两个反向手性分支和一个静止分支；五模情形需两个谐波，包含最近邻与次近邻手性跃迁。三模实例表明，在特定参数区间内路由操作对系统误差与耗散保持高精度。

Conclusion: 建立了可控手性路由与任意奇数维网络多模 NOON 态生成的系统 Floquet 构造方法，在量子信息处理与量子计量中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: The NOON states possess Heisenberg-limited phase sensitivity and are crucial for quantum-enhanced metrology. In our work, we develop a Floquet-engineered conditional-routing framework in which a $d$-level quantum controller mediates the dynamics of $d$ bosonic modes. For an arbitrary odd dimension $d=2s+1$, the $d$-level system can act as a ``quantum knob" by tuning its initial states to coherently control the direction of chiral excitation flow, such that different controller eigenstates generate distinct cyclic-routing branches of the bosonic excitations. By choosing $s$ harmonics, we construct an exact qudit-controlled cyclic permutation and use it to formulate a general protocol for preparing odd-$d$-mode NOON states. The same framework also enables programmable complex amplitudes through appropriate preparation of the controller state. As concrete realizations, we show that the three-mode case requires only a single harmonic and exhibits two oppositely directed chiral branches together with a stationary branch, whereas the five-mode case requires two harmonics and both nearest- and next-nearest-neighbor chiral hoppings. Meanwhile, taking the three-mode realization as a representative example, we assess the influence of systematic imperfections and dissipation on the conditional-routing dynamics and identify parameter regimes in which the routing operation remains accurate. Our results establish a systematic Floquet construction for controllable chiral routing and multimode NOON-state generation in arbitrary odd-dimensional networks, with potential applications in quantum information processing and quantum metrology.

</details>


### [135] [Parameter-Efficient Quantum NLP for Paraphrase Detection: Performance, Robustness, and Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14529)
*Farha Nausheen,Khandakar Ahmed,Farina Riaz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对10量子比特混合量子-经典变分电路（2148参数）在MRPC、QQP、PAWS三个复述检测基准上进行参数匹配的实证评测：QQP上达75.53%±0.75%并显著优于经典基线，MRPC上达BERT-base的92%且参数少54,000，PAWS上展现无需对抗训练的鲁棒性（召回98.2%）。研究首次系统完成NLP混合变分电路的参数匹配多基准验证，全部结果基于经典模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习在自然语言任务上的严格实证验证仍然稀缺，缺乏参数匹配、多基准的系统性对比证据，因此需要针对复述检测开展严谨的实证研究。

Method: 构建10量子比特混合量子-经典变分电路（2,148参数），在MRPC、Quora Question Pairs（QQP，n=10随机种子）与对抗性PAWS三个基准上评测；与参数量匹配的经典基线（DeepMLP、DistilBERT-4bit）及BERT-base比较；开展电路深度分析，并用Meyer-Wallach度量进行纠缠分析（四个变体）。

Result: QQP上达75.53%±0.75%准确率，显著优于参数匹配经典基线（DeepMLP：p=0.015，Cohen's d=1.20；F1：p<0.001，d=2.32），并以少31,191参数超越DistilBERT-4bit；MRPC上最优2层变体以低54,000参数达到BERT-base精度的92%；深度分析显示数据集-深度缩放效应；Meyer-Wallach纠缠分析表明多量子比特纠缠为主要性能驱动因素（r=0.85）；PAWS上无对抗训练即呈现涌现鲁棒性（召回98.2% vs 经典81.6%，+16.6个百分点，d=1.24，p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 该工作构成针对NLP的混合变分电路首个系统的、参数匹配的多基准实证验证；多量子比特纠缠是性能主要驱动力，并观察到无需对抗训练的鲁棒性。所有结果均来自经典模拟，硬件验证为未来工作。

Abstract: Rigorous empirical validation of quantum machine learning on natural language tasks remains scarce. We evaluate a 10-qubit hybrid quantum-classical variational circuit (2,148 parameters) for paraphrase detection across three benchmarks: MRPC, Quora Question Pairs (QQP), and adversarial PAWS. On QQP (n = 10 seeds), the circuit achieves 75.53% +/- 0.75%. accuracy, statistically outperforming parameter-matched classical baselines (DeepMLP: p = 0.015, Cohen's d = 1.20; F1: p < 0.001, d = 2.32) and surpassing DistilBERT-4bit with 31,191 fewer parameters. On MRPC the optimal 2-layer variant reaches 92% of BERT-base accuracy at 54,000 lower parameter cost. Circuit depth analysis reveals a dataset-depth scaling effect; entanglement analysis via the Meyer-Wallach measure identifies multi-qubit entanglement as the primary performance driver (r = 0.85 across four variants). Adversarial evaluation on PAWS reveals emergent robustness: 98.2% recall versus 81.6% classical (+16.6 pp, d = 1.24, p < 0.001), without adversarial training. These results constitute the first systematic parameter-matched multi-benchmark empirical validation of hybrid variational circuits for NLP. All results are from classical simulation; hardware validation is future work.

</details>


### [136] [Proving olympiad geometry theorems on a superconducting quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14533)
*Ning Wang,Zheng-Zhi Sun,Zhengyi Cui,Yiren Zou,Aosai Zhang,Fanhao Shen,Jiarun Zhong,Zehang Bao,Zitian Zhu,Han Wang,Jia-Nan Yang,Jiayuan Shen,Gongyu Liu,Yanzhe Wang,Yihang Han,Yiyang He,Jiahua Huang,Sailang Zhou,Xinrong Zhang,Yaozu Wu,Zixuan Song,Jinfeng Deng,Hang Dong,Qi Ye,Weikang Li,Si Jiang,Yixuan Ma,Shuangyue Geng,Zhide Lu,Chao Song,Hekang Li,Pengfei Zhang,Qiujiang Guo,H. Wang,Dong-Ling Deng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在可编程超导量子处理器上实验实现了自动几何定理证明。作者提出两个互补的量子证明框架：其一是用“量子伪除法”实现吴代数消元法的量子代数定理证明，并以叠加态表示多元多项式；其二是通过混合量子策略引导架构，将全角法实现为逆向符号推理。作为示例，在超导量子处理器上证明了正方形对角线互相垂直定理和一道1978年国际数学奥林匹克几何题。该工作从实验层面表明自动逻辑推理是近期量子处理器的可行任务，并为量子增强的符号智能提供了具体路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动定理证明是人工智能的核心目标之一，支撑众多应用。尽管近期神经符号系统取得显著进展，但其运行最终仍受经典计算架构约束。量子计算能够实现超越经典极限的信息编码与相干并行性，从而为加速结构化符号演绎提供了可能。因此，作者希望在实验层面验证自动逻辑推理能否成为近期量子处理器的可行任务，并探索量子增强符号智能的路径。

Method: 在完全可编程的超导量子处理器上实验实现自动几何定理证明，并开发了两个互补的量子证明框架。第一个框架用“量子伪除法”实现吴（Wu）代数消元法，将多元多项式表示为量子叠加态，实现量子代数定理证明。第二个框架通过混合量子策略引导架构，将全角法实现为逆向符号推理，展示了通向量子符号证明搜索的一般路线。具体以正方形对角线垂直定理和一道1978年国际数学奥林匹克几何题作为示例进行证明验证。

Result: 在超导量子处理器上成功证明了两个定理：正方形对角线互相垂直，以及一道1978年国际数学奥林匹克几何问题。两个互补框架——基于量子伪除法的吴消元法与基于混合量子策略引导的全角法——均在实验中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该研究在实验层面确立了自动逻辑推理作为近期量子处理器可行任务的地位，并提供了通向量子增强符号智能的具体路径。这表明量子计算有潜力突破经典架构对神经符号系统的限制，为加速结构化符号演绎和构建量子增强的符号智能系统开辟了方向。

Abstract: Automated theorem proving seeks to use computational systems to prove or disprove mathematical and logical statements [1, 2]. It underpins a wide range of applications, and enhancing theorem-proving capabilities remains a central objective in artificial intelligence [3]. Although recent neuro-symbolic systems have achieved remarkable progress [4-7], their operation is ultimately constrained by classical computational architectures. Quantum computing [8], by contrast, enables information encoding and coherent parallelism beyond classical limits [9-14], raising the possibility of accelerating structured symbolic deduction [15]. Here we report the experimental realization of automated geometry theorem proving on a fully programmable superconducting quantum processor. We develop two complementary quantum proving frameworks. The first implements Wu's algebraic elimination method using quantum pseudo-division, with multivariate polynomials represented in superposition states, enabling quantum algebraic theorem proving. The second implements the full-angle method as backward symbolic reasoning through a hybrid quantum strategy-guided architecture, demonstrating a general route toward quantum symbolic proof search. As illustrative examples, we prove two theorems on a superconducting quantum processor: the perpendicularity of the diagonals of a square and a 1978 International Mathematical Olympiad geometry problem. Our results establish, at the experimental level, automated logical reasoning as a viable task for near-term quantum processors and provide a concrete pathway toward quantum-enhanced symbolic intelligence.

</details>


### [137] [Controlling quantum state transfer in rooted products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14537)
*Addison Ballif,Christino Tamon,Gabriel Tucker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将图论中的根积（rooted product）引入量子态传输研究，证明了一个简洁的传递原理：若图 X 具有量子态传输且 Y 可控，则根积 X^Y 继承量子态传输；并指出根积图更稀疏，即使 X 本身不具备量子态传输，也能构造高效高保真态传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Godsil 与 McKay（1978）表明根积是构造非同行谱图对的有力工具。本文的动机在于：尽管根积缺乏便利的张量积结构，但作者希望挖掘其在量子态传输（quantum state transfer）中的潜力。现有文献中笛卡尔积（Cartesian product）保留完美态传输是已知性质，但根积作为更稀疏的图结构，可能在构造高效、高保真度态传输方面更具优势，甚至能在底图 X 不具备量子态传输时仍实现良好传输。

Method: 作者提出并证明了一个简洁的传递原理（transference principle）：若图 X 具有量子态传输，且图 Y 可控（controllable），则它们的根积 X^Y 具有量子态传输，且该性质继承自 X。证明利用了根积能够产生大量强共谱顶点对（strongly cospectral pairs of vertices）这一事实，并通过其悬挂子图（pendant subgraph）控制条件数（condition number）。

Result: 证明了根积 X^Y 在 X 具态传输、Y 可控时继承量子态传输性质，该结论与笛卡尔积保留完美态传输的性质互补。进一步表明根积图显著更稀疏，且即使 X 无量子态传输，根积仍能被用于构造高效高保真度的态传输。

Conclusion: 根积虽无便利的张量积结构，但在量子态传输构造中是有用工具。它为在稀疏图中实现高效、高保真态传输提供了新途径，其机理依赖于根积产生的大量强共谱顶点对以及由悬挂子图可控的条件数。

Abstract: Godsil and McKay (1978) showed that the rooted product is a powerful tool for constructing non-isomorphic cospectral pairs of graphs. Despite lacking a convenient tensor product structure, we show that the rooted product is useful for constructing graphs with good quantum state transfer properties. In particular, we prove a simple transference principle: if a graph $X$ has quantum state transfer and $Y$ is a controllable graph, their rooted product $X^Y$ has quantum state transfer (inherited from $X$). This complements a folklore property of Cartesian product which preserves perfect state transfer. However, the rooted product is a significantly sparser graph and, more importantly, can be easily used to construct efficient high-fidelity state transfer even if $X$ has no quantum state transfer. Our proof exploits the fact that a rooted product creates a large number of strongly cospectral pairs of vertices and that its condition number can be controlled by its pendant subgraph.

</details>


### [138] [Exact $1/4$ mean-rung population and $\sqrt{2}$ continuum revival-time ratio in the anti-Jaynes--Cummings vertex at $f_\star=\sqrt{\bar n+1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14563)
*Onyango Stephen Okeyo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究Jaynes-Cummings (JC) 与anti-Jaynes-Cummings (AJC) 顶点在零失谐与零Kerr条件下对无量纲驱动参数 f=ω/λ 的不同响应，证明AJC的平均激发态布居在 f⋆=√(n̄+1) 处精确等于1/4，而JC为1/2，并给出泊松平均、调制深度、灵敏度及参数映射等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望揭示JC与AJC两种旋转/反旋转耦合顶点在驱动参数扫描下的本质差异：JC在零失谐零Kerr时对 f 不敏感（"盲"），而AJC敏感。通过对AJC激发态布居、调制深度与复活时间比的定量刻画，为探测反旋转项提供可行的观测量，并借助Floquet工程与具体实验平台实现该效应。

Method: 解析计算AJC块的平均rung激发态布居，得到在 f⋆=√(n̄+1) 处精确值1/4，与JC在同一位置的1/2对比；对泊松分布进行平均得到 n̄=16 时的包络值0.24650，并用 -n̄/[16(n̄+1)²] 复现偏差；计算AJC反转的调制深度(1/2)及连续区复活时间比 r=t_R^AJC/t_R^JC 的相对灵敏度最大值 1/(2√(n̄+1))；用 2(n_max+1) 态的全对角化验证解析结果；提出由Floquet工程的反旋转耦合生成1/4值，条件是静态横向速率满足 g₀≪g₁；并给出理想囚禁离子蓝边带与磁通可调电路QED耦合器的参数翻译。

Result: AJC块的平均rung激发态布居在 f⋆=√(n̄+1) 处精确等于1/4，而JC在同一点为1/2；泊松平均包络在 n̄=16 时为0.24650，与精确值偏差在 3.5×10⁻³ 内，且偏差由 -n̄/[16(n̄+1)²] 再现；在 f⋆ 处AJC反转调制深度为1/2，复活时间比 r 的相对灵敏度最大，值为 1/(2√(n̄+1))；2(n_max+1) 态全对角化确认解析值；1/4值可由Floquet工程反旋转耦合产生，条件为 g₀≪g₁；并给出囚禁离子蓝边带与磁通可调电路QED耦合器的参数翻译。

Conclusion: JC顶点在零失谐零Kerr时对 f 不敏感，而AJC顶点敏感；AJC在 f⋆=√(n̄+1) 的激发态布居1/4构成区别于JC 1/2的清晰判别信号，其调制深度与复活时间比灵敏度亦在 f⋆ 处达到特征极值。该结果可通过Floquet工程反旋转耦合（g₀≪g₁）实现，并可映射到囚禁离子蓝边带与磁通可调电路QED平台，为实验探测反旋转项提供了定量方案。

Abstract: At atomic resonance the Jaynes--Cummings (JC) vertex of a single bosonic mode coupled to a two-level emitter is blind to $f=ω/λ$ at zero detuning and Kerr; the anti-Jaynes--Cummings (AJC) vertex is not. We show that the mean-rung excited population of the AJC block equals exactly $1/4$ at $f_\star=\sqrt{\bar n+1}$, while the JC population at the same point is $1/2$. The Poisson-averaged packet value at $\bar n=16$ is $0.24650$, within $3.5\times 10^{-3}$ of the exact value, with the deviation reproduced by $-\bar n/[16(\bar n+1)^2]$. At $f_\star$ the modulation depth of the AJC inversion is $1/2$ and the relative sensitivity of the continuum revival-time ratio $r=t_R^{\rm AJC}/t_R^{\rm JC}$ is maximal with value $1/(2\sqrt{\bar n+1})$. Full diagonalization on $2(n_{\max}{+}1)$ states confirms the analytic values. The $1/4$ value can be generated by Floquet-engineered counter-rotating coupling provided that the static transverse rate satisfies $g_0\ll g_1$. Parameter translations for an ideal trapped-ion blue sideband and a flux-tunable circuit-QED coupler are reported.

</details>


### [139] [Phase-controlled bipartite and tripartite entanglement and Bell nonlocality in a closed-loop optomechanical system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14577)
*Sha-Sha Zheng,Zi-Nan Wu,Di-Di Xu,Chang-Yu Li,Peng-Wei Gong,Qiongyi He,Feng-Xiao Sun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出闭环三模光力系统中利用相位敏感耦合实现纠缠与贝尔非局域性的产生、调控与验证方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多体量子关联的产生、调控与验证，寻求相位可调、抗噪声的量子网络与量子基础检验平台。

Method: 构建闭环三模光力系统，两个光学模同时与机械模通过典型光力相互作用耦合，并经由场传输相互耦合，形成相位敏感耦合；通过调节相对相位控制光学模间布居分布；采用位移宇称测量在相空间构造二体与三体贝尔不等式。

Result: 实现两个光力对间二体纠缠的确定性切换及三模间可调真三体纠缠；二体与三体贝尔不等式最大违背分别约为2.32与3，与理想EPR态可达值完全一致；发现真三体纠缠消失时三体贝尔非局域性仍可存在；系统分析机械耗散与热噪声影响并确定贝尔违背存活的参数区域。

Conclusion: 建立了多体量子关联产生、控制与验证的综合框架，为相位可调量子网络及抗噪声量子基础检验提供途径。

Abstract: We propose a novel scheme to generate and manipulate bipartite and tripartite entanglement and Bell nonlocality in a closed-loop three-mode optomechanical system, where two optical modes are simultaneously coupled to a mechanical mode via typical optomechanical interactions and also coupled to each other through field transmission. This configuration gives rise to a phase-sensitive coupling, in which the relative phase directly controls the population distribution between two optical modes, enabling coherent redistribution of quantum correlations. By tuning this relative phase, we achieve deterministic switching of bipartite entanglement between two optical-mechanical pairs, as well as tunable genuine tripartite entanglement among three modes. Employing the displaced-parity measurement, we construct both the bipartite and tripartite Bell inequalities in phase space and observe maximal violations of approximately $2.32$ and $3$, respectively, which coincide exactly with the values achievable for ideal bipartite and tripartite Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen states. Counterintuitively, we find that the tripartite Bell nonlocality can persist even when the genuine tripartite entanglement is absent, providing deeper insight into the relationship between these two types of quantum correlations. Furthermore, we systematically analyze the effects of mechanical dissipation and thermal noise, identifying the parameter regions where Bell violation survives under realistic experimental conditions. Our results establish a comprehensive framework for generating, controlling, and verifying multipartite quantum correlations, paving the way towards phase-tunable quantum networks and noise-resilient tests of quantum foundations.

</details>


### [140] [When is global evolutionary search useful for variational quantum algorithms? A landscape-first study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14594)
*Vojtěch Novák,Ivan Zelinka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 采用景观优先设计，控制QAOA实验揭示使局域搜索失效的两种机制，并验证自适应DE在困难自旋玻璃上的优势及景观评分的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法将态制备转化为经典非凸优化，但何时多重启局域搜索足够、何时需付出种群式全局搜索的评估代价尚不明确。

Method: 景观优先设计；控制QAOA实验识别机制；在八个未见自旋玻璃、新N=10与N=12实例及MaxCut、横场Ising、Heisenberg模型上测试自适应差分进化（DE）与多重启BFGS/Powell对比；设置独立深度对照；用随机起点局域结果与1D参数切片构造预基准景观评分，并在50个新量子目标上验证其条件级准确率。

Result: 在两个困难构造的全部八个未见自旋玻璃上，自适应DE的中位误差低于多重启BFGS与Powell，独立深度对照无此效果；参数复用效应在两种规模的MaxCut上可迁移，标准VQE模型仍利于局域搜索；景观评分以80–86%条件级准确率预测自适应DE是否优于多重启BFGS超过一个谱范围百分点。

Conclusion: 全局搜索效用的最清晰指标是局域搜索常陷入劣质盆地，而非电路深度或曲率各向异性；结果为“在具备稳健全局搜索时以量子电路复杂度换取更难经典优化”提供动机。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms recast state preparation as classical nonconvex optimization, but it is often unclear when multistart local search suffices and when population-based global search justifies its evaluation cost. Using a landscape-first design, controlled QAOA experiments identify two mechanisms making local search unreliable: parameter reuse across circuit layers and competition between 2-local and 3-local cost terms. Increasing QAOA depth alone does not replicate this effect. We test these mechanisms across eight unseen spin glasses, fresh $N=10$ and $N=12$ instances, and standard MaxCut, transverse-field Ising, and Heisenberg models. On all eight unseen spin glasses, adaptive differential evolution (DE) achieves lower median error than both multistart BFGS and multistart Powell on the two difficult constructions, whereas the independent-depth control does not. The parameter-reuse effect transfers to MaxCut at both sizes, while standard VQE models remain favorable to local search. A pre-benchmark landscape score combining random-start local outcomes and 1D parameter slices is fixed from confirmation data before evaluating new optimizers. It predicts whether adaptive DE outperforms multistart BFGS by over one spectral-range percentage point on 50 new quantum objectives with 80--86% condition-level accuracy. The clearest indicator of global-search utility is that local search frequently traps in inferior basins, rather than circuit depth or curvature anisotropy. The findings motivate whether quantum circuit complexity can be traded for harder classical optimization when robust global search is available.

</details>


### [141] [Simulating Quantum Physics Experiments One Event at a Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14600)
*Hans De Raedt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨经验实验数据与连续理论物理模型之间的根本分歧，将"量子测量悖论"视为一种本体论错误（Jaynes的"心理投射谬误"），并提出事件逐次模拟（EBES）框架，通过因果驱动的确定性学习机自下而上生成离散事件数据，以规避连续时间与预设概率分布，从而建模标准量子理论无法触及的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子理论虽能成功预测系综平均，却对个体、逐次检测统计保持沉默；传统解释依赖波函数坍缩等特设机制，存在将抽象的数学工具（如波函数）误认为物理实在的本体论混淆。作者希望在不引入特设解释的前提下破解这些概念谜团。

Method: 提出事件逐次模拟（EBES）：一种模块化、离散事件仿真框架，采用因果驱动的确定性学习机（DLMs），绕过连续时间和预先设定的概率分布，以严格局域化的因果关系自下而上生成离散数据。

Result: EBES提供了一种计算高效的方法论，能够镜像真实的实验室观测，并对标准量子理论完全无法触及的现象进行建模。

Conclusion: 量子测量悖论源于本体论误置而非物理实在本身的矛盾；以离散、因果、事件驱动的建模范式（EBES）替代连续理论框架，为个体事件的检测统计提供了一条可行的、计算高效的研究路径。

Abstract: This text explores the fundamental divide between empirical laboratory data and continuous theoretical physics models, framing the "quantum measurement paradox" as an ontological error, specifically E.T. Jaynes' ``mind projection fallacy'' where abstract mathematical tools like wavefunctions are mistaken for physical reality. While quantum theory successfully predicts ensemble averages, it remains inherently silent on individual, event-by-event detection statistics. To resolve these conceptual mysteries without resorting to ad-hoc explanations like wavefunction collapse, the text introduces Event-By-Event Simulation (EBES). As a modular, discrete-event simulation framework utilizing causally-driven Deterministic Learning Machines (DLMs), EBES bypasses continuous time and pre-specified probability distributions. Instead, it generates discrete data from the bottom up in a strictly localized, cause-and-effect manner. Ultimately, EBES provides a computationally efficient methodology that mirrors actual laboratory observations, modeling phenomena that lie entirely beyond the reach of standard quantum theory.

</details>


### [142] [Dynamical Readout of Measurement Statistics and Emergent Entanglement-Like States in Classical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14620)
*Songbo Xie,Ethan Dickey,Syed Ahmed Taimoor,Sabre Kais*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"网络原生"读出方案：利用结构化经典网络的集体动力学，从其主导增长率的谱响应中信息完备地重构等效双量子比特态的测量统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图回答一个基础性问题：经典网络能否不仅编码类量子态，还能通过自身的集体动力学直接读出其测量统计。这关系到经典系统中类量子关联的可读出性与信息完备性问题。

Method: 构建一个结构化经典网络，其社区模式构成等效双量子比特态空间；网络连通性选取一个主导集体模式来编码状态；用一组固定的十个基本连通性微扰探测集体响应；主导增长率的变化构成实双量子比特扇区期望值的完备基，进而通过线性组合重构任意实投影算子对应的联合结果概率。

Result: 测得这十个谱响应后，同一数据可生成连续测量设置族上的关联。作为基准，编码的贝尔态在四个选定关联下给出 |S|=CHSH>2，而可分离乘积态参照仍处于 CHSH 界内（|S|≤2）。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了直接从经典网络谱响应中对等效双量子比特态进行信息完备动力学读出的方案，表明经典网络可通过集体动力学实现类量子测量统计的读取与贝尔型关联的再现。

Abstract: Can a classical network not only encode a quantum-like state, but also read out its measurement statistics through its own collective dynamics? Here we introduce a network-native readout scheme for a structured classical network whose community modes form an effective two-qubit state space. Network connectivity selects a dominant collective mode that encodes the state, while a fixed set of ten elementary connectivity perturbations probes its collective response. The resulting shifts of the dominant growth rate form a complete basis of expectation values for the real two-qubit sector, from which joint-outcome probabilities associated with arbitrary real projectors are reconstructed by linear combination. Once these ten spectral responses are measured, the same data generate correlations over a continuous family of measurement settings. As a benchmark, for an encoded Bell state four selected correlations give $|S|=\chsh>2$, whereas a separable product-state reference remains within the Clauser--Horne--Shimony--Holt (CHSH) bound $|S| \leq 2 $. These results establish an informationally complete dynamical readout of effective two-qubit states directly from the spectral response of a classical network.

</details>


### [143] [A pretty-tight converse on the classical capacity of generalized amplitude-damping channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14621)
*Kun Fang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对广义振幅阻尼信道经典容量这一开放问题，对Brandão等人提出的逆定理界进行松弛，使其可高效计算，并在数值上验证该界紧致且优于现有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义振幅阻尼信道的经典容量是量子香农理论中的未解问题。已有许多基本量子信道的容量已确定，但该信道仍缺乏严格结果，需要新的可计算且紧致的逆定理上界来逼近其容量。

Method: 重新审视Brandão等（PRL 106, 230502, 2011）提出的逆定理界，该界用某种关联度量对经典容量进行上界约束；针对广义振幅阻尼信道，推导该度量的一个松弛形式，使其可被高效计算，并进行数值比较。

Result: 数值比较表明，所得上界总体相当紧致，在非零温度下几乎与Holevo信息重合，并在整个参数范围内显著优于已有界。

Conclusion: 通过松弛Brandão等人的关联度量，获得了可高效计算的经典容量逆定理界，该界在数值上紧致且在全参数范围改进现有结果，为逼近广义振幅阻尼信道容量提供了有力工具。

Abstract: The classical capacity of a quantum channel is the highest rate at which classical information can be transmitted with vanishing error. Although the capacities of many fundamental quantum channels have been established, the capacity of the generalized amplitude-damping channel remains an open problem in quantum Shannon theory. In this work, we revisit a converse bound introduced by Brandão et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 106, 230502 (2011)], which upper-bounds the classical capacity in terms of a correlation measure. For the generalized amplitude-damping channel, we derive a relaxation of this measure that makes the converse bound efficiently computable. Numerical comparisons demonstrate that our bound is pretty tight in general and nearly coincides with the Holevo information at nonzero temperatures, significantly improving upon existing bounds across the entire parameter range.

</details>


### [144] [Derivatives of Quantum Randomness: Separating Pseudorandom Unitaries from Pseudorandom (Function-like) States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14626)
*Minki Hhan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了伪随机函数型量子态生成器（PRFSG）与伪随机酉算符（PRU）之间存在完整的酉oracle分离：即使是最强意义下的PRFSG也不能推出最弱意义下的PRU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算催生了态与酉算符的新型伪随机原语（PRSG、PRFSG、PRU），需厘清这些原语之间的蕴含关系，特别是态伪随机性与酉伪随机性是否存在本质区别。

Method: 将访问态生成oracle的候选PRU构造视为从底层oracle态到所实现酉算符的映射，研究该映射的导数；利用导数的固有低秩结构，将构造所得酉算符与真随机酉算符区分开。

Result: 在最强态概念与最弱酉概念之间实现了分离：即使自适应安全、量子可访问的PRFSG（允许非酉实现并使用任意数量辅助量子比特）也无法推出非自适应安全、仅前向的PRU。

Conclusion: 量子态伪随机性与量子酉伪随机性存在根本性区别；所提出的微分视角可用于研究量子态与酉算符的其它结构性问题。

Abstract: Quantum computation gives rise to new pseudorandom primitives for states and unitaries, including pseudorandom state generators (PRSGs), pseudorandom function-like state generators (PRFSGs), and pseudorandom unitaries (PRUs). In this paper, we show a full unitary oracle separation between PRFSGs and PRUs.
  The separation holds between the strongest state notion and the weakest unitary notion: even adaptively secure, quantum-accessible PRFSGs do not imply non-adaptively secure, forward-only PRUs, even when their implementations are allowed to be non-unitary and use an arbitrary number of ancillary qubits. This reveals a fundamental distinction between pseudorandomness for quantum states and for quantum unitaries.
  Our main technical idea is to view a candidate PRU construction with access to state generation oracles as a map from the underlying oracle states to implemented unitaries, and to study the derivatives of this map. These derivatives are inherently low rank, and we exploit this low-rank structure to distinguish the resulting unitaries from truly random ones. We believe this differential perspective may be useful for studying other structural questions about quantum states and unitaries.

</details>


### [145] [Linear equations mod $n$ are pseudo-telepathic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14632)
*Lorenzo Ciardo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明量子monad到模n线性方程多态克隆不存在自然变换，从而对每个n≥2构造出无解线性方程组，其约束系统博弈存在完美有限维量子策略，并完全刻画有限维伪传态约束语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子伪传态现象刻画的核心问题：哪些约束满足问题（CSP）在有限维量子策略下可被"伪传态"地欺骗（即经典无解但量子有完美策略）。已有工作用多态-量子minion刻画量子伪传态，但缺少对具体约束语言，尤其线性方程组的完整分类。

Method: 结合两条主线：(1) Harding–Jager–Smith关于Hilbert空间子空间上群值测度的结果；(2) 量子伪传态的多态-minion（polymorphism-minion）刻画。核心证明是量子monad（dimension 2n）到模n线性方程多态克隆之间不存在自然变换。

Result: 主定理：维数2n的量子monad不存在到模n线性方程多态克隆的自然变换。推论一：对每个n≥2，存在Z_n上无解线性方程组，其约束系统博弈拥有完美有限维量子策略。推论二：完全刻画有限维伪传态约束语言。

Conclusion: 以范畴论（monad自然变换不存在性）统一解释了模n线性方程系统的有限维量子伪传态现象，给出有限维伪传态约束语言的完整分类，深化了量子伪传态与CSP多态理论之间的联系。

Abstract: We prove that the quantum monad in dimension $2n$ admits no natural transformation to the polymorphism clone of linear equations modulo $n$. Consequently, for every $n\geq 2$, there exists an unsatisfiable system of linear equations over $\mathbb{Z}_n$ whose constraint system game admits a perfect finite-dimensional quantum strategy. As a corollary, we completely characterise pseudo-telepathic constraint languages in finite dimension. The proof combines a result of Harding, Jager, and Smith on group-valued measures on subspaces of Hilbert spaces with the polymorphism-minion characterisation of quantum pseudo-telepathy.

</details>


### [146] [Collective-dissipation-controlled thermal rectification and entropy production in a three-terminal three-qubit XXZ spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14644)
*Alireza Nourmandipour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究三量子比特XXZ自旋链在局域浴与集体浴共同耦合下的热整流及热力学代价，发现集体浴电流随温度改变符号，各向异性可调控边界热流，整流系数最高约20.1%，并存在低不可逆操作窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索集体耗散作为量子热输运的可调控制机制，并揭示热整流、热流大小与不可逆损耗之间的权衡关系。

Method: 构建三量子比特XXZ自旋链模型，耦合三个热库：两个局域浴分别连接边界量子比特，一个集体浴同时耦合中间与右侧量子比特；计算稳态热流与熵产生率，分析整流系数随偏置、各向异性及边界耦合的变化。

Result: 集体浴携带的热流随其温度变化而改变符号；自旋链各向异性可有效抑制边界热流。最大偏置下整流系数约3.36%，强各向异性区可达20.1%，但后者伴随热流显著降低。识别出集体电流反转附近的低不可逆操作窗口，同时发现增大局域边界耦合会单调提高热力学代价。

Conclusion: 集体耗散可作为量子热输运的可调控制手段，且整流性能、热流强度与不可逆损耗之间存在非平凡的权衡关系。

Abstract: We investigate thermal rectification and its thermodynamic cost in a three-qubit XXZ spin chain coupled to three thermal reservoirs: two local baths attached to the boundary qubits and a collective bath jointly coupled to the middle and right qubits. This asymmetric dissipative architecture allows the collective environment to actively reshape energy transport. We show that the heat current carried by the collective bath changes sign as its temperature is varied, and that the spin-chain anisotropy provides an effective internal control parameter for suppressing the boundary heat currents. These features yield thermal rectification coefficients of about $3.36\%$ at maximum bias and up to $20.1\%$ in the strongly anisotropic regime, although the latter comes at the cost of severely reduced heat currents. Beyond rectification, we evaluate the steady-state entropy production rate and identify a low-irreversibility operating window near the collective-current reversal, whereas increasing the local boundary couplings monotonically raises the thermodynamic cost. Our results establish collective dissipation as a tunable control mechanism for quantum heat transport and uncover a nontrivial trade-off among rectification, heat-current magnitude, and irreversible losses.

</details>


### [147] [Exact Asymptotic Rates and an Exponential Strong Converse for quantum SMP and One-Way Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14658)
*Daiki Suruga*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明在同时消息传递（SMP）模型下，无论经典或量子、有无共享随机性/纠缠，计算 $f^n$ 的最优最坏情况每实例通信量渐近收敛到 $D^\parallel(f)=\log|\mathrm{Row}(f)|+\log|\mathrm{Col}(f)|$，并给出指数强逆定理及共享资源下的速率刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究联合计算多个实例是否能降低每实例通信量，以及这种节省在SMP模型中如何依赖量子消息与共享资源；同时厘清量子指纹识别等单实例量子优势在渐近速率下是否依然存在。

Method: 对所有有限全函数 $f$ 和固定误差 $\varepsilon$，证明 $f^n$ 的最优最坏情况每实例通信量收敛到 $D^\parallel(f)=\log|\mathrm{Row}(f)|+\log|\mathrm{Col}(f)|$；证明将SMP归约到单向通信，并将成功计算与基于量子最小熵的采样相联系；还给出用 pretty-good measurement 对关键引理的更简证明。

Result: 经典与量子SMP（无共享纠缠、有/无共享随机性）均达到同一阈值 $D^\parallel(f)$；量子指纹识别对单实例相等性的指数优势在渐近速率中消失；固定最坏情况通信率低于该阈值时，全部 $n$ 个输出正确的概率以 $2^{-\Omega(n)}$ 衰减（指数强逆）；有共享随机性时最优期望通信率为 $(1-\varepsilon)D^\parallel(f)$；每对发送者与裁判共享纠缠使两个速率减半，任意三方纠缠无进一步降低；结果推广到一类全关系，单向刻画中以 $\log|\mathrm{Row}(f)|$ 替代 $D^\parallel(f)$。

Conclusion: 联合计算在渐近意义下不能突破发送行、列索引的通信量下界，即使允许有界误差、联合计算和量子消息；共享随机性可节省因子 $(1-\varepsilon)$，发送者-裁判间纠缠可减半速率，任意三方纠缠无额外收益。

Abstract: Computing many instances jointly can reduce communication per instance. We ask whether such savings occur in the simultaneous-message-passing (SMP) model and how they depend on quantum messages and shared resources. For every finite total function $f$ and fixed error $0\le\varepsilon<1$, we prove that the optimal worst-case communication per instance for $f^n$ converges to \[ D^\parallel(f)=\log|\mathrm{Row}(f)|+\log|\mathrm{Col}(f)|, \] where $\mathrm{Row}(f)$ and $\mathrm{Col}(f)$ are the sets of distinct rows and columns of the table $(f(x,y))_{x,y}$. This holds for classical and quantum SMP without shared entanglement, with or without shared randomness. Thus, transmitting row and column indices is asymptotically optimal, even with bounded error, joint computation, and quantum messages. In particular, the exponential advantage of quantum fingerprinting for a single instance of equality without shared randomness disappears in the asymptotic rate. We also prove that the same threshold governs an exponential strong converse: every fixed worst-case communication rate below it forces the probability of computing all $n$ outputs correctly to decay as $2^{-Ω(n)}$, even on average under a fixed product input distribution. With shared randomness, the optimal expected communication rate is $(1-\varepsilon)D^\parallel(f)$. Entanglement shared between each sender and the referee halves both rates; arbitrary tripartite entanglement gives no further reduction. The results extend to a class of total relations and yield one-way characterizations with $D^\parallel(f)$ replaced by $\log|\mathrm{Row}(f)|$. Our proof reduces SMP to one-way communication and relates successful computation to sampling based on quantum min-entropy; we also give a simpler proof of the key lemma using the pretty-good measurement.

</details>


### [148] [Unique determination and generation of rank two single-qubit quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14707)
*Nina Kuklina,Vasilii Vasilenko,Boris Volkov,Alexander Pechen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明：在开放量子系统中，唯一确定秩二单量子比特非酉量子通道只需其作用于三个适当选择的密度矩阵；并据此数值研究了相干与非相干控制下的目标通道生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子技术中需要生成开放量子系统的目标演化，其核心是判断实际演化是否与目标演化一致。已有工作仅针对酉量子通道证明了用三个特殊密度矩阵即可判定，而非酉通道的受控生成与唯一确定问题尚待解决。

Method: 在开放量子系统中研究受控生成与唯一确定非酉量子通道，重点关注秩二单量子比特量子通道；从理论上证明仅需三个适当选择的密度矩阵上的作用即可唯一确定此类通道；并基于该结论，利用相干控制与非相干控制对多种非酉目标单量子比特通道的生成进行数值研究。

Result: 理论结果：对秩二单量子比特非酉量子通道，其作用在三个适当选择的密度矩阵上即足以唯一确定该通道。数值结果：在相干与非相干控制下实现了多种非酉目标单量子比特量子通道的生成研究。

Conclusion: 将Goerz等人关于酉通道三密度矩阵判定条件的结果推广到秩二单量子比特非酉量子通道，给出了唯一确定的充分条件，并通过数值实验验证了相干与非相干控制在非酉通道生成中的可行性。

Abstract: An important problem in quantum technologies is the generation of a target evolution of an open quantum system. A core component of this task is the ability to determine whether the actual evolution of the system coincides with the target evolution. For the generation of unitary quantum channels in open quantum systems, it was shown by Goerz, Reich and Koch [New J. Phys. {\bf 16} 055012 (2014)] that for determining whether the actual evolution coincides with a desired unitary it is sufficient to compare their action on three special density matrices. In this work, we consider controlled generation and unique determination of non-unitary quantum channels in open quantum systems with particular emphasis on rank two single-qubit quantum channels. We prove that to uniquely determine such quantum channels it is sufficient to consider their action on only three suitably chosen density matrices. Based on this theoretical result, we numerically investigate generation of various non-unitary target single-qubit quantum channels in open quantum systems using coherent and incoherent controls.

</details>


### [149] [Universal computation with magic Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14757)
*Marius Junge,Jason Pollack,Luke Visser*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出量子计算中"连续通用性"的概念：在廉价局部控制算符集合上加入一个昂贵的哈密顿量控制，即可实现通用量子计算，并针对1/2-局部泡利生成元与n-qubit"魔"哈密顿量（特别是Ising哈密顿量）进行了具体分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通用性理论基于酉门集合的性质，但许多实验装置实际提供的是参数化哈密顿量集合（可对任意时长取指数）。因此需要建立一种连续的通用性理论，刻画用少量昂贵哈密顿量控制配合廉价局部控制实现通用计算的条件。

Method: 寻找具体的1-或2-局部泡利生成元集合S与n-qubit"魔"哈密顿量H（重点为Ising哈密顿量J），使其共同生成完整的李代数su(2^n)；提出通过对这些生成元集合取指数直接实现任意酉变换的方法；给出所需魔哈密顿量作用时间的界；并采用Chow/Rashevskii技巧算法化地实现对应迭代对易子的酉变换。

Result: 找到多组使S与H共同生成su(2^n)的具体生成元集合；给出魔哈密顿量作用时间的界；approximating任意酉变换至精度ε所需H的应用次数随qubit数呈指数增长，与标准Solovay-Kitaev定理应用预期一致；发现随Ising哈密顿量参数变化，所生成李代数尺寸出现相变。

Conclusion: 连续通用性框架为哈密顿量型量子计算提供了理论刻画，表明单个昂贵控制哈密顿量配合廉价局部控制即可实现通用计算；Ising哈密顿量的参数可调控生成李代数的结构并出现相变，Chow/Rashevskii技巧可用于高效实现迭代对易子对应的酉变换。

Abstract: In the conventional theory of quantum computation, universality is discussed in terms of properties of unitary gate sets. In many experimental setups, however, we instead have access to a parametrized set of Hamiltonians, which can be exponentiated for any desired time. Accordingly, we formulate a continuous analogue of universality, where adding one expensive Hamiltonian control to a cheap set of local control operators allows for universal computation. We find several concrete sets of 1- or 2-local Pauli generators $S$ and $n$-qubit ``magic" Hamiltonians $H$ that together generate the full Lie algebra $\mathfrak{su}(2^n)$, with a special focus on the Ising Hamiltonian $J$. We propose a method to directly implement any unitary by exponentiating these generator sets, and give bounds on the needed time of application of the magic Hamiltonian $H$. We find that the number of applications of $H$ needed to approximate any unitary up to accuracy $ε$ scales exponentially in the number of qubits as expected from the standard application of the Solovay-Kitaev theorem. We also find phase transitions in the size of the generated Lie algebras as the parameters of the Ising Hamiltonian are varied. We find an algorithmic application of the Chow/Rashevskii technique to implement unitaries corresponding to iterated commutators.

</details>


### [150] [Systematic construction of multiqubit unextendible product bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14788)
*Caohan Cheng,Lin Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用图论方法研究多量子比特不可扩展正交矩阵（UOM），系统构造了多量子比特不可扩展乘积基（UPB），并完全确定了任意维度下多量子比特UPB的尺寸谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多量子比特不可扩展乘积基（UPB）的构造长期是一个未解决问题，缺乏对一般维度下可能尺寸的系统性刻画。

Method: 通过图论方法研究UPB对应的不可扩展正交矩阵（UOM）；利用小UOM的行分解得到主要区间，用补全图确定其余较小UPB尺寸（其中一个尺寸由良好的列结构得到），并通过纤维数与行对计数证明其他尺寸的不存在性；仅用计算机进行少量有限UOM验证，精确测试列于证书附录。

Result: 确定了每个维度下完整的多量子比特UPB尺寸谱。

Conclusion: 图论与组合计数方法可系统解决多量子比特UPB的尺寸谱问题，为UPB构造提供了完整刻画。

Abstract: The construction of multiqubit unextendible product bases (UPBs) has been a long-standing problem. We give a systematic construction of multiqubit UPBs by studying their unextendible orthogonal matrices (UOMs) with graph-theoretic methods. Row decompositions of small UOMs produce the main intervals. Com- pletion graphs settle the remaining small UPB sizes, except for one size obtained from a good column structure. Nonexistence of the other UPB sizes follows from counts of fibres and row pairs. The computer is used only for a few finite UOM checks, whose exact tests are stated in the certificate appendix. These arguments determine the complete multiqubit UPB size spectrum in every dimension.

</details>


### [151] [Quantum Geometry in Hyperbolic Band Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14876)
*Ahmed Adel Mahmoud,Riccardo Sorbello,Ronny Thomale,Steven Rayan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"holonomy shaking"方法，通过周期性格点驱动在双曲能带理论（HBT）中动态提取量子度量，并在{8,3}双曲晶格与类kagome晶格上验证了高保真提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲能带理论揭示了负曲率空间中的诸多奇异物理现象，量子度量则成为描述量子几何与拓扑相的基本张量。然而连接这两大前沿的量子度量研究尚属空白；更关键的是，传统的欧几里得度量提取方案在双曲空间中的非对易几何下根本失效，因此需要发展新的提取手段。

Method: 提出holonomy shaking（和乐抖动）技术：利用周期性格点驱动，在非对易双曲几何中动态提取量子度量。以{8,3}正则双曲晶格和类kagome晶格为具体模型进行验证。

Result: 在{8,3}双曲晶格与类kagome晶格上实现了量子度量的高保真动态提取，验证了holonomy shaking方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为探测双曲量子几何提供了稳健蓝图，为在双曲量子物质中发现奇异拓扑现象开辟了道路。

Abstract: Hyperbolic band theory (HBT) has recently unveiled a wealth of exotic physical phenomena in negatively curved spaces. Concurrently, the quantum metric has emerged as a fundamental tensor governing quantum geometry and topological phases. In this Letter, we bridge these two frontiers by investigating the quantum metric in HBT. Because conventional Euclidean metric extraction protocols fundamentally fail in these non-commutative geometries, we introduce holonomy shaking, a novel, experimentally viable technique utilizing periodic lattice driving to dynamically extract the metric. Using the {8,3} regular hyperbolic and kagome-like lattices as concrete examples, we demonstrate the high-fidelity dynamical extraction of the quantum metric. Our results establish a robust blueprint for probing hyperbolic quantum geometries, paving the way for the discovery of exotic topological phenomena in hyperbolic quantum matter.

</details>


### [152] [The Ergotropy of Quantum Batteries under Unruh Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14919)
*Yiding Wang,Shengyan Ma,Xiaofen Huang,Tinggui Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究匀加速（Unruh效应）对Unruh-DeWitt探测器型量子电池ergotropy的影响，分别在二分与三分体系中分析加速电池、加速充电器及同时加速的情形，揭示Unruh效应对量子电池性能的双重作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将相对论量子场论与量子热力学相结合，探究匀加速（Unruh效应）如何影响量子电池的能量提取（ergotropy），并厘清是哪个子系统加速以及多体结构如何决定其增强或退化效应。

Method: 以Unruh-DeWitt探测器作为量子电池模型，采用微扰论方法，在二分体系（电池与充电器）和三分体系（一个电池与两个充电器）中系统比较三种加速情景：仅加速电池、仅加速充电器、同时加速两者。

Result: 二分体系中，仅电池加速会在临界加速度阈值处诱导ergotropy的突然出现，而复合体系能量变化随初始状态增加或减少；充电器加速在微扰区内使电池ergotropy保持恒定；同时加速因q依赖的相干相消导致ergotropy单调衰减。三分体系中，电池加速同样诱导ergotropy出现，而加速相邻充电器则不会，与二分体系充电器加速结果一致。

Conclusion: Unruh效应具有双重作用，既能增强也能退化量子电池性能，取决于被加速的子系统及多体结构，为相对论量子场论与量子热力学的交叉提供桥梁，并对实验可及平台具有参考意义。

Abstract: We study the effects of uniform acceleration on the ergotropy of quantum batteries modeled as Unruh-DeWitt detectors, in both bipartite and tripartite setups. In the bipartite system, we systematically compare three scenarios: accelerating the battery, accelerating the charger, and accelerating both simultaneously. We find that only battery acceleration can induce a sudden emergence of ergotropy at a critical acceleration threshold, while the corresponding composite-system energy change may increase or decrease depending on the initial state at the onset of acceleration. Charger acceleration leaves the battery ergotropy constant within the perturbative regime, while simultaneous acceleration leads to monotonic decay due to coherent cancellation of the q-dependence. Extending to a tripartite system with one battery and two chargers, we find that battery acceleration again induces ergotropy emergence, whereas accelerating adjacent charger does not--consistent with the bipartite charger-acceleration case. These results reveal that the Unruh effect plays a dual role, both enhancing and degrading quantum battery performance, depending on which subsystem is accelerated and on the multipartite structure, bridging relativistic quantum field theory and quantum thermodynamics with relevance to experimentally accessible platforms.

</details>


### [153] [Entanglement free Metrology Exploiting Multimode Hong Ou Mandel Sensor Advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14937)
*Qian Li,Jianning Han*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文用频率直积态（无纠缠、无玻色交换对称性）替代SPDC频率纠缠态进行多模频域HOM干涉测量，通过后选择频率反关联结果，证明其计量优势源自光谱反关联而非纠缠或玻色交换对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模频域HOM计量依赖SPDC产生的频率纠缠态，其需要强泵浦激光，难以实际实现；作者旨在寻找无需纠缠、易于实现的替代探测态，并厘清计量优势的真正物理来源。

Method: 采用频率直积态作为探针态（不具备纠缠与路径模式交换对称性），并对频率反关联的测量结果进行后选择，开展多模频域HOM干涉计量分析。

Result: 频率直积态在后选择频率反关联结果条件下，仍可获得多模HOM计量优势（如抗色散与相位噪声、大动态范围、兼容易损样品）。

Conclusion: 这些计量优势既不源于纠缠，也不源于玻色交换对称性，而是源于光谱反关联；该结论澄清了多模HOM计量优势的物理机制，并为无需强泵浦的实用化方案提供依据。

Abstract: The Hong-Ou-Mandel (HOM) interference in the multimode frequency domain has been widely used in precision metrology, benefiting from robustness against dispersion and phase noise, as well as a large dynamic range and compatibility with fragile samples. Conventional multimode HOM metrology exploits frequency-entangled states, which naturally satisfy bosonic exchange symmetry under any centered symmetric joint spectral distribution, to provide these advantages. However, these entangled states are typically generated via spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC), requiring strong pump lasers that hinder practical implementation. In this paper, we employ frequency product states, which do not possess entanglement or path-mode exchange symmetry, as the probe state and post-select measurement outcomes exhibiting frequency anti-correlation. Our results demonstrate that these metrological advantages arise neither from entanglement nor from bosonic exchange symmetry, but rather from spectral anti-correlation

</details>


### [154] [Universal Defect Statistics in Reverse Quenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14960)
*Eduard J. Braun,Daniel Rubin,Margaux Cartier,Gerhard Zürn,Matthias Weidemüller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将广义Kibble-Zurek机制(KZM)的随机缺陷形成理论推广到反向淬火协议，论证反向淬火中缺陷的普适标度行为一般成立，且等于正向淬火缺陷密度方差的二倍，并通过解析与数值方法验证，提出一种仅依赖全局磁场操控与全局磁化测量的实验可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向淬火中的缺陷普适标度行为此前仅在一维链等特定系统中被观察到，缺乏一般性理论依据；同时，探测量子临界行为通常需要微观层面的单个缺陷或局部测量，实验上难以实现。

Method: 基于概率论视角推导广义KZM的扩展形式，将随机缺陷形成方法应用于反向淬火协议；对一维横场Ising模型进行解析验证，对其键无序变体（由无限随机不动点支配）进行数值验证。

Result: 反向淬火缺陷的普适标度行为具有一般性，其标度由正向淬火缺陷密度方差的二倍给出；该结果在一维横场Ising模型中得到解析确认，并在键无序变体中数值确认。

Conclusion: 该协议从顺磁相出发，仅需系统磁场的全局操控与全局磁化测量，无需微观访问单个缺陷即可提取临界指数，为通过纯全局操作探测量子临界行为提供了鲁棒且实验可行的途径。

Abstract: We derive an extension of the generalized Kibble-Zurek mechanism (KZM), which provides a stochastic approach to defect formation, to reverse quench protocols. While the universal scaling behavior of defects in reverse quenches has been observed previously in certain 1D chains, we argument from a probability-theoretical perspective that this scaling persists in general, and is given by the double of the defect density variance in forward quenches. We validate these results analytically for the one-dimensional transverse field Ising model and numerically for its bond-disordered variant governed by an infinite randomness fixed point. Starting from the paramagnetic phase, this protocol relies solely on global control of the system's magnetic field and global magnetization measurements, enabling the extraction of critical exponents without microscopic access to individual defects. This approach offers a robust and experimentally feasible method for probing quantum critical behavior through entirely global operations.

</details>


### [155] [Exact Breit-Rabi formulae for the nitrogen-vacancy center in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14964)
*Alex Tritt,Lincoln D. Turner,Michael S. J. Barson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文给出了金刚石氮-空位（NV）色心与氮核超精细相互作用的精确闭式解，涵盖氮-14和氮-15两种同位素情形，替代了以往的数值方法和解析近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV色心的超精细结构对量子传感和量子计算至关重要，但现有计算其关于轴向磁场的超精细能量本征值与本征态（即NV Breit-Rabi公式）的方法依赖数值计算和解析近似，缺乏精确的闭式表达式。

Method: 推导并给出NV色心超精细能量本征值与本征态的精确、闭式（解析）表达式，分别针对氮-14和氮-15两种核自旋情形。

Result: 获得了针对氮-14和氮-15的NV超精细能量本征值与本征态的精确闭式解。

Conclusion: 该工作以精确解析形式取代了此前的数值方法与近似处理，为NV色心的超精细物理提供了完整的闭式描述，有助于量子传感与量子计算应用。

Abstract: The hyperfine structure that the nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center in diamond has with its nitrogen nucleus is of immense importance for quantum sensing and quantum computing. Current methods of calculating the NV hyperfine energy eigenvalues and eigenstates with respect to an axial magnetic field - i.e., calculating the NV Breit-Rabi formula - resort to numerical methods and analytic approximations. Here we provide an exact, closed-form expression to the NV hyperfine energy eigenvalues and eigenstates for both the nitrogen-14 and -15 cases.

</details>


### [156] [Correlation and Branching Mechanisms in the Quantum-to-Classical Transition of Interacting Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14989)
*Bingyu Cui*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从"约化动力学闭合"的视角分析相互作用系统的量子—经典过渡，用玻姆轨迹诊断概率流，通过三个模型揭示：约化坐标的经典描述能否涌现，取决于相互作用诱导关联是否被抑制，以及所关注的观测量是否对残余关联不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上量子—经典过渡常被归因于波包局域化（退相干）。但相互作用系统中，即使存在关联，约化动力学也可能闭合，从而出现经典或半经典描述。作者希望厘清：除局域化之外，相互作用诱导的关联如何进入约化运动方程，才是控制过渡的关键。

Method: 以玻姆轨迹作为全波函数概率流的诊断工具（而非替代性量子预言）。选取三个可分离不同机制的模型：(1) 硬核碰撞中宽相对坐标波包的领头尾部先于中心到达边界，经反射与干涉产生空间不可分离性；(2) Rabi 模型中自旋—位置协方差使混合量子—经典 Ehrenfest 近似失效；(3) 内态依赖力模型中总平均位置满足精确 Ehrenfest 方程，但波函数分裂为分支分辨的指针分量。

Result: 硬核碰撞中，波包尾部先行到达边界导致反射与干涉，产生空间不可分离性；Rabi 模型中，当自旋动力学不能仅由平均振子坐标决定时，自旋—位置协方差破坏混合 Ehrenfest 近似；内态依赖力模型中，总平均位置虽严格遵循 Ehrenfest 方程，波函数仍分裂为分支分辨的指针分量，即平均经典性并不意味着分支层经典性。

Conclusion: 相互作用系统的量子—经典过渡不仅由波包局域化控制，还取决于相互作用诱导的关联是否以及如何进入约化运动方程。平均量（如均值位置）表现出的经典性，可能掩盖分支层面上的量子关联，因此约化动力学的闭合性是判别经典性涌现的更精细判据。

Abstract: We analyze the quantum-to-classical transition of interacting systems from the viewpoint of reduced dynamical closure. Bohmian trajectories are used as a diagnostic of the probability flow generated by the full wavefunction, rather than as an alternative set of quantum predictions. A closed classical or semiclassical description of a retained coordinate can emerge either because interaction-induced correlations are suppressed or because the observable of interest is insensitive to the correlations that remain. Three models are used to isolate distinct mechanisms. In a hard-core collision, the leading tail of a broad relative-coordinate wave packet reaches the boundary before its center and generates spatial nonseparability through reflection and interference. In the Rabi model, the mixed quantum-classical Ehrenfest approximation fails when spin-position covariances prevent the spin dynamics from being determined solely by the mean oscillator coordinate. In an internal-state-dependent force model, the total mean position obeys an exact Ehrenfest equation, while the wavefunction separates into branch-resolved pointer components; mean classicality therefore does not imply branch-level classicality. These examples show that the quantum-to-classical transition of interacting systems is controlled not only by wave-packet localization but also by whether and how interaction-induced correlations enter the reduced equations of motion.

</details>


### [157] [Failure of the Asymptotic Equipartition Property for Quantum Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15004)
*Gilad Gour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子信道的渐近均分性质（AEP），证明基于CPTP映射平滑的AEP一般不成立，而基于子信道平滑的AEP等价于并行信道Stein引理的强逆定理、信道hockey-stick散度的尖锐阈值以及信道Lorenz散度的AEP。反例揭示了信道平滑与子信道平滑之间的分离，该分离在渐近意义下无界，即使子信道逼近误差指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道AEP的成立性是量子信息论中的核心问题。此前有四种被猜测等价的表述（分别涉及CPTP平滑、子信道平滑、Stein引理强逆、hockey-stick与Lorenz散度阈值），但缺乏统一刻画。作者希望澄清这些表述之间的关系，特别是精确保迹（CPTP）约束是否构成障碍。

Method: 构建反例：构造qutrit信道对，其最大相对熵有限，但CPTP平滑速率严格超过正则化信道相对熵；建立态与经典信道的维度无关完成界；提出“一致滤波器”（uniform filter），将Lorenz平滑转化为单个邻近子信道，用于证明四种表述的等价性。

Result: 1）CPTP平滑的AEP一般不成立；2）子信道平滑的AEP等价于并行信道Stein引理强逆、hockey-stick散度尖锐阈值及Lorenz散度AEP；3）信道与子信道平滑之间存在分离，单次间隙对qubit即无界，渐近间隙在整个族上无界（即使子信道逼近误差指数衰减）；4）态与经典信道存在尖锐的维度无关完成界，而量子信道不存在。

Conclusion: 四种猜测表述共享同一渐近阈值，但该阈值对一般信道对的有效性仍开放。本文结果将阈值问题与精确保迹约束所导致的障碍分离开来，说明CPTP平滑的失败根源在于保迹约束而非阈值本身。

Abstract: We establish two results on the asymptotic equipartition property (AEP) for quantum channels. First, the AEP formulated using smoothing over completely positive trace-preserving maps fails in general. Second, the AEP with smoothing over subchannels (completely positive trace-nonincreasing maps) is equivalent to the strong converse for parallel quantum channel Stein's lemma, to a sharp threshold for the channel hockey-stick divergence, and to an AEP for the channel Lorenz divergence. The counterexample reveals a surprising separation between channel and subchannel smoothing. Although states and classical channels admit a sharp, dimension-independent completion bound, which we establish, the corresponding single-shot gap for quantum channels is unbounded already for qubits. This separation persists asymptotically: we construct qutrit channel pairs with finite max-relative entropy whose CPTP-smoothed rate strictly exceeds the regularized channel relative entropy. The asymptotic gap between channel and subchannel smoothing is unbounded across the family, even when the subchannel approximation error decays exponentially. To establish the equivalences, we develop a uniform filter that converts Lorenz smoothing into a single nearby subchannel. The four conjectured formulations thus share a common asymptotic threshold, whose validity for general channel pairs remains open. Together, these results separate this threshold question from the obstruction imposed by exact trace preservation.

</details>


### [158] [Scaling up multi-mode entanglement generated by mode swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15040)
*Yuki Kodama,Holger F. Hofmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过交换两个局域多模系统的模式对可产生纠缠，本文探讨三模及以上系统中高效产生纠缠的策略，并指出局域系统光子数输入的选择为输出光子数统计中的非局域干涉效应增添了新的自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域多模系统间可通过模式交换产生纠缠，但对三模及以上系统如何高效产生纠缠尚需研究，本文旨在探索此类高效纠缠产生策略。

Method: 考虑两个局域多模系统，通过交换一对模式来实现纠缠产生；针对具有三个或更多模式的系统，分析不同光子数输入选择下的纠缠产生策略，并研究输出光子数统计中的非局域干涉效应。

Result: 研究表明，局域系统中光子数输入的选择为输出光子数统计中观察到的非局域干涉效应增加了一个新的自由度。

Conclusion: 在三个及以上模式的系统中，通过恰当选择局域系统的光子数输入，可为高效产生纠缠提供额外的调控自由度。

Abstract: Entanglement between two local multi-photon multi-mode systems can be generated by swapping a pair of modes between the two local multi-mode systems. In this presentation, we consider possible strategies for the efficient generation of entanglement between systems with three or more modes. It is shown that the choice of photon number inputs in the local systems adds a new degree of freedom to the non-local interference effects observed in the output photon number statistics.

</details>


### [159] [Fermionic quantum error correction is never free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15059)
*Yifan Tang,Ingo Roth,Philippe Faist,Zi-Wen Liu,Jens Eisert,Zhenhuan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明非平凡的费米子量子纠错码的逻辑空间中不含纯费米子高斯态，因此精确或足够精确的近似费米子纠错必然需要非高斯操作；所需有界权重非高斯门的数量至少随码距和编码模式数线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子平台（如拓扑保护的Majorana量子比特、费米子冷原子）是可扩展量子计算的重要架构，但实现可扩展性需要量子纠错。与量子比特情形中可高效经典模拟的稳定子操作构成标准纠错框架不同，费米子纠错是否能在可高效模拟的二次（高斯）动力学范围内完成尚不清楚，这一基本问题亟需回答。

Method: 作者从理论层面研究费米子高斯（二次）操作在量子纠错中的能力：证明任意非平凡费米子纠错码的逻辑空间不含纯费米子高斯态，其核心依据是费米子纠错与Wick定理之间的根本不相容性；并进一步分析幺正码字制备所需的有界权重非高斯门数量下界，以及费米子高斯操作在纠缠蒸馏中的表现。

Result: 证明了任何精确且足够精确的近似费米子量子纠错都必须超出二次动力学的自由费米子范畴而使用非高斯操作；非平凡费米子纠错码的逻辑空间中不存在纯费米子高斯态；幺正码字制备所需的有界权重非高斯门数量至少随码距和编码模式数线性增长，构成随纠错强度和逻辑容量同步增加的内在资源开销；并揭示了费米子高斯操作在纠缠蒸馏中与玻色子对应情形的差异。

Conclusion: 该结果从物理实现和经典模拟两个角度揭示了费米子纠错的基本困难，表明其无法像量子比特稳定子框架那样局限于可高效模拟的高斯操作，并暗示其与费米子物相及态制备复杂度之间的联系。

Abstract: Fermionic platforms offer compelling architectures for quantum computing, ranging from topologically protected Majorana-based qubits to fermionic cold atoms. To achieve scalability, however, they require quantum error correction. In this work, we prove that any exact and sufficiently accurate approximate fermionic quantum error correction necessarily requires non-Gaussian operations, beyond the free-fermion regime of quadratic dynamics. This is in sharp contrast to the qubit setting, where the efficiently classically simulable stabilizer operations form the standard framework for quantum error correction. Specifically, we show that the logical space of any non-trivial fermionic error-correcting code contains no pure fermionic Gaussian state, utilizing a fundamental incompatibility between fermionic error correction and Wick's theorem. We further show that the required number of bounded-weight non-Gaussian gates for unitary codeword preparation grows at least linearly with both the code distance and the number of encoded modes, revealing an intrinsic resource overhead that increases simultaneously with error-protection strength and logical capacity. Furthermore, we analyze the performance of fermionic Gaussian operations in entanglement distillation, revealing a distinction from their bosonic counterparts. Our results reveal fundamental difficulties for fermionic error correction from the perspectives of both physical implementation and classical simulation, suggesting connections to fermionic phases of matter and state preparation complexity.

</details>


### [160] [Biorthogonal Time-Dependent Variational Principle for Non-Hermitian Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15099)
*Younes Javanmard,Sina Kazemian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出非厄米量子多体系统实时动力学的双正交含时变分原理：左右MPS满足耦合双变分切空间方程，交叉Gram矩阵定义斜投影；用无矩阵缩放Taylor作用传播非正规局域生成元，区分全耦合算法与独立传播近似，并可分辨双正交动力学量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米量子多体系统实时动力学缺乏兼顾精度与效率的变分框架：非正规生成元使传统厄米TDVP不适用；独立演化左右态易导致配对近奇异与重叠漂移。需要建立双正交变分原理，并厘清全耦合与近似传播的适用范围。

Method: 构建双正交含时变分原理，用独立左右矩阵乘积态满足耦合双变分切空间方程，以交叉Gram矩阵定义斜投影；采用无矩阵缩放Taylor作用传播非正规局域生成元，不组装稠密矩阵、不存Krylov基；提出全耦合算法（解交叉配对问题、联合截断两个键基）与适合大系统的独立传播近似，并以重叠漂移和键交叉矩阵最小奇异值诊断失效。

Result: 精确基准与收敛性测试验证方法；独立截断会使保留的左右配对近乎奇异，重叠漂移与键交叉矩阵最小奇异值能暴露该失效，耦合截断显著延缓之；应用于相互作用长程非厄米Ising链，分辨出双正交动力学量子相变，并给出弱虚场使主导临界时间由 t*|J|=1.84 移至 1.04。

Conclusion: 该双正交TDVP配合无矩阵缩放Taylor传播，为非厄米多体实时动力学提供可控精度的变分工具；全耦合截断在数值稳定性上优于独立传播近似，后者适用于大系统但需监测配对条件；方法可定量刻画弱虚场驱动的双正交动力学量子相变临界时间移动。

Abstract: We develop a biorthogonal time-dependent variational principle for real-time dynamics of non-Hermitian quantum many-body systems. Independent left and right matrix-product states obey coupled bivariational tangent-space equations whose cross-Gram matrix defines an oblique projection. A matrix-free scaled Taylor action propagates the resulting non-normal local generators without assembling dense matrices or storing a Krylov basis. We distinguish the fully coupled algorithm, which solves the cross-pairing problem and truncates the two bond bases jointly, from an efficient independently propagated approximation used for large systems. Independent truncation can make the retained left-right pairing nearly singular; overlap drift and the smallest singular value of the bond cross matrix expose this failure, while coupled truncation substantially delays it. Exact benchmarks and convergence tests validate the method. Applied to an interacting long-range non-Hermitian Ising chain, it resolves a biorthogonal dynamical quantum phase transition and shows that a weak imaginary field shifts the leading critical time from $t^{\ast}|J|=1.84$ to $1.04$.

</details>


### [161] [A train--prune--readout--rewrite workflow for interpretable quantum learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15139)
*Siran Zhang,Shuming Cheng,Xiang Li,Jinyi Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"训练—剪枝—读出—改写"工作流，用复值KAN将物理域约束与三类递进分析主张（代数等价读出、域内符号改写、变换检验表征）分离，并在1–10量子比特任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI for Science 不应止于数据拟合预测，还需从模型内部提取数学结构与可物理检验的表征；然而"物理意义"常被笼统宣称，缺乏区分物理接地、可读计算、符号压缩与表征有效性的分层判据。

Method: 构建 train–prune–readout–rewrite 流程；采用复值 Kolmogorov–Arnold 网络，利用显式边函数使剪枝后可解析读出保留计算；设置三级递进检验：代数等价读出、采样域上紧凑且忠实于教师的符号改写、基于变换的隐表征检验。任务包括单量子比特纯度与冯·诺依曼熵、双量子比特纠缠相关任务（局域酉变换、固定解码器迁移）、三量子比特分类与十量子比特纯度回归。

Result: 单量子比特任务中改写恢复了纯度的二次结构，而熵只能得到域受限的符号代理；物理对齐的变量分组保持符号保真度。双量子比特中共享学习暴露出共同内部表征：局域酉变换否定了直接不变坐标解释，固定解码器迁移显示共享激活以变换一致形式携带泡利关联信息，低阶非线性读出恢复任务相关的不变谱特征。预测测试中三量子比特分类与十量子比特纯度回归（超百万复值输入）保持高精度。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了证据分辨框架，可在受限复值科学学习中有区别地判定物理接地、可读计算、忠实符号压缩与经变换检验的物理结构，为AI for Science的可解释性与物理可信性提供分层标准。

Abstract: AI for Science aims not only to predict complex physical systems from data, but also to extract mathematical structure and physically testable representations from learned models. Here, a train--prune--readout--rewrite workflow is developed that separates physical-domain grounding from three increasingly stringent analysis claims: algebraically equivalent readout of a trained predictor, compact teacher-faithful symbolic rewriting on the sampled physical domain, and transformation-based tests of learned internal representations. The workflow is implemented with complex-valued Kolmogorov--Arnold networks, whose explicit edge functions enable post-pruning analytic readout of the retained computation. In analytically controlled single-qubit tasks, rewriting recovered the quadratic structure of purity, whereas von Neumann entropy yielded only a domain-bounded symbolic surrogate; physics-aligned variable grouping preserved symbolic fidelity. For two-qubit entanglement-related tasks, shared learning exposed a common internal representation whose physical content was interrogated directly. Local-unitary transformations rejected a direct invariant-coordinate interpretation, while fixed-decoder transfer showed that the shared activation carries Pauli-correlation information in a transformation-consistent form. Task-related invariant spectral features were subsequently recovered through low-order nonlinear readouts. Separate predictive tests retained high accuracy for three-qubit classification and controlled ten-qubit purity regression with over one million complex inputs. These results establish an evidence-resolved framework for distinguishing physical grounding, readable computation, faithful symbolic compression and transformation-tested physical structure in constrained complex-valued scientific learning.

</details>


### [162] [Comment on J.Qin et al., Unconditional and Robust Quantum Metrological Advantage beyond N00N States, PRL 130, 070801 (2023) and J.A. H. Nielsen et.al., Deterministic Quantum Phase Estimation beyond N00N States. PRL 130, 123603 (2023)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15212)
*Zdenek Hradil,Jaroslav Rehacek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文质疑Qin等与Nielsen等关于压缩真空相位传感可实现无条件量子计量优势的结论，认为其以每次试验或每探测光子的量子费舍尔信息（QFI）作为性能指标在统计上不成立，若一致地计入构建估计量所需的总实验资源，则所报协议相对经典相位传感策略并无无条件计量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注量子计量学中"无条件优势"这一强主张的证据充分性问题：指出原文以QFI/trial或QFI/探测光子作为计量性能度量，缺少对估计量构建所需总资源的统计考量，因而该解释缺乏统计学依据，需重新审视结论的成立条件。

Method: 本文为评论性/分析性工作，采用资源核算（resource accounting）的统计论证框架：对两篇原文所依赖的QFI性能指标进行重新诠释，考察每次试验或每探测光子归一的QFI是否可作为计量性能的有效度量，并在计入构建估计量所需总实验资源的条件下，与经典相位传感策略进行对比。

Result: 分析表明，以QFI/trial或QFI/探测光子为依据的性能解读在统计上缺乏正当性，因为其未纳入获得估计量所需的总资源；在一致资源核算下，所报协议的计量表现不再超越经典相位传感策略，即"无条件"优势无法成立。

Conclusion: Qin等与Nielsen等所声称的无条件量子计量优势依赖于不当的性能度量解释；若采用统计上一致且完整的资源核算方式，压缩真空相位传感协议不具备相对经典策略的无条件优势。量子计量优势的宣称应以完整的资源核算与可实现的估计量性能为依据。

Abstract: The papers by Qin et al. [1] and Nielsen et al. [2] report an unconditional quantum metrological advantage based on squeezed-vacuum phase sensing. The principal evidence for this claim is the Quantum Fisher Information (QFI) evaluated per trial or per detected photon, which is subsequently interpreted as a measure of metrological performance. This interpretation is statistically unjustified because it does not account for the total experimental resources required to construct an estimator.
