Consequently, the reported protocols do not provide an unconditional metrological advantage over classical phase-sensing strategies when resource accounting is performed consistently.

</details>


### [163] [Feature-Adaptive Fusion in Hybrid Quantum-Classical Neural Networks for Robust Biomedical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15267)
*Yan-Yan Hou,Jian Li,Chongqiang Ye,Hengji Li,Zhuo Wang,Qinghui Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出特征自适应融合的混合量子-经典神经网络FAF-HQNN，用于生物医学图像分类，在MedMNIST上取得最优精度与更强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合量子-经典模型多采用固定或全局共享的融合策略，难以充分利用量子分支携带的互补信息，尤其在分布偏移（数据损坏）条件下性能受限。

Method: 模型由经典深度特征编码器与变分量子电路（VQC）组成，并引入特征自适应融合机制，动态为经典预测与量子预测分配权重；在PathMNIST与BloodMNIST两个MedMNIST基准上，于干净与受损测试条件下进行评估，并分析电路布局、测量基与电路深度的影响。

Result: 在干净数据上取得对比方法中最优的整体性能；在高斯噪声、椒盐噪声与泊松噪声损坏下鲁棒性提升；即使浅层变分量子电路也能提供有用的互补信息。

Conclusion: 特征自适应融合是提升混合量子-经典模型在生物医学图像分类中精度与鲁棒性的有效策略。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical neural networks provide a promising approach for incorporating quantum circuits into machine learning in the noisy intermediate-scale quantum regime. However, existing hybrid models often rely on fixed or globally shared fusion strategies, which may limit their ability to exploit complementary information carried by quantum branches, especially under distribution shifts. In this work, we propose a Feature-Adaptive Fusion Hybrid Quantum-Classical Neural Network (FAF-HQNN) for biomedical image classification. The model combines a classical deep feature encoder with a variational quantum circuit (VQC) and introduces a feature-adaptive fusion mechanism to dynamically weight classical and quantum predictions. We evaluate FAF-HQNN on two MedMNIST benchmarks, PathMNIST and BloodMNIST, under clean and corrupted test conditions. FAF-HQNN achieves the strongest overall performance on clean data among the compared methods and shows improved robustness under Gaussian, salt-and-pepper, and Poisson corruptions. Further analysis of circuit layout, measurement basis, and depth shows that even shallow variational quantum circuits can provide useful complementary information. These results demonstrate that feature-adaptive fusion is an effective strategy for improving accuracy and robustness in hybrid quantum-classical models for biomedical image classification.

</details>


### [164] [Variational Real-Time Dynamics on Reduced Operator Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15272)
*Aeishah Ameera Anuar,PV Sriluckshmy,Riccardo Rossi,Fedor Simkovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出算符投影变分量子实时演化（OVQRTE），仅需期望值测量即可实时演化参数化电路，在超导处理器上验证并用于QSCI采样，实现关联凝聚态系统研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联量子系统的精确实时模拟对经典方法与近期量子硬件均具挑战性；现有变分实时演化算法量子资源开销大，需要降低测量成本与资源需求。

Method: OVQRTE通过强制选定观测算子集合满足Ehrenfest方程来更新参数化电路；仅依赖期望值测量，通过算子集合选择在精度与测量成本间权衡；在模拟器上实现Heisenberg模型动力学，并在IQM Emerald超导处理器上以最多24量子比特基准测试Anderson杂质模型；进一步用OVQRTE为量子选择组态相互作用（QSCI）采样计算基态，在自洽ghost-Gutzwiller Ansatz（gGut）嵌入回路中计算基态能量与态密度。

Result: 相较于现有变分实时演化算法显著降低量子资源需求；在Heisenberg模型模拟与24量子比特Anderson杂质模型硬件实验中完成基准测试；结合QSCI与gGut嵌入回路成功计算基态能量与态密度。

Conclusion: OVQRTE是面向近期量子硬件研究关联凝聚态系统的有前景方法。

Abstract: Accurate real-time simulation of correlated quantum systems remains challenging for both classical methods and near-term quantum hardware. We introduce operator-projected variational quantum real-time evolution (OVQRTE), which updates a parameterized circuit by enforcing the Ehrenfest equations for a selected set of observables. OVQRTE requires only expectation-value measurements, while the choice of operator set enables a systematic trade-off between accuracy and measurement cost, substantially reducing quantum-resource requirements relative to existing variational real-time-evolution algorithms. After implementing OVQRTE dynamics of Heisenberg model on a simulator, we benchmark the algorithm for the Anderson impurity models on the IQM Emerald superconducting processor using up to 24 qubits. We further use OVQRTE to sample computational-basis states for quantum-selected configuration interaction (QSCI), enabling the calculation of the ground-state energy and density of states within a self-consistent ghost-Gutzwiller Ansatz (gGut) embedding loop. Our results establish OVQRTE as a promising approach for investigating correlated condensed-matter systems on near-term quantum hardware.

</details>


### [165] [Klein Tunneling of Dirac Fermions through Electromagnetic Barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15287)
*Lingang Zhang,Hua Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用狄拉克方程的洛伦兹协变性，求出了面内电场与垂直磁场下狄拉克费米子的一般解，并据此重新研究了异质结中的克莱因隧穿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹协变性是电磁规律在不同惯性系下的基本原理。作者希望利用该协变性统一处理面内电场与垂直磁场共存时狄拉克费米子的行为，并揭示其对克莱因隧穿的调控机制，为新型电子器件设计提供理论基础。

Method: 通过洛伦兹协变性，将面内电场E与垂直磁场B的问题变换到漂移速度沿E×B方向的惯性系，使场退化为纯磁场或纯电场；以临界场比E/B=v_F（v_F为费米速度）划分磁性与电性两种区制，求解一般解，并用布洛赫球上的立体角分析干涉相位。

Result: 磁区制下透射呈法布里-珀罗干涉引起的振荡；电区制下在漂移参考系中正入射时出现完全透射。干涉相位获得基于布洛赫球立体角的几何解释。此外建立了狄拉克锥倾斜与面内电场的对应关系：欠倾斜对应磁区制，过倾斜对应电区制。

Conclusion: 电磁场可有效调控克莱因隧穿，上述解与几何解释揭示了其内在机制，并为设计新型电子器件提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The Lorentz covariance of relativistic Dirac equations serves as a fundamental principle underlying the laws of electromagnetism across different inertial frames. Exploiting the covariance, we obtain the general solutions for Dirac fermions under both the in-plane electric $\boldsymbol{E}$ and perpendicular magnetic $\boldsymbol{B}$ fields, which reduce to either a magnetic or electric field in the inertial frame with drift velocity along the $\boldsymbol{E}\times\boldsymbol{B}$ direction. This dichotomy defines the magnetic and electric regimes, separated by the critical field ratio $E/B=v_\text{F}$ with $v_\text{F}$ denoting the Fermi velocity of Dirac fermions. Using these solutions, we revisit Klein tunneling through a heterojunction with generalized electromagnetic potentials. In the magnetic regime, the transmission exhibits oscillations governed by the Fabry-Pérot interference. In the electric regime, perfect transmission occurs at normal incidence in the drifted frame. The interference phase is further analyzed in terms of the solid angles on the Bloch sphere, providing a geometric interpretation of Klein tunneling. Finally, we briefly discuss the relation between the tilting of Dirac cones and the in-plane electric field, establishing the correspondence of the undertilted and overtilted cases to the magnetic and electric regimes, respectively. Our findings reveal the manipulation of Klein tunneling by electromagnetic fields, offering a theoretical basis for designing novel electronic devices.

</details>


### [166] [Universality from Quadratic Angular-Momentum Operations: Exact Dynamical Lie Algebras and the Role of the Casimir Obstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15302)
*Tim Heib,Pérola Milman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视对称粒子系统的普适性，证明线性与二次操作在每个固定粒子数子空间上仍可普适控制，但抽象层面所有次数≤2元素生成的动力学李代数并不穷尽 su_R(2) 的普遍包络代数，因二次Casimir的高次幂无法由对易子和线性组合生成；该障碍在固定不可约表示下不构成矛盾，且相关代数分别同构映射到 su_R(n+1) 与 u_R(n+1)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普适性依赖于结合具有不同代数性质的算符，而这些性质又取决于编码信息的Hilbert空间的物理结构。此前有更强的主张认为所有次数≤2元素能生成完整的普遍包络代数，本文对此进行重新审查与澄清。

Method: 在角动量系统框架下，考察由转动及二次自旋压缩相互作用（即线性与二次操作）生成的动力学李代数；分析所有次数至多二的元素所生成的代数结构，确定其抽象动力学李代数，并推导实用的二次普适性判据。

Result: 确认线性与二次操作在每个固定粒子数子空间上提供普适控制；但在抽象层面，所有次数≤2元素生成的动力学李代数并不能穷尽 su_R(2) 的普遍包络代数，二次Casimir的高次幂无法由对易子和线性组合生成；该障碍因固定自旋-n/2不可约表示下Casimir幂为标量倍数而不影响固定扇区普适性；中央自由代数与完整次数≤2代数分别映射到 su_R(n+1) 和 u_R(n+1)。

Conclusion: 线性与二次操作在固定粒子数子空间上具有普适性，且分别对应特殊酉普适性与完整酉普适性；抽象层面的障碍与固定扇区普适性不冲突，并给出了实用的二次普适性判据及有限维SSRC普适性的讨论。

Abstract: Universality relies on combining operators with different algebraic properties, which depend on the physical structure of the Hilbert space encoding the information. Systems of identical symmetric particles---including bosons and symmetric collections of spin-$1/2$ particles---can be mapped to angular-momentum systems, where rotations supplemented by quadratic, spin-squeezing interactions provide universal control. We revisit universality in this framework and confirm that such linear and quadratic operations provide universal control on every fixed-particle-number subspace. At the abstract level, however, we show that the dynamical Lie algebra generated by all degree-at-most-two elements does not exhaust the full universal enveloping algebra of $\mathfrak{su}_{\mathbb{R}}(2)$, contrary to a stronger claim made previously: higher powers of the quadratic Casimir element cannot be generated by commutators and linear combinations. This obstruction does not conflict with fixed-sector universality because, under a fixed irreducible spin-$n/2$ representation, all powers of the Casimir element act as scalar multiples of the identity. We determine the resulting abstract dynamical Lie algebras exactly and show that the central-free and full degree-at-most-two algebras map, respectively, onto $\mathfrak{su}_{\mathbb{R}}(n+1)$ and $\mathfrak{u}_{\mathbb{R}}(n+1)$, establishing special-unitary and full-unitary universality. We further derive a practical degree-two universality criterion and discuss finite-dimensional SSRC universality.

</details>


### [167] [Improving precision scaling via backaction-evading continuous measurement in a driven-dissipative Kerr parametric oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15345)
*Cheng Zhang,Xinhui Cui,Jiaying Pan,Xin-Qi Li,Mauro Cirio,Pengfei Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究驱动-耗散Kerr参量振荡器中耗散相变的临界增强量子传感，扩展此前高斯线性情形下的backaction-evasion策略，分析量子与经典Fisher信息在耗散临界点随Kerr非线性的标度行为，并实现改进统计收敛性的时间离散近似方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散相变为实现基于连续测量的临界增强量子传感提供了有前景的途径，但如何通过实际可行的测量方案实现这种增强仍是未解决的挑战。

Method: 将作者此前针对高斯线性情形提出的backaction-evasion策略推广至Kerr非线性系统，分析量子与经典Fisher信息在耗散临界点随Kerr非线性的标度；同时实现并基准测试具有更好统计收敛性的时间离散近似方案，用以计算连续零拍探测的经典Fisher信息。

Result: backaction-evading零拍监测实现了超越标准量子极限的增强光子数标度，并显著优于连续光子计数等替代协议；所提出的时间离散近似方法能够高效获取耗散临界点附近的经典Fisher信息。

Conclusion: backaction-evasion策略可有效提升耗散临界点的光子数标度并超越标准量子极限，所发展的离散化数值方法拓展了现有方法在临界点附近计算经典Fisher信息的可达范围。

Abstract: Dissipative phase transitions in the driven-dissipative Kerr parametric oscillator offer a promising route for realizing criticality-enhanced quantum sensing based on continuous measurements. However, achieving such enhancement through realistic measurement schemes remains an outstanding challenge. Here, we extend the backaction-evasion strategy introduced in our earlier work for the Gaussian linear case [arXiv:2511.22248 (2025)] to analyze how the quantum and classical Fisher information scale with the Kerr nonlinearity at dissipative critical points. Our results show that backaction-evading homodyne monitoring achieves enhanced photon-number scaling that surpasses the standard quantum limit, and significantly outperforms alternative protocols such as continuous photon counting. As an additional methodological contribution, we also implement and benchmark time-discrete approximation schemes with improved statistical convergence properties. We use these methods to compute the classical Fisher information for continuous homodyne detection, and demonstrate that they provide efficient access to this quantity near dissipative critical points, thereby extending the reach of existing methods.

</details>


### [168] [Object Detection Using Quantum Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15394)
*Mohammad Bahrami,Morteza Rafiee,Mohsen Norouzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种物理启发的混合经典-量子目标检测架构，将预训练MobileNetV2特征映射到八比特变分量子希尔伯特空间，并划分语义分类与几何定位两个子空间，通过量子期望值回归边界框。实验表明环形CNOT拓扑在抑制纠缠的同时保持高性能（mAP@0.5=0.9448），并用热力学框架将损失解释为亥姆霍兹自由能变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子迁移学习主要聚焦分类任务，不支持回归，因而无法直接应用于需要同时学习语义身份与连续空间几何的目标检测。作者希望填补量子迁移学习在目标检测（尤其是连续坐标回归）方面的空白，并探索纠缠、电路拓扑与检测性能之间的关系。

Method: 构建物理启发的混合经典-量子架构：预训练MobileNetV2提取视觉特征并映射至八比特变分量子希尔伯特空间；将该空间划分为语义分类与几何定位两个任务专属子空间；利用局部量子期望值预测连续边界框坐标；以电路拓扑调节两任务间的量子信息流动，并对比线性CNOT与环形CNOT两种拓扑；进一步引入热力学公式，将总学习损失解释为亥姆霍兹自由能变化。

Result: 环形架构实现二分冯·诺依曼熵为0.1688比特、Meyer-Wallach纠缠度为0.0370，同时取得mAP@0.5为0.9448、mAP@0.5:0.95为0.6558。相比线性拓扑，周期边界条件在抑制二分纠缠的同时未降低检测性能。热力学解释显示几何定位对应有效内能贡献，语义分类对应熵贡献。

Conclusion: 所提目标检测模型的高性能并不依赖最大化纠缠，而是可以通过拓扑控制的信息流、对纠缠的定向限制以及希尔伯特空间内的信息重整化实现高效学习。

Abstract: Object detection requires the joint learning of semantic identity and continuous spatial geometry. Existing quantum transfer learning approaches have focused mainly on classification tasks and do not support regression. Here, we introduce a physics informed hybrid classical-quantum architecture for object detection in which a pre-trained MobileNetV2 maps visual features into an eight qubits variationally quantum Hilbert space. The Hilbert space is partitioned into two task specific subspaces for semantic classification and geometric localization. Local quantum expectation values are used to predict continuous bounding box coordinates, while circuit topology regulates the flow of quantum information between the two tasks. We compare linear and circular CNOT topologies and show that periodic boundary conditions suppress bipartite entanglement without degrading detection performance. The circular architecture reaches a bipartite von Neumann entropy of 0.1688 bits and a Meyer-Wallach entanglement measure of 0.0370, while achieving an mAP@0.5 of 0.9448 and an mAP@0.5:0.95 of 0.6558. A thermodynamic formulation further interprets the total learning loss as a variation in Helmholtz free energy. It interprets geometric localization as an effective internal energy contribution and semantic classification as an entropic contribution. These results indicate that high performance in the proposed object detection model does not require maximal entanglement. Instead, efficient learning can emerge through topology controlled information flow, targeted restriction of entanglement, and information renormalization within the Hilbert space.

</details>


### [169] [Exact quantum circuits for a Heisenberg triangle with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15411)
*Andrey Zhukov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构建了三自旋海森堡三角形（含DM相互作用）的精确量子电路：变基后纯DM演化可化为两个单比特旋转，最多8个CNOT门；均匀耦合时固定基下最多10个CNOT门；不等DM耦合与非零交换相互作用时最多14个CNOT门；另给出仅用5个两比特DM门的精确实现，并以12自旋kagome格为例说明其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用的三自旋海森堡三角形，需要在其完整希尔伯特空间上实现精确的时间演化，以便为格点模型的量子模拟提供可复用的局域精确积木。同时希望降低电路开销，并解决纯DM演化以及DM门实现中的精确性（非近似）问题。

Method: 通过基变换将任意耦合下的纯DM演化约化为两个单比特旋转，从而构造CNOT门数最多为8的电路；在J与D各键相同的情况下，选取固定基得到对任意实数J、D、t均适用、最多10个CNOT门的电路；利用局域自旋旋转将构造推广到不等DM耦合与非零交换相互作用的族，最多需14个CNOT门；所有电路均在完整希尔伯特空间上作用。另一种纯DM实现使用五个两比特DM门，通过对一个对称Strang步的角度进行解析重调使其精确。

Result: 纯DM演化电路≤8个CNOT门；均匀J、D情形≤10个CNOT门（任意实数J、D、t）；推广到不等DM耦合及非零交换相互作用≤14个CNOT门；替代纯DM方案仅需5个两比特DM门（一个对称Strang步解析重调后精确）；以12自旋kagome格点模拟示例验证了这些精确局域块在格点模拟中的可用性。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了含DM相互作用三自旋海森堡三角形的精确量子电路构造，门开销低且作用于完整希尔伯特空间，可作为格点量子模拟中的精确局域积木；对称Strang步经解析角度重调可实现精确纯DM演化。

Abstract: We construct exact quantum circuits for a three-spin Heisenberg triangle with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interaction. A change of basis reduces pure DM evolution with arbitrary couplings to two single-qubit rotations, giving a circuit with at most 8 CNOT gates. When $J$ and $D$ are each the same on all bonds, one fixed basis gives a circuit with at most 10 CNOT gates for any real $J,D,t$. Local spin rotations extend the construction to a family with unequal DM couplings and nonzero exchange, requiring at most 14 CNOT gates. All circuits act on the full Hilbert space. An alternative pure DM implementation uses five two-qubit DM gates: analytically retuning the angles of one symmetric Strang step makes it exact. A 12-spin kagome example illustrates the use of these exact local blocks in lattice simulation.

</details>


### [170] [Noise robust self testing from genuine local operation shared randomness multipartite nonlocality Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15436)
*Som Kanjilal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对Phys. Rev. Lett. 129, 150401 (2022)中提出的真实多体非局域性N体不等式，在LOSR规则下构造了设备无关的自测试协议，并给出解析的噪声鲁棒自测试，证明当Bell分数亏损为ε时，态与测量作用存在显式的O(√ε)向量范数界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 违反该N体不等式可排除所有由至多N-1方任意资源局部组合（即使辅以全部N方共享随机性）得到的因果网络模型，因此该不等式的违反可作为判定真实多体非局域性的判据。然而，如何据此实现设备无关的自测试，尤其是噪声鲁棒情形，尚缺乏具体协议与定量界。

Method: 基于LOSR规则下的N体不等式，设计设备无关自测试协议；并进一步建立解析的噪声鲁棒自测试方法，通过Bell分数亏损ε推导态与对应测量作用的向量范数界。

Result: 证明了该不等式的违反可支撑一个设备无关自测试协议；给出解析的噪声鲁棒自测试，对于Bell分数亏损ε，得到态与测量作用的显式O(√ε)向量范数界。

Conclusion: 该工作将N体非局域性不等式的违反提升为可操作的设备无关自测试工具，并在噪声下给出定量鲁棒性保证。

Abstract: Violation of the $N$-partite inequality introduced in Phys. Rev. Lett. 129, 150401 (2022) for genuine multipartite nonlocality under local operations and shared randomness (LOSR) rules out all causal-network models obtained by locally composing arbitrary resources involving at most $N-1$ parties, even when supplemented by shared randomness among all $N$ parties. Here, we demonstrate a device-independent self-test protocol for this. We further establish an analytic noise-robust self-test. For a Bell-score deficit $ε$, we derive explicit $O(\sqrtε)$ vector-norm bounds for the state and the corresponding measurement actions.

</details>


### [171] [Electromagnetic Selection Rules and Coherent Manipulation of Quantum Skyrmion via Surface Acoustic Wave Phonons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15456)
*Geng Li,Yu-Yuan Chen,Yu-xi Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在压电表面声波（SAW）腔中，通过SAW声子实现对量子斯格明子的相干控制，并揭示其作为量子比特与单模声子耦合的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯格明子是信息存储单元的竞争性候选，并在量子信息处理方面具有广阔前景，因此需要探索对其量子态的相干操控手段。

Method: 对有限Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya簇进行精确对角化，获得具有稳健标量手性的量子斯格明子本征态；通过晶格-自旋对称性导出电磁跃迁的偏振依赖选择定则；利用压电效应感生的电场，推导量子斯格明子作为量子比特与单模量子化SAW之间的相互作用哈密顿量。

Result: 发现单声子水平下的耦合强度随斯格明子半径线性增加，从而对更大的斯格明子结构实现增强的自旋-声学耦合，并具有低磁耗散。

Conclusion: 少自旋量子斯格明子可作为混合量子信息处理的紧凑构建单元，并为实现更高密度集成的片上量子器件提供了一条途径。

Abstract: Skyrmions are competitive candidates for information-storage units and have great prospect in quantum information processing. We here study coherent control of a quantum skyrmion via surface acoustic wave (SAW) phonons in a piezoelectric SAW cavity. Exact diagonalization of a finite Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya cluster reveals the eigenstates of quantum skyrmion with robust scalar chirality. The underlying lattice-spin symmetry imposes polarization-dependent selection rules for the electromagnetic transitions between eigenstates states. Considering the small mode volume of the SAW phonon easy to reach strong coupling, we derive the interaction Hamiltonian between the quantum skyrmion as a qubit and a single-mode quantized SAW via the electric field induced by piezoelectric effect, and find that the coupling strength at the single-phonon level increases linearly with skyrmion radius. This enables enhanced spin-acoustic coupling for larger skyrmionic textures and for low magnetic dissipation. Our results establish few-spin quantum skyrmions as compact building blocks for hybrid quantum information processing and suggest a route toward more densely integrated on-chip quantum devices.

</details>


### [172] [Engineering dissipation and control pulses for high-fidelity fault-tolerance quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15477)
*Shao-Wei Xu,Zhe-Yuan Zhang,Yi-Tong Shi,Ye-Hong Chen,Yan Xia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出针对双光子耗散稳定的猫态量子比特的最优鲁棒控制协议，通过有效二能级描述与绝热捷径逆向工程实现快速高保真态转移，并给出强抑制驱动误差的最优鲁棒条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 猫态量子比特是面向硬件高效容错量子计算的重要玻色编码方案，但驱动场不完美引起的系统控制误差以及纯退相位导致的子空间泄漏会降低门保真度，因此需要兼顾快速性、鲁棒性与泄漏抑制的控制协议。

Method: 在猫态子空间推导有效二能级描述，并利用逆向工程应用绝热捷径技术设计控制协议；随后分析对系统控制误差的灵敏度以确定最优鲁棒条件，并考察耗散约束对纯退相位引起的泄漏的抑制效果。

Result: 所设计的协议实现了猫态量子比特中快速且高保真的态转移；识别出可强烈抑制驱动场不完美所诱导误差的最优鲁棒条件；并证明耗散约束能有效抑制纯退相位造成的猫态子空间外泄漏。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个兼具鲁棒性与泄漏抑制的高保真玻色量子比特控制框架，凸显了耗散猫态量子比特的内在优势，为可扩展容错量子计算提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Cat-state qubits, a prominent class of bosonic encodings, offer a promising pathway toward hardware-efficient fault-tolerant quantum computing. In this manuscript, we propose an optimally robust control protocol for the cat-state qubits which are stabilized by engineering two-photon dissipation. By deriving an effective two-level description in the cat-state subspace and applying shortcut-to-adiabaticity via inverse engineering, we design a robust protocol to achieve fast and high-fidelity state transfer in the cat-state qubit. We analyze the sensitivity to systematic control errors and identify an optimal robustness condition that strongly suppresses errors induced by imperfections in the driving fields. Furthermore, we show that dissipative confinement efficiently suppresses leakage out of the cat-state subspace caused by the pure dephasing, highlighting an intrinsic advantage of dissipative-cat qubits. This work establishes a robust and leakage-suppressing framework for high-fidelity bosonic qubit control, offering a promising route toward scalable fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>


### [173] [Bell-inequality violation in light transmitted through disordered emitter ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15575)
*Ruolin Guan,Vineesha Srivastava,Kasper J. Kusmierek,Ivan Vybornyi,Klemens Hammerer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 无序弱耦合双能级发射体集合中透射光可在连续波Franson型测量中违反Bell不等式，其Bell信号由稳态输出场的等时强度关联g^(2)(0)与相位敏感的 two-photon coherence R 决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无序、弱耦合的量子发射体集合是否能作为连续波条件下违反Bell不等式的量子资源，并区分其可能的物理机制（反聚束与聚束）。

Method: 对多体主方程采用四阶累积量展开，计算双向传播通过无序发射体集合的等时强度关联g^(2)(0)与相位敏感双光子相干R；并将其外推到代表性弱单发射体耦合β=0.01。

Result: Bell不等式违反出现在两个不同区域：等时符合被抑制的反聚束区，以及光子对保持相位相干的聚束区。外推数值表明，在β=0.01时，反聚束区在原子数N≈75–220可预测违反，聚束区在N≳250可预测违反。

Conclusion: 无序弱耦合双能级发射体集合的透射光可产生连续波Franson型Bell不等式违反，且存在反聚束与聚束两类机制；在弱耦合β=0.01下，违反对应特定原子数范围。

Abstract: We show that light transmitted through a disordered ensemble of weakly coupled two-level emitters can violate a Bell inequality in a continuous-wave Franson-type measurement. The Bell signal is determined by two steady-state output-field correlations, the equal-time intensity correlation $g^{(2)}(0)$ and the phase-sensitive two-photon coherence $R$. We compute these quantities for bidirectional propagation through disordered emitter ensembles using a fourth-order cumulant expansion of the many-body master equation. The resulting Bell inequality violation appears in two distinct regimes, an antibunched regime where equal-time coincidences are suppressed and a bunched regime where photon pairs remain phase coherent. Extrapolating the numerics to a representative weak single-emitter coupling $β=0.01$ predicts violation in the antibunched regime for atom numbers $N\simeq75$-$220$, and in the bunched regime for $N\gtrsim250$.

</details>


### [174] [Information Erasure and Quantum Imprint in Quantum Measurement and First-Order SPAM Error Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15586)
*Taiga Suzuki,Yuki Ito,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"信息擦除"与"量子印记"两个性质对量子仪器进行分类，揭示该分类的非平凡结构，并证明在足够可靠的后选择下，信息擦除可实现态制备误差与测量误差（SPAM）的一阶分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量过程（量子仪器）的分类缺乏刻画其状态依赖性的判据；同时，SPAM误差分离通常需要同时表征态制备与测量，难度较大，本文试图寻找一种新的分类视角并借此简化误差分离问题。

Method: 定义两个互补性质：信息擦除（存在合适的后选择使早期测量结果分布与初始量子态无关，同时保留所有结果分支）与量子印记（不存在任何可允许的后选择能消除这种状态依赖性）。在此基础上分析该分类的结构，并研究在后选择足够可靠时后验分布中态制备误差与测量误差的一阶贡献。

Result: 该分类具有非平凡结构；"提供更多初始态信息的测量更不易出现信息擦除"这一直觉在一般情况下并不成立。在足够可靠的后选择下，态制备误差的一阶贡献从后验分布中消失，而测量误差的可见一阶贡献仍然保留，从而实现SPAM误差的一阶分离。

Conclusion: 信息擦除为量子仪器提供了一种新的分类框架，并将SPAM误差分离问题从"同时表征态制备与测量"转化为"实现可靠后选择"的问题，为量子测量表征提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We introduce information erasure and quantum imprint as two properties that classify quantum instruments. Information erasure is the property that an appropriate postselection can render the distribution of earlier measurement outcomes independent of the initial quantum state while retaining all outcome branches. Quantum imprint is the complementary property that no admissible postselection can eliminate this state dependence. We show that this classification has a nontrivial structure and that the natural intuition that measurements providing more information about the initial quantum state should be less likely to exhibit information erasure does not hold in general. We further show that, under a sufficiently reliable postselection, information erasure enables first-order separation of state-preparation and measurement (SPAM) errors. Specifically, the first-order contribution of state-preparation error vanishes from the posterior distribution, whereas visible first-order contributions of measurement error remain. This result recasts SPAM error separation from the problem of simultaneously characterizing state preparation and measurement into the problem of realizing a reliable postselection.

</details>


### [175] [Parametric two-qubit gates via Landau-Zener interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15604)
*Simon Geisert,Albert Hertel,Soeren Ihssen,Zhongyi Jiang,Paul Kugler,Nicolas Zapata,Nicolas Gosling,Ameya Nambisan,Yuan Gao,Asier Galicia,Jéferson R. Guimarães,Yorgo Haddad,Marc Neis,Harsh Bhardwaj,Dmitriy A. Volkov,Juan Cereijo,Marcello Guardascione,Yebin Liu,Markus Jerger,Pavel Bushev,Frank Wilhelm-Mauch,Wolfgang Wernsdorfer,Shai Machnes,Mohammad Ansari,Rami Barends,Ioan M. Pop*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于连续Landau-Zener跃迁的量子干涉，提出并实现超导量子比特两比特门，兼具基带与参量控制优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文的动机是弥合基带控制与参量两比特控制之间的鸿沟，并克服色散耦合器在控制频率可调范围与门速度方面的局限。

Method: 利用连续Landau-Zener（LZ）跃迁之间的量子干涉构造两比特门机制，实现控制频率在原位连续区间内调谐（可达数百MHz）；并在两类平台上进行实验验证：耦合广义通量比特的模块化chiplet架构，以及单片transmon架构。

Result: 实验在模块化chiplet（耦合广义通量比特）和单片transmon两种平台上成功演示LZ门；控制频率可在高达数百MHz的连续区间内原位调谐，且门速度快，达到全耦合强度量级。

Conclusion: 该LZ门的可调谐性与高门速度相结合，使其成为复用控制脉冲及互连超导chiplet架构的独特工具。

Abstract: We propose and demonstrate gates between two superconducting qubits based on quantum interference of consecutive Landau-Zener (LZ) transitions. This gate mechanism bridges between baseband and parametric two-qubit control, enabling in situ tuning of the control frequency across a continuous interval up to hundreds of MHz. Another advantage compared to dispersive couplers is that the speed of the LZ gate is on the order of the full coupling strength. We experimentally demonstrate the gate on two platforms, a modular chiplet architecture of coupled generalized flux qubits, and on a monolithic transmon architecture. The combination of tunability and gate speed establishes the LZ gate as a unique tool for multiplexing control pulses and interconnecting superconducting chiplet architectures.

</details>


### [176] [Reducing Decoding Latency in Quantum Error Correction by Early Starting Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15612)
*Tommaso Peduzzi,Lukas Bödeker,Markus Müller,Luis Colmenarez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出Cluster-As-You-Go（CAYG）解码器，在稳定子测量过程中同步进行聚类与纠错，减少回积问题，实现实时早启动解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要快速低延迟解码，处理症状数据的延迟会导致回积问题（backlog problem）。现有解码器（包括可并行的Union-Find）必须等待一个纠错周期内所有稳定子测量结果收齐后才开始解码，固有地引入了解码起始延迟。

Method: CAYG是对Union-Find解码器的改进：在稳定子测量进行的同时处理症状信息，边测量边聚类、边纠错，而非等待全部症状收齐。该方法在周期结束时缩小剩余解码问题的规模，从而缩短完成解码所需时间。通过表面码仿真评估其速度-精度权衡特性。

Result: 提前启动聚类会带来一定程度的解码精度下降，但保持了可扩展性。表面码仿真显示，测量后空闲时间的减少可超过精度损失，从而获得更优的速度-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 实时、早启动的纠错周期内解码是可行的，且对量子纠错有利。CAYG虽在表面码上验证，但广泛适用于其他支持基于聚类解码的量子纠错码，并可扩展至专用实时解码硬件。

Abstract: In quantum error correction, fast low-latency decoding is essential for fault-tolerant quantum computation, as delays in processing syndrome data can lead to the backlog problem. Existing decoders, including parallelizable approaches such as Union-Find, begin decoding only after all stabilizer measurement outcomes from an error-correction cycle have been received, inherently introducing a delay before decoding begins. We introduce Cluster-As-You-Go (CAYG), a modification of the Union-Find decoder that processes syndrome information during stabilizer measurements by clustering and correcting errors as they appear. This approach reduces the size of the remaining decoding problem at the end of the quantum error correction cycle and, consequently, the time required to complete the decoding process. While this early start of clustering incurs a modest reduction in decoding accuracy, it preserves the decoder's scalability. Surface-code simulations show that the resulting reduction in post-measurement idling can outweigh the accuracy loss, yielding an improved speed-accuracy trade-off. These results demonstrate that real-time, early-starting decoding during QEC cycles is both feasible and can be advantageous for quantum error correction. Demonstrated here for the surface code, CAYG is broadly applicable to other quantum error-correcting codes, which allow for clustering-based decoding approaches, and is extensible to dedicated real-time decoding hardware.

</details>


### [177] [A Nonrecursive Lindblad Quantization of Dissipative Polynomial Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15632)
*Tingfei Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直接且非递归的构造方法，将任意平面多项式耗散流映射为GKSL形式的开放量子系统，其哈密顿量与坍缩算符按各阶齐次分量独立代数确定，并在半经典极限下重现经典动力学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望在经典耗散动力学与开放量子系统之间建立简单、系统的对应桥梁，以便既能分析给定量子模型，又能设计具有指定半经典动力学结构的开放量子系统。

Method: 对平面多项式耗散流 $\dot{\alpha}=h(\alpha,\alpha^*)$，按经典漂移的齐次分量逐阶、独立地代数构造GKSL中的哈密顿量与坍缩算符块；无需低阶项的递归抵消。在大振幅极限 $|\alpha|\sim S\to\infty$ 下，序化产生的低阶项被参数化压低，平均场闭合给出预设的 $O(S)$ 主导漂移。

Result: 对稳定不动点、Hopf分岔和双稳环流三个例子完成构造：Liouvillian谱分别重现经典弛豫指数、极限环的径向Floquet指数与中性相位方向，以及双稳情形下局域弛豫与环间切换之间的分离尺度。

Conclusion: 该构造提供从耗散经典动力学到显式开放量子系统实现的简单且系统化途径，可用于设计具有预设半经典动力学结构的开放量子系统；但作者不主张这是经典流的唯一微观量子化。

Abstract: We present a direct and nonrecursive construction that maps an arbitrary planar polynomial dissipative flow $\dotα=h(α,α^*)$ to an open quantum system in Gorini--Kossakowski--Sudarshan--Lindblad form. Given the homogeneous components of the classical drift, the corresponding Hamiltonian and collapse-operator blocks are obtained algebraically and independently at each degree. No recursive cancellation of lower-order terms is required: in the large-amplitude limit $|α|\sim S\to\infty$, ordering-generated lower-degree terms are parametrically suppressed, while mean-field closure yields the prescribed leading $O(S)$ drift for semiclassically localized states. This provides a simple and systematic route from dissipative classical dynamics to explicit open-quantum-system realizations, without claiming a unique microscopic quantization of the classical flow. We demonstrate the construction for stable fixed points, a Hopf bifurcation, and a bistable-ring flow. The corresponding Liouvillian spectra recover the classical relaxation exponents, the radial Floquet exponent and neutral phase direction of a limit cycle, and the separation between local relaxation and inter-ring switching in the bistable case. These examples show that the construction can be used not only to analyze a given quantum model, but also to design open quantum systems with prescribed classical dynamical structures in the semiclassical limit.

</details>


### [178] [Efficient quantum state tomography with two complementary projective measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15662)
*Xiang Li,Yong Wang,Lijun Liu,Yiguang Hong,Qing Gao,Shuming Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于Kirkwood-Dirac准概率的量子态层析协议，仅需两组互补秩一投影测量，并用复数逻辑回归估计器与投影梯度算法实现高效、可扩展的量子态重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态层析是刻画量子系统的基础任务，但测量与计算成本随量子比特数指数增长，严重制约其实际应用，因此亟需降低测量开销与计算复杂度的可扩展层析方案。

Method: 利用Kirkwood-Dirac（KD）准概率进行量子态重构：仅采用两组互补的秩一投影测量以降低测量成本；提出复数逻辑回归估计器处理KD数据，并结合投影梯度算法缓解数值不稳定并加速收敛；进一步利用KD准概率的乘积算子结构降低计算成本。

Result: 大量实验验证了协议的有效性；在GPU实现下，随机生成的15量子比特混合态实例可在20分钟内完成全重构。

Conclusion: 该工作为可扩展的量子态层析及大规模量子系统基准测试提供了一条有前景的途径。

Abstract: Quantum state tomography (QST) is of fundamental importance to characterize quantum systems in quantum information processing, but its practical implementation is severely hindered by the exponential scaling of measurement and computational costs. In this paper, we present a novel QST protocol that utilizes Kirkwood-Dirac (KD) quasiprobability to reconstruct quantum states. First, it enables state reconstruction with only two complementary rank-one projective measurements, thus significantly reducing the measurement cost. Then, a complex logistic regression estimator is proposed to process collected KD data, together with a projected gradient algorithm to mitigate numerical instability and to accelerate convergence. The product-operator structure of KD quasiprobability is further exploited to reduce the computational cost. Finally, extensive experiments are implemented to confirm the validity of our protocol. Notably, the full reconstruction of randomly generated 15-qubit mixed-state instances can be accomplished within 20 minutes under the GPU implementation. These results suggest a promising route toward scalable QST and benchmarking large-scale quantum systems.

</details>


### [179] [Distribution of light-matter quantum correlations with a temporally multiplexed solid-state quantum memory array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15689)
*Aya Mneimneh,Susana Plascencia,Manuel Gundin,Jonathan Hänni,Samuele Grandi,Markus Teller,Hugues de Riedmatten*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道基于Pr³⁺:Y₂SiO₅晶体的时间复用固态量子存储器阵列，实现了电信波段光子与最多60个时空模式的量子关联，并在巴塞罗那城域39.1 km光纤上验证了复用对纠缠分发速率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长距离量子中继架构中，纠缠分发速率受限于通信往返时间与单模存储容量。通过多维复用（空间与时间）可提升与量子存储器关联的光子探测速率，从而支撑高速率远程纠缠分发。

Method: 采用Pr³⁺:Y₂SiO₅晶体，使用完整原子频率梳（AFC）协议并支持按需读出；将电信波段预报单光子依次存储于最多10个存储单元；结合空间与时间复用，构建最多60个时空模式的量子存储器阵列。随后将预报用电信光子经巴塞罗那城域已部署的39.1 km光纤传输，并在往返时间受限的realistic场景下评估性能。

Result: 实现了电信光子与最多60个时空模式的量子关联；在受限场景中，393 μs通信往返时间中最多15 %被通信试验填充；相比单模存储器，与量子存储器阵列关联的电信光子探测速率提升60倍。

Conclusion: 随着存储时间与效率的提升，该复用量子存储器阵列有望成为长距离量子网络的骨干，实现高速率远程纠缠分发。

Abstract: Multiplexed quantum memories increase the entanglement distribution rate in long-distance quantum repeater architectures by harnessing storage in several degrees of freedom. Here, we report on the distribution of light-matter quantum correlations using an array of time-multiplexed solid-state quantum memories. We store telecom-heralded single photons sequentially in up to ten memory cells using the full atomic frequency comb protocol with on-demand read-out in a Pr$^{3+}$:Y$_2$SiO$_5$ crystal. Leveraging both spatial and temporal multiplexing, we demonstrate quantum correlations between the telecom photon and up to 60 spatio-temporal modes of the quantum memory array. We then transmit the heralding telecom photon over 39.1 km of deployed optical fiber in the Metropolitan Area of Barcelona. In a realistic scenario where the generation rate is limited by the two-way communication time, we show that up to 15 % of the $393 μs$ round-trip communication time is filled with communication trials, leading to a 60-fold enhancement in the rate of detected telecom photons correlated with the quantum memory array, compared to a single-mode memory. With increased storage times and efficiencies, our multiplexed quantum memory array will constitute the backbone of a long-distance quantum network, establishing remote entanglement at high rates.

</details>


### [180] [OpenQARP: a modular framework for quantum application research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15697)
*Stefano Scali,Vicente P. Soloviev,Antonio Márquez Romero,Brian Coyle,Giuseppe Buonaiuto,Annie Paine,Jonathan H. Fetherolf,Marcos Diez García,Michal Krompiec,Josh Kirsopp*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: OpenQARP 是一个基于 C++ 内核、开源 Python 量子应用研究框架，采用 blocks/primitives/engines 三层可替换架构，覆盖近中期至容错算法，并在多基准测试中取得显著性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子软件生态中，算法库、原语栈与执行后端往往彼此割裂，缺乏统一数值约定与可替换分层，且性能与正确性验证（独立 oracle、计时伴随校验）不充分，导致应用研究在电子结构到组合优化等任务上开发与验证成本高。

Method: 提出 OpenQARP：开源 Python 框架 + 编译型 C++ 核心。应用由算法库编排三层可互换组件——blocks 描述电路、primitives 描述提取内容、engines 描述运行方式。功能覆盖近中期至容错方法，支持电子结构与组合优化等，集成 device-aware compilation、circuit cutting、resource estimation，并统一数值约定；每个数值特性对照独立 oracle 检查，每次公布计时均附带正确性校验。与 OpenFermion、Qiskit/Aer、PennyLane/Lightning、Qulacs、qsim、pytket 对比。

Result: 在算子代数上比对比框架快 1–2 个数量级；模拟速度处于最快之列；在两比特门数量上与成熟编译器持平；表达完整算法所需代码量接近框架级（而非原语栈所需的繁琐管线）。

Conclusion: OpenQARP 通过统一分层设计、C++ 内核与严格验证，在性能与开发效率上同时优于现有主流量子软件栈，适合作为量子应用研究的统一开源平台。

Abstract: We introduce OpenQARP, the Open Quantum Application Research Package: an open-source Python framework for quantum application research, built on a compiled C++ core. In OpenQARP, an application is assembled from an algorithm library that orchestrates three interchangeable layers: blocks describe circuits, primitives describe what to extract from them, and engines describe how they run. The release spans near-term through fault-tolerant methods, targeting problems from electronic structure to combinatorial optimization, with device-aware compilation, circuit cutting, and resource estimation over a single set of numerical conventions that the code must follow. Every numerical feature is checked against an independent oracle, and every published timing carries a correctness check. Against OpenFermion, Qiskit/Aer, PennyLane/Lightning, Qulacs, qsim, and pytket, OpenQARP is one to two orders of magnitude faster on operator algebra and among the fastest on simulation. It matches mature compilers on two-qubit gate count, and expresses a complete algorithm in the lines a framework needs rather than the plumbing a primitive stack demands.

</details>


### [181] [Performance in Symmetry-Restricted Searches for Post-Quantum Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15705)
*Marek Gazdzicki,Francesco Giacosa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"流行率"与"投影接受度"两个指标，其乘积定义搜索性能，用以量化后量子关联搜索中关联丰度与表示区分度的作用；在具有交换对称性的二结果Bell场景下比较CHSH与金字塔表示，发现同步量子关联与仅满足交换对称性的关联存在本质区别，导致投影搜索效果发生定性变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索后量子关联依赖两个独立因素：关联在关联空间中的丰度，以及所选表示对关联与量子关联区分度的保持效率。此前缺乏统一框架来量化并比较这两者对搜索性能的影响。

Method: 引入流行率（popularity rate）与投影接受度（projection acceptance）两个量，以二者乘积定义搜索性能。在具有对称性相关位点的二结果Bell场景中，使用CHSH表示与金字塔（pyramid）表示进行说明，并重点区分交换对称的同步关联与仅满足交换对称性的关联。

Result: 在同步量子区域，共向量表示由同步性自动给出；而仅施加交换对称性时，所定义的关联空间显著更大。该区别可定性改变投影搜索的有效性：金字塔表示在同步区域保留较强的区分能力，但在仅施加交换对称性时完全丧失区分能力。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个用于评估与比较后量子关联搜索的简洁框架。

Abstract: Searches for post-quantum correlations depend on two distinct ingredients: how abundant such correlations are in the underlying correlation space and how efficiently the chosen representation preserves their distinguishability from quantum correlations. We quantify these effects by introducing the popularity rate and the projection acceptance, whose product defines the search performance. We illustrate these concepts in binary-outcome Bell scenarios with symmetry-related sites, using the CHSH and pyramid representations. Particular attention is paid to the distinction between exchange-symmetric synchronous correlations and correlations satisfying only exchange symmetry. In the synchronous quantum sector, the common-vector representation follows automatically from synchrony, whereas exchange symmetry alone defines a substantially larger correlation space. We show that this distinction can qualitatively change the effectiveness of a projected search: the pyramid representation, which retains substantial discriminatory power in the synchronous sector, has no discriminatory power when only exchange symmetry is imposed. Our results provide a simple framework for assessing and comparing searches for post-quantum correlations.

</details>


### [182] [A Modular, Topology-Aware Software Stack for Entanglement-Based Distributed Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15728)
*Luke Andreesen,Shobhit Gupta,Sean Sullivan,Manish Kumar Singh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个开源、拓扑感知的分布式量子程序编译与调度框架，支持在确定性与随机纠缠生成模型下进行分块、重构与调度，并以OpenQASM互通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算需跨量子网络、编译与调度领域进行软硬件协同设计，但现有工具在单体电路编译器与长距离量子网络模拟器之间彼此割裂，缺少统一支持分布式程序编译调度的工具。

Method: 该框架以输入电路和目标网络描述为输入，将电路划分并重构为满足QPU间与QPU内拓扑约束的分布式程序；通过门传送与态传送覆盖跨QPU操作；在确定性或随机纠缠生成模型下对结果进行调度；采用标准OpenQASM作为输入输出以兼容现有单体工具链，并通过标准化模块接口支持划分与调度策略的替换与基准测试。

Result: 实验表明，最优编译策略随电路与网络配置而变化；网络拓扑与QPU内连通性均显著影响执行的纠缠开销。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了分布式量子计算中协同设计方法的必要性，而该框架正是为支持此类协同设计而构建。

Abstract: Distributed quantum computing (DQC) seeks to scale beyond the limits of monolithic processors by interconnecting multiple quantum processing units (QPUs) through entanglement-based links. Realizing this vision requires the co-design of hardware and software across the domains of quantum networking, compilation, and scheduling, yet existing tools remain fragmented between monolithic circuit compilers and long-distance quantum network simulators. We present an open-source, topology-informed framework for the compilation and scheduling of distributed quantum programs. Given an input circuit and a description of the target network, the framework partitions and reconstructs the circuit into a distributed program that respects the specified inter- and intra-QPU topology, covers cross-QPU operations through gate and state teleportation, and schedules the result under either deterministic or stochastic entanglement-generation models. By accepting and emitting standard OpenQASM, the framework interoperates with existing monolithic toolchains, and its standardized module interfaces allow partitioning and scheduling strategies to be interchanged and benchmarked. Using this framework, we show that the best-performing compilation strategy varies across the tested circuits and network configurations, and that both network topology and intra-QPU connectivity substantially affect the entanglement cost of execution. These findings underscore the need for a co-design approach to distributed quantum computing, which our framework is designed to support.

</details>


### [183] [Disentangling QAOA: From Weakly Entangled Circuits to a Classical QUBO Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15738)
*Boris I. Bantysh,Andrey Yu. Chernyavskiy,Denis A. Kulikov,Maksim A. Gavreev,Evgeniy O. Kiktenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在固定参数扩展深度的QAOA体系中，纠缠呈现先增后减并饱和的键维数曲线，据此提出投影到乘积态流形的经典求解器BOND-1，在GSet MaxCut上取得>0.95的切割比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优化中纠缠的作用仍存争议。作者希望在无法进行完整态矢量模拟的大规模、深线路场景下，定量考察纠缠的演化，并检验在固定参数扩展深度这一特定体系中，纠缠到底是否为优化能力所必需。

Method: 聚焦QAOA的固定参数扩展深度体系：一个双参数调度在小实例上训练一次，随后在问题规模与线路深度同时增大时直接沿用。用近似矩阵乘积态模拟最多50量子比特、100层线路，以键维数度量纠缠。基于观测到的纠缠—退纠缠曲线（峰值键维数随深度下降并饱和），做极端近似——每经过一个双量子比特门后把态投影到乘积态流形（键维数为1），由此构造量子启发的经典求解器BOND-1。

Result: BOND-1在标准GSet MaxCut基准（最多20000个变量）上相对已知最优值取得>0.95的切割比，部分实例甚至持平最优值；且无需逐实例优化，内存开销为线性，逐实例调参可进一步提升。

Conclusion: 在该固定参数扩展深度体系中，即使完全去除纠缠，QAOA仍有相当大比例的优化能力得以保留。但该结论仅适用于这一特定设定，并不意味着一般意义上的量子优化不需要纠缠。

Abstract: The role of entanglement in quantum optimization remains actively debated. To address this question, we focus on the fixed-parameter expanding-depth regime of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA), where a compact two-parameter schedule is trained once on small instances and then applied as the problem size and circuit depth increase. To probe this regime beyond full state-vector simulation, we perform approximate matrix product state simulations for up to 50 qubits and 100 layers and quantify entanglement by the bond dimension. We observe an entangle--disentangle profile, with the peak bond dimension decreasing with depth and eventually saturating. This observation motivates an extreme approximation: projecting the state onto the product-state manifold (bond dimension one) after every two-qubit interaction. Based on this approximation, we introduce BOND-1, a quantum-inspired classical solver. Despite the drastic simplification, BOND-1 achieves cut ratios above 0.95 relative to the best known values on standard GSet MaxCut benchmarks with up to 20000 variables, and in some cases it matches those values. It achieves these results without per-instance optimization and has linear memory cost, while per-instance tuning can provide further improvement. These results show that, in this regime, a substantial fraction of the optimization power of QAOA survives even in the complete absence of entanglement. Our conclusions, however, are specific to this setting and do not imply that entanglement is unnecessary for quantum optimization in general.

</details>


### [184] [Pauli spectrum and nonstabilizerness of random fermionic Gaussian states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15739)
*Xhek Turkeshi,Piotr Sierant,Poetri Sonya Tarabunga*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文刻画随机费米子高斯态的"魔力"（非稳定子性），通过Pauli谱与稳定子熵进行精确分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子魔力（magic）是通用量子计算的关键资源，但随机费米子高斯态的魔力结构此前缺乏精确刻画，尤其是其Pauli谱与稳定子熵的解析行为及可认证性尚不清楚。

Method: 对Majorana高斯态的Haar系综，推导平均稳定子纯度的有限N闭式表达，并精确重构Pauli谱（按Majorana权重分解的beta分布乘积混合）；对固定填充数的Haar随机高斯态，将正整数q处的平均稳定子纯度表示为SU(2q)上的系数积分，并精确计算q=2情形；建立冻结密度与Majorana系综、半填充粒子数守恒系综及相应SYK_2基态的关系；分析丢模后的认证魔力保留。

Result: 稳定子熵及其滤波版本在Rényi指标q_c=2处冻结：q>2时魔力密度为1/(q-1)，由稀有的权重为2的Majorana弦主导。该区域需要指数多特征样本，直接采样估计稳定子熵效率低下。冻结密度刻画了Majorana系综与半填充粒子数守恒系综的典型态。典型费米子高斯态在丢弃任意固定比例κ<κ_*^G≈0.7613的模式后仍保持认证魔力，甚至超过全Hilbert空间中Haar随机态的2/3阈值。

Conclusion: 随机费米子高斯态的魔力在q>2时被冻结，其密度由稀有低权重Majorana弦控制，导致采样估计在冷冻区失效；冻结密度是Majorana与半填充系综典型态的普遍特征，并与SYK_2基态相关联。典型费米子高斯态在大量丢模后仍保留认证魔力，且阈值超过2/3，显示了费米子高斯态魔力的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We characterize the magic of random fermionic Gaussian states through their Pauli spectrum and their stabilizer entropies. For the Haar ensemble of Majorana Gaussian states we derive closed finite-$N$ expressions for the average stabilizer purities and reconstruct the Pauli spectrum exactly, as a mixture of products of beta-distributed variables resolved by Majorana weight. The stabilizer entropies, and their filtered versions, freeze at Rényi index $q_c=2$: for $q>2$ the magic density is $1/(q-1)$, controlled by rare weight-two Majorana strings. Resolving these contributions requires exponentially many characteristic samples, making direct sampling estimates of the stabilizer entropies inefficient in this frozen regime. For Haar-random Gaussian states at fixed filling, we express the averaged stabilizer purities at positive integer $q$ as coefficient integrals over $\mathrm{SU}(2q)$, with dimension independent of $N$, and evaluate the $q=2$ case exactly. We establish that the frozen density describes typical states in the Majorana and half-filled number-conserving ensembles and relate these ensembles to the corresponding $\mathrm{SYK}_2$ ground states. Finally, we show that typical fermionic Gaussian states retain certified magic after discarding any fixed fraction $κ<κ_\star^{\rm G}\simeq0.7613$ of their modes in the thermodynamic limit, remaining magical even beyond the $2/3$ threshold for Haar-random states in the full Hilbert space.

</details>


### [185] [Optimal entanglement-assisted source coding under a balanced-difference promise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15757)
*Julius A. Zeiss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一种带平衡差承诺的零误差源编码任务，证明在共享纠缠与单条经典消息下，所需消息数由 $(q-1)\ell$ 的奇偶性决定：偶数时为 $n$，奇数时为 2，从而刻画量子色数并形式化验证证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠可降低通信成本，但确定任务的最小可达代价是理解其极限的关键。该任务中 Alice 不知候选对而需让 Bob 无差错识别其词，需明确其最优通信复杂度。

Method: 结合傅里叶分析与组合计数论证，确定相关图的最小特征值；对偶数情形解决 Cao 等人猜想 6.3 的谱断言，并构造奇数情形显式二部划分；所有引理定理在 Lean 中形式化验证。

Result: 对任意 $q\geq2,\ell\geq1$，当 $(q-1)\ell$ 为偶数任务需 $n$ 条消息，为奇数需 2 条；偶数情形达到已有纠缠辅助协议最优性，奇数情形给出无纠缠确定性协议达到一比特最优。

Conclusion: 由此确定平衡循环广义 Hadamard 图的量子色数：偶数情形为 $n$，奇数情形为 2（经典色数亦为 2），并完成 Lean 形式化验证。

Abstract: Entanglement can reduce the communication required for coding tasks, but establishing the minimum achievable cost is essential to understanding its limits. We address this question in a zero-error source-coding task where Alice receives a word and Bob knows an unordered pair of candidates containing it. Alice does not know the pair and must enable Bob to identify her word without error using shared entanglement and one classical message. The candidates satisfy a balanced-difference promise: for words in $\mathbb{Z}_q^n$ with $n=q\ell$, each residue modulo $q$ occurs exactly $\ell$ times in their coordinatewise difference. For all integers $q\geq2$ and $\ell\geq1$, we prove that the task requires exactly $n$ messages when $(q-1)\ell$ is even and two messages when it is odd. These minima allow arbitrary finite-dimensional shared states independent of the inputs and arbitrary local measurements. In even parity, this establishes optimality of an existing entanglement-assisted protocol. In odd parity, an explicit deterministic protocol achieves the optimum of one bit without entanglement. Our proof combines Fourier analysis with a combinatorial counting argument to determine the smallest eigenvalue of the associated graphs. In even parity, this resolves the spectral assertion of Cao et al.'s Conjecture 6.3 for balanced cyclic generalized Hadamard graphs. Together with an explicit odd-parity bipartition, this determines the quantum chromatic number as $n$ in even parity and $2$ in odd parity, where the classical chromatic number is also $2$. All lemmas, theorems, and corollaries are formalized and verified in Lean.

</details>


### [186] [Low-Depth Initial-State Preparation for Ground-State Energy Estimation of Two-Dimensional Strongly Correlated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15764)
*Ryo Watanabe,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作通过先制备近似海森堡基态、再施加源自Schrieffer-Wolff生成元的电荷涨落门，为二维半填充Hubbard模型设计了浅层量子电路，实现低成本基态能量估计的初态制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子基态能量估计算法要求初态与基态具有不可忽略的保真度。对于强关联体系（如二维半填充Hubbard模型），如何以低代价制备高质量初态是核心难题，且大晶格超出经典模拟能力，需要可迁移的电路设计方法。

Method: 基于经典模拟设计浅层电路：(1)先制备近似海森堡基态；(2)施加由Schrieffer-Wolff（SW）生成元直接导出的电荷涨落门。验证海森堡模型参数从4×4晶格向10×10晶格的可迁移性（无需再优化）。利用张量网络模拟、变分蒙特卡洛参考态以及基态能量与单重态能隙估计来给出保真度下界，并用精确4×4计算检验SW门效果。

Result: 海森堡参数可从4×4成功迁移至最大10×10晶格；获得了有前景的基态保真度下界；精确4×4计算表明SW门显著提升嵌入海森堡态对应的Hubbard基态保真度。对10×10晶格，含Clifford+T合成的制备代价约10^5个T门，处于megaquop量级。

Conclusion: 海森堡参数的可迁移性支持用小晶格结果设计超出经典模拟能力的大晶格Hubbard浅层电路；该方案为强关联系统提供了一条低成本的初态制备路径。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms for ground-state energy estimation require initial states with non-negligible fidelity to the ground state. Guided by classical simulations, we design shallow circuits for the two-dimensional half-filled Hubbard model by first preparing an approximate Heisenberg ground state and then applying charge-fluctuation gates derived directly from the Schrieffer-Wolff (SW) generator. For the Heisenberg model, we demonstrate effective parameter transfer from a $4\times4$ lattice to lattices up to $10\times10$ without further optimization. We obtain promising lower bounds on the ground-state fidelity using tensor-network simulations, variational Monte Carlo reference states, and estimates of the ground-state energy and singlet gap. Exact $4\times4$ calculations show that the SW gates substantially improve the Hubbard ground-state fidelity of the embedded Heisenberg state. The successful Heisenberg parameter transfer supports the use of these small-lattice results to design shallow Hubbard circuits on larger lattices beyond the reach of classical simulations. For a $10\times10$ lattice, the estimated preparation cost is on the order of $10^5$ $T$ gates, including Clifford+$T$ synthesis, which is well within the megaquop regime. These results offer a route to low-cost initial-state preparation for ground-starongly correlated systems.

</details>


### [187] [Security framework for practical quantum key distribution with imperfect devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15790)
*Jerome Wiesemann,John Burniston,Devashish Tupkary,Norbert Lütkenhaus*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用边际约束熵累积定理(MEAT)，为存在不完美且表征不确定的源与探测器的通用制备-测量型QKD协议（含诱骗态BB84）给出安全证明，实现可实用密钥率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际QKD系统在源与探测器上存在不可避免的缺陷，且由于表征不确定性、参数漂移及潜在攻击者影响，这些不完美器件的实际行为无法被精确已知。现有证明多针对特定协议，缺乏统一且可扩展的框架。

Method: 采用边际约束熵累积定理(MEAT)进行安全性分析；引入独立源映射(source maps)与压缩映射(squashing maps)的系列证明技术，构建模块化框架，统一处理源与探测器的不完美性及表征不确定性。

Result: 在该框架下可对诱骗态BB84等协议给出安全证明；即使多种不完美性叠加，仍能获得实用的密钥率。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了统一基础，避免针对特定协议的专门论证，并可方便地推广到其他协议与器件缺陷场景。

Abstract: Practical quantum key distribution (QKD) systems inevitably exhibit imperfections in both the source and detector. At the same time, the behavior of these imperfect devices is never exactly known due to characterization uncertainty, parameter fluctuations, and potential influence by an adversary. In this work, we present a security proof for generic prepare-and-measure QKD protocols, including decoy-state BB84, with imperfect and imperfectly characterized sources and detectors using the marginal-constrained entropy accumulation theorem (MEAT). Our approach uses a sequence of proof technique independent source maps and squashing maps, yielding a very modular framework. We show that practical key rates can be achieved even when multiple imperfections are combined. More broadly, our work provides a unified foundation that avoids the need for dedicated protocol-specific arguments and can be readily extended to other protocols and device imperfections.

</details>


### [188] [Higher-order quantum thermodynamics: equilibrium and causal structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15829)
*Simon Milz,Kyrylo Simonov,Zoltán Zimborás,Tamal Guha,Saptarshi Roy,Giulio Chiribella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将平衡态保持视为Gibbs态在higher-order量子变换上的自然推广，证明在完全平衡保持（即任意辅助扩展下）要求下，所有变换区分的差异消失，且所有满足该条件的变换都是因果有序的，从而揭示因果序是热力学平衡的涌现结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子热力学以平衡态及态变换为研究对象，而higher-order量子变换（超越态与信道的物理情景，为因果序提供系统框架）的最新进展引出一个问题：在这一更一般的框架下，平衡应如何定义与保持。论文旨在回答该问题，并建立热力学平衡与因果结构之间的根本联系。

Method: 以Gibbs态作为唯一平衡态出发，将"平衡保持"定义为它的higher-order extension；分析不同平衡保持类别所对应的变换类；进一步施加"完全平衡保持"条件，即在任意ancillary extension下均要求保持不变；在任意higher-order map的一般性层面证明该条件下的结构性结论，并据此为量子信道引入类自由能量（free-energy-like）的量。

Result: 平衡保持原则一般会产生彼此不同的变换类；但当要求完全平衡保持（任意辅助扩展下）时，这些区分全部消失。关键结论是：每一个满足完全平衡保持的变换都必然是因果有序的。该结果对任意higher-order map均成立，并由此得到量子信道上的类自由能量量。

Conclusion: 热力学平衡与因果结构之间存在根本联系，因果序可作为热力学平衡的涌现性结果；该框架为构建完整的higher-order量子热力学理论奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quantum thermodynamics is traditionally formulated as a theory of equilibrium states and state transformations. Recent advances in higher-order quantum transformations, which describe physical scenarios beyond states and channels and provide a systematic framework for causal order, raise the question of how equilibrium should be defined and preserved in this more general setting. Starting from the Gibbs state as the unique equilibrium state, we identify equilibrium preservation as its natural higher-order extension. We show that, while this principle can in general give rise to distinct classes of transformations, all such distinctions disappear when equilibrium preservation is required completely, namely under arbitrary ancillary extensions. Remarkably, every transformation satisfying this condition is causally ordered, making causal order an emergent consequence of thermodynamic equilibrium. We establish this result for arbitrary higher-order maps and use the resulting framework to introduce free-energy-like quantities for quantum channels. Our findings reveal a fundamental connection between thermodynamic equilibrium and causal structure, providing a foundation for a fully fledged theory of higher-order quantum thermodynamics.

</details>


### [189] [Instantiating Microcrypt: Obstacles and opportunities via tailored state certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15842)
*Jose Carrasco,Jens Eisert,Soumik Ghosh,Dominik Hangleiter,Nicky Kai Hong Li,Ryan Sweke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证伪了"HPS假设可在单向函数不存在时为真"的猜想，证明HPS假设实际上蕴含单向函数存在，并给出从单向态生成器构造单向谜题的一般性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作提出哈密顿相位态（HPS）假设，认为其可实例化伪随机与单向态生成器，并猜测即使单向函数不存在该假设也可能为真；若成立，则HPS可通往Microcrypt的实例化。本文旨在检验并证伪这一猜想。

Method: 提出从单向态生成器构造单向谜题的方法，结合定制的"先测量、后询问"态认证协议，推广了此前基于经典影子（classical shadows）的构造，将单向谜题性质与所用态认证协议性质相关联：若认证协议支持高效经典后处理，则得到可高效验证的单向谜题；若认证协议在某种意义下可被高效经典模拟，则得到经典单向谜题，从而蕴含单向函数。随后利用相位态度认证协议的性质证明HPS假设蕴含单向函数。

Result: 证明若HPS假设成立，则单向函数存在，从而证伪了"HPS假设在单向函数不存在时仍可为真"的猜想。同时给出构造可高效验证单向谜题的新工具箱。

Conclusion: HPS假设无法用于实例化真正的Microcrypt密码学，但可作为构造经典密码学的新的内在量子假设；所提出的框架将单向态生成器与态认证协议性质相联系，为构造可高效验证的单向谜题提供了通用工具。

Abstract: Recent work has introduced the Hamiltonian phase state (HPS) assumptions, which postulate that Hamiltonian phase states can be used to instantiate pseudorandom and one-way state generators. Additionally, it has been conjectured that these assumptions can be true, even if one-way functions do not exist. This is exciting, because if true, then the HPS assumptions provide a route to the instantiation of Microcrypt. In this work we falsify this conjecture, by proving that if the HPS assumptions are true, then one-way functions exist. While this removes the possibility of instantiating genuine Microcrypt cryptography with Hamiltonian phase states, it shows that the HPS assumptions provide novel inherently quantum assumptions for the construction of classical cryptography. Technically we achieve this via a method for the construction of one-way puzzles from one-way state generators and tailored "measure first, ask later" state certification protocols. This generalizes prior constructions of one-way puzzles from one-way state generators via classical shadows and allows us to relate properties of the one-way puzzle to properties of the state certification protocol used in the construction. Specifically, if the state certification protocol admits efficient classical post-processing then one obtains an efficiently verifiable one-way puzzle, and if the state certification protocol can be efficiently classically simulated in a certain sense, then one obtains a classical one-way puzzle, which implies one-way functions. The latter observation allows us to prove that the HPS assumptions imply one-way functions, by exploiting properties of state certification protocols for phase states. The former observation provides a new toolbox for the construction of efficiently verifiable one-way puzzles by exploiting tailored state certification protocols for pseudorandom and one-way state generators.

</details>


### [190] [Limits on the atomic description of four-wave mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15843)
*Irvin F. Ángeles-Aguillón,Nieves Arias-Téllez,Pablo Yanes-Thomas,Alejandro Kunold,Daniel Sahagún-Sánchez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过冷原子系综中金刚石构型四波混频实验，检验单原子模型的适用边界，发现双光子符合计数需引入集体效应才能完整解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验描述原子系综四波混频产生光子对的成熟单原子模型之适用极限，明确单原子内部能级动力学能否完整解释偏振分辨的关联测量结果。

Method: 以冷原子为源，经偏振分析器将发射光分解为实验室坐标下的水平与垂直分量；抽运第一、第二激发跃迁的激光分别取水平与垂直偏振。采用包含相关超精细态全部塞曼子能级的综合模型直接计算一阶、二阶关联函数，预测各偏振通道的单光子计数与符合率。比较磁光阱有无再抽运激光时的布居动力学，据此在模型中额外纳入两个超精细能级及其塞曼子能级。用所得密度矩阵计算远场电场算符的期望值，并在不同抽运功率下与实验比较。

Result: 在大部分抽运功率范围内，模型对光子计数的预测与测量结果高度一致；符合测量中，当第一（第二）衰减产生的光子偏振平行于第一（第二）抽运光偏振时吻合良好，而在相反偏振配置下模型持续低估实验符合率。

Conclusion: 单原子内部能级动力学不足以完整解释原子系综四波混频产生的光子符合，尤其是交叉偏振配置；需引入超出单原子范畴的效应，尤其是集体现象。

Abstract: The limits of the well-established single-atom model for describing photon-pair generation via four-wave mixing in a diamond configuration in atomic ensembles are experimentally tested. Using a cold-atom source, biphotons are generated and detected through polarization analyzers that resolve the emitted light into horizontal and vertical components in the laboratory frame. The pump lasers driving the first and second excitation transitions are horizontally and vertically polarized, respectively. To predict single-photon counts and coincidence rates in all polarization channels, the first- and second-order correlation functions are calculated directly using a comprehensive model that accounts for all Zeeman sublevels of the relevant hyperfine states. Furthermore, the population dynamics with and without the re-pump laser of the magneto-optical trap are compared, revealing substantial differences in the populations of the Zeeman sublevels. This motivates the inclusion of two additional hyperfine levels and their corresponding Zeeman sublevels in the final model. The resulting density matrix is used to calculate expectation values of the far-field electric-field operators and compare them with experimental measurements over a range of pump-laser powers. Excellent agreement with the measured photon counts is obtained over most of this range. For the coincidence measurements, good agreement is found when the polarization of the photon generated by the first (second) decay is parallel to that of the first (second) pump beam. In contrast, the model consistently underestimates the experimental coincidence rates for the opposite polarization configuration. These findings indicate that effects beyond the internal level dynamics of individual atoms, most notably collective phenomena, are required to fully account for photon coincidences generated by four-wave mixing in atomic ensembles.

</details>


### [191] [Generation of entanglement and magic via continuous homodyne monitoring of a qubit pair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15868)
*Debmalya Das,Giuseppe Magnifico,Maria Maffei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过碰撞模型推导双量子比特与一维电磁场耦合、左右输出通道零差探测下的随机主方程（SME），揭示连续零差监测这一机制可生成纠缠并增强量子魔幻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续弱测量在量子基础与应用中具有重要价值。本文关注连续监测是否能在无条件动力学原本不产生资源的情况下，作为一种可调控机制生成量子资源（纠缠与量子魔幻）。

Method: 将两个量子比特在不同位置耦合到共同的一维电磁场，在右传与左传输出通道同时进行零差探测；采用碰撞模型描述，推导支配扩散型量子轨迹的解析随机主方程（SME），其中包含两测量通道间的干涉。通过对比量子轨迹上非线性量（如熵）的平均值与无条件态对应量，分析资源生成。

Result: 连续监测可在无条件动力学中不存在的纠缠上生成纠缠，并在衰减过程中增强量子比特对的量子魔幻；两种资源均可通过量子比特间积累的光学相位以及零差本振相位进行调节。

Conclusion: 对多个发射体的连续零差监测是一种可调控的量子资源生成机制。

Abstract: Continuous weak measurements of quantum systems are of great relevance in quantum foundations and applications. They can be achieved by probing the quantum system of interest by repeatedly measuring an auxiliary system weakly coupled to it. Here we study two qubits coupled in different points to a common one-dimensional electromagnetic field and simultaneously monitored in the right- and left-propagating output channels by homodyne detection. Using a collision-model description, we derive an analytical Stochastic Master Equation (SME) governing the resulting diffusive quantum trajectories, including the interference between the two measurement channels. For nonlinear functions of the quantum state, such as entropies, averages over quantum trajectories generally differ from the corresponding quantities evaluated on the unconditional state. Through this mechanism, we show that continuous monitoring generates entanglement, absent in the unconditional dynamics, and enhances quantum magic in the qubit pair during the decay. Both resources can be tuned through the optical phase accumulated between the qubits and the phases of homodyne local oscillators. Our results establish continuous homodyne monitoring of multiple emitters as a tunable mechanism for generating quantum resources.

</details>


### [192] [Relative Entropy Decay via BKM coercivity for Quantum Markov Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15902)
*Li Gao,Jingyu Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 引入BKM矫顽力常数，证明其正值性支配半群的指数相对熵衰减；修正log-Sobolev常数与该常数在依赖于渐近条件期望的常数意义下等价，并给出完全修正log-Sobolev不等式成立的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子马尔可夫半群相对熵的指数衰减速率，寻求刻画修正log-Sobolev不等式（及其完全版本）的等价量，并在不假设细致平衡条件下推广已有结果。

Method: 定义BKM矫顽力常数；证明修正log-Sobolev常数与BKM矫顽力常数至多相差一个依赖于渐近条件期望的常数；考察矩阵放大下的BKM矫顽力常数，发现其由qubit辅助系统即可达到，且与生成Lindblad量的GNS谱隙一致。

Result: BKM矫顽力常数的正值性控制相对熵的指数衰减；修正log-Sobolev常数与BKM矫顽力常数等价；矩阵放大的BKM矫顽力常数在qubit辅助系统下即达到，并等于GNS谱隙；由此得到完全修正log-Sobolev不等式成立当且仅当半群的GNS谱隙严格为正。

Conclusion: 在不假设细致平衡、仅要求存在忠实渐近条件期望作为长期平衡的量子马尔可夫半群框架下，给出完全修正log-Sobolev不等式的GNS谱隙判据；作为应用，证明有限维Chen-Kastoryano-Gilyén Gibbs采样器总满足完全修正log-Sobolev不等式。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of the BKM coercivity constant and show that the positivity of this constant governs the exponential relative entropy decay of the semigroup. Indeed, we prove that the modified log-Sobolev constant is equivalent to the BKM coercivity constant up to a constant depending on the asymptotic conditional expectation. We also discover that the BKM coercivity constants for matrix amplifications are already attained with a qubit auxiliary system, which also coincides with the GNS spectral gap of the generating Lindbladian. As a corollary, we establish a criterion that the complete modified log-Sobolev inequality holds if and only if the GNS gap of the semigroup is strictly positive. All the discussion above applies to quantum Markov semigroups that admit a faithful asymptotic conditional expectation as long-time equilibration, with no detailed balance condition assumed. As an application, we show the finite-dimensional Chen-Kastoryano-Gilyén Gibbs samplers always satisfy the complete modified log-Sobolev inequality.

</details>


### [193] [Quantum Codes for Generalized Amplitude-damping Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15924)
*Sourav Dutta,Anubhab Rudra,Manav Seksaria,Anil Prabhakar,Prabha Mandayam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出概率近似量子纠错（PAQEC）框架，结合近似量子纠错的灵活性与后选择恢复，构造五量子比特置换不变码，在广义振幅阻尼噪声下实现保真度损失随阻尼强度呈二次方增长，优于现有纠错码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义振幅阻尼（GAD）噪声是当前量子硬件中最现实且普遍的噪声来源之一，而传统确定性量子纠错码因其固有结构难以有效纠正GAD噪声，导致保真度损失随阻尼强度线性增长。因此需要一种资源高效、高保真的纠错方案来应对实际噪声。

Method: 提出概率近似量子纠错（PAQEC）框架，将近似量子纠错的灵活性与后选择恢复方案相结合；构造了一个五量子比特置换不变码；并将PAQEC形式化为优化问题，基于Charnes-Cooper变换与半定规划提出数值技术，以寻找任意PAQEC码的最优恢复映射。

Result: 在概率恢复下，所构造的五量子比特置换不变码实现保真度损失随阻尼强度呈二次方增长，性能优于现有量子纠错码；同时所提出的数值技术可有效识别任意PAQEC码的最优恢复映射。

Conclusion: PAQEC被确立为开发资源高效、高保真且针对现实噪声定制的量子码的有力工具，对近term量子器件及未来容错架构具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: Quantum error correcting (QEC) plays a crucial role in protecting quantum information against decoherence and enabling scalable, reliable quantum computing. One of the most realistic and ubiquitous sources of noise affecting quantum hardware today is generalized amplitude-damping (GAD) noise. Conventional, deterministic QEC codes struggle to correct for GAD noise because of their inherent structure, leading to fidelity losses that scale linearly with the damping strength. In this work, we introduce the framework of probabilistic approximate quantum error correction (PAQEC), that combines the flexibility of approximate QEC with the potential of post-selected recovery, enabling high-fidelity, resource-efficient error correction. We construct a five-qubit permutation-invariant code that, under probabilistic recovery, achieves a fidelity loss quadratic in the damping strength, thus outperforming existing QEC codes. Formulating PAQEC as an optimization problem, we present a numerical technique based on Charnes-Cooper and semidefinite programming to identify the optimal recovery map for any PAQEC code. Our results establish PAQEC as a powerful tool for developing resource-efficient, high-fidelity quantum codes tailored to realistic noise, with promising implications for near-term quantum devices and future fault-tolerant architectures.

</details>


### [194] [Achieving perfect completeness for one- and two-message quantum proof systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15926)
*Yupan Liu,Thomas Vidick*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了QIP(2)、qq-QAM、QAM与QMA均可实现完美完备性，解决了自Watrous (FOCS 2000)与Aharonov-Naveh (2002)以来关于单消息与双消息量子证明系统完美完备性的公开问题；核心技术为精确可构造的块编码矩阵与保持完备性的回合减半变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子交互证明系统在三消息及以上时可由Kitaev-Watrous (STOC 2000)实现完美完备性，但单消息（QMA）与双消息（QIP(2)、qq-QAM、QAM）情形长期悬而未决。Watrous (FOCS 2000)、Aharonov-Naveh (2002) 以及 Jain-Upadhyay-Watrous (FOCS 2009)、Kobayashi-Le Gall-Nishimura (SICOMP 2019) 分别提出或隐含提出了这些开放问题，本文旨在彻底解决该问题。

Method: 两项主要技术贡献：1) 针对QMA（并直接适用于QAM），由验证电路诱导的接受算子构造出精确可构造的块编码矩阵，其核空间可认证yes实例；2) 针对QIP(2)（隐含适用于qq-QAM），提出一种新的回合减半变换，在保持完备性的同时确保所得证明系统仍保留至少两条消息，前提是最终测量前的终态可高效制备。

Result: 证明了QIP(2)、qq-QAM、QAM与QMA四类量子证明系统均能达到完美完备性，其中qq-QAM指验证者唯一消息由半EPR对组成的双消息量子公开硬币量子交互证明系统所对应的promise问题类。

Conclusion: 单消息与双消息量子交互证明系统的完美完备性问题得到完整肯定回答，表明完美完备性在量子证明系统中具有比先前认知更广泛的普适性，所提出的块编码构造与回合减半变换为后续量子证明复杂性研究提供了通用技术工具。

Abstract: While quantum interactive proof systems using at least three messages can achieve perfect completeness, as shown by Kitaev and Watrous (STOC 2000), whether perfect completeness is achievable for one- and two-message quantum proof systems has remained open. For the one-message case, whether $\sf QMA$ can achieve perfect completeness was posed as an open problem in Watrous (FOCS 2000) and Aharonov and Naveh (2002); for the two-message case, the corresponding problems were (implicitly) posed in Jain, Upadhyay, and Watrous~(FOCS 2009) and Kobayashi, Le Gall, and Nishimura (SICOMP, 2019).
  In this work, we establish that ${\sf QIP}(2)$, ${\rm qq}\text{-}{\sf QAM}$, $\sf QAM$, and $\sf QMA$ can achieve perfect completeness. Here ${\rm qq}\text{-}{\sf QAM}$ denotes the class of promise problems admitting two-message quantum-public-coin quantum interactive proof systems in which the verifier's only message consists of half-EPR pairs. Our main technical contributions are the follows:
  1. For $\sf QMA$ (and directly for $\sf QAM$), an exactly constructible block-encoded matrix whose kernel certifies yes instances, constructed from the acceptance operator induced by the verification circuit.
  2. For ${\sf QIP}(2)$ (and implicitly ${\rm qq}\text{-}{\sf QAM}$), a new turn-halving transformation that preserves completeness and ensures that the resulting proof system retains at least two messages, provided that the terminal state before the final measurement is efficiently preparable.

</details>


### [195] [Strong-coupling quantum optics in free space with holes in a Fermi sea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15935)
*Hao Wang,Hayden C. Orth,Duo Xu,Emily J. Davis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用单原子的质心波函数构造“波包天线”，在自由空间中实现定向辐射与强光-物质耦合，单原子协同度可媲美最先进的光学腔/波导QED系统，并可用于单原子镜、亚/超辐射链、任意空间散射等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实现原子与单模电磁场强耦合依赖光学腔或波导来隔离特定光模，如何在不依赖外部腔/波导的自由空间中实现可控、定向的光-物质接口是量子技术的重要挑战。

Method: 提出从单原子质心波函数设计天线：利用有序囚禁超冷原子可产生可控定向散射的思路，将天线工程化于单个原子的质心波函数中；进而研究波包天线在线性响应区作为单原子镜的行为、由该类发射体组成的链的偶极-偶极相互作用与能带结构，并扩展到多能级原子以实现任意空间散射（含单向自发辐射）；最后将其用于增强紧密囚禁原子阵列的协同散射。

Result: 预测所得到的单原子协同度可与当前光学腔和波导QED实验的最高水平相当；波包天线在线性响应区表现为单原子镜；发射体链在远大于光波长的间距下可呈现亚辐射与超辐射；多能级原子方案可实现包括单向自发辐射在内的任意空间散射；该方法可增强常规紧密囚禁原子阵列的协同散射。

Conclusion: 通过工程化单原子质心波函数，可在自由空间中构建强耦合、定向的光-物质接口，拓展了协同散射与量子光学器件的设计空间。

Abstract: Coherent and efficient light-matter interfaces between an atom and a single mode of the electromagnetic field are essential for quantum technologies. Traditionally, these systems employ optical cavities or waveguides to isolate a specific mode of light, but a more recent approach is to engineer antennas from ordered configurations of trapped ultracold atoms that exhibit controllable and directed scattering. In this work, we propose a method to engineer an antenna from the center-of-mass wavefunction of a single atom, which can produce directed emission and thereby exhibit strong coupling to a target mode of light in free space. We predict that the resulting single-atom cooperativity can be comparable to the current state of the art in optical cavity and waveguide QED experiments. Building on this approach, we show that a wavepacket antenna becomes a single-atom mirror in the linear response regime. We study the modified dipole-dipole interactions and band structure of chains of such emitters, which can exhibit sub- and superradiance at spacings much larger than the wavelength of the light. Extending our approach to multi-level atoms, we propose a method to achieve arbitrary spatial scattering from a single atom, including uni-directional spontaneous emission. Finally, we elucidate how our approach can enhance cooperative scattering in conventional arrays of tightly trapped atoms.

</details>
